## 2026-08-26 (cycle 1198 — SHIPPED `16064-seismscore`, WIDE ×3) — the planet plays his piano; two real-world/living-surface siblings banked

Cycle 1198 went **WIDE ×3** to cash the two jury provocations (2026-08-24) still open after 1197 cashed #2: **#5 real-world-data-as-carrier** (only 1× — "the definition of a local minimum you can see and step around") and **#4 build-ON-morphonate**. Three divergent briefs; shipped **`16064-seismscore`** (live USGS earthquake feed → one grain of his take per quake, WebGL2 Ikeda black-white-red globe) — it cashes #5 with a fresh 2026 Venice Biennale anchor (Estrela *RedSkyFalls*) AND #3 (retires the 5× warm rut). Both non-winners were fully built, compliance-clean (0 oscillators, `createSafeMaster`, no `ctx.destination`, no api route, on-brand chrome, drug-clean), own-folder-isolated, and `rm -rf`'d pre-build — never committed. Bank with resurrect notes:

- **`16080-windnave` — LIVE GLOBAL WEATHER CONDUCTS A SLOW SPATIAL DRIFT THROUGH HIS TAKES (Open-Meteo → cosmic-ambient bed crossfade).** The brief: the second real-world-data domain and the calm/cosmic-ambient POLE opposite seismscore's stochastic-visceral one. Poll the public keyless CORS-open **Open-Meteo** `current` endpoint (`temperature_2m, wind_speed_10m, wind_direction_10m, surface_pressure`) for ~8 globe-spanning cities every ~120s; the aggregate field SLOWLY (tens-of-seconds `setTargetAtTime`) conducts 2–3 of his real takes playing as looping beds: aggregate wind → shared lowpass cutoff + gain shimmer; surface-pressure spread → crossfade balance (a low-pressure region lifts its take); mean wind-direction → slow field pan; temperature spread → highshelf tilt. Output = WebGL2 point-sprite field (~2600 pts) advecting along the live wind vectors (inverse-distance from city nodes placed by equirectangular lon/lat), breathing with `master.analyser`. Palette cold cosmic-ambient (near-black + whisper cyan/blue-white). Files: page.tsx + weather.ts + audio.ts + field.ts + README (tsc+eslint clean in isolation). Named ref: kin to the lab's own `9512-heliosong` (live NOAA) + Eno systems-music stance. **Why banked not shipped:** seismscore is the more legible + fresher-anchored (2026 Biennale) realization of the SAME jury #5 lane, and the more diverse pick against the recent cluster; windnave's named ref is softer (self-referential + Eno). **Resurrect:** ship on the next real-world-data slot as the meditative counterpart, or FUSE — one page that crossfades between the seismic (intense) and weather (calm) carriers by global energy. Deepen: add ocean/tide (NOAA CO-OPS) as a third carrier band.
- **`16096-spiralbloom` — HIS HARMONY GROWS A BZ EXCITABLE-MEDIUM SPIRAL-WAVE MEMBRANE (build-ON-morphonate, jury #4).** The brief: the deliberate SIBLING to `16000-morphonate`, deepening its "his harmony grows a living surface" vein with a DIFFERENT medium — a **Belousov–Zhabotinsky / Barkley excitable-medium** (activator `u` + slow recovery `v` on `RGBA16F` ping-pong textures) that throws rotating **spiral / target waves**, NOT Gray-Scott coral. His full take plays start-to-finish; `loadTrackAnalysis` is walked against `ctx.currentTime` and each **chord change** fires an excitation — root pitch-class → angle/position, major = broad target wave, minor = tight spiral; melody note-density → excitability (lower ε/b, more sub-steps). Never resets (min-5 ≠ min-1). Cold ink + violet-only chroma. Files: page.tsx + bz.ts + README (tsc+eslint clean in isolation; spectral-flux onset fallback when analysis null). Named ref: BZ reaction / Winfree *When Time Breaks Down* / Barkley 1991 — honest that it is NOT "first reaction sim." **Why banked not shipped:** morphonate shipped only 2 cycles ago (1196), so its harmony-membrane tag profile (autonomous + GPU-field + harmony-reading + achromatic) is the CLOSEST of the three to a just-shipped piece; opening the months-starved real-world-data lane (#5) was the bigger diversity move this fire. **Resurrect:** ship on the next cycle wanting to cash jury #4 / extend morphonate; it's a strong, ready 3/5. Deepen: dual-take counterpoint (two excitable fields, one per hand's register) or let chord TENSION (dissonance) locally raise excitability so harmonic climaxes visibly storm the membrane.

## 2026-08-25 (cycle 1197 — SHIPPED `16032-headnave`, DEEP ×3) — your physical head walks a nave of his takes; two embodied-nav siblings banked

Cycle 1197 went **DEEP ×3** on the 2026-08-24 jury's #1 provocation: the "embodied webcam-head-position spatial-audio room" it named as the lab's next GENUINE grep-0 subsystem — *"make the webcam your head — lean and walk to compose the mix from a field of his takes… graduate the antiphon idea instead of restarting it."* One concept (webcam head = the WebAudio listener over an HRTF field of Karel's real takes, graduating `15536-antiphon`), three technical approaches. All three: Ikeda **black-white-red** (the jury's requested grep-0 palette, #3), **WebGL2** GPU-field output (the jury-celebrated substrate), rule-10-clean (6 distinct real takes, 0 oscillators, `createSafeMaster`), pointer/keyboard default so they're instantly demoable with zero camera prompts. Shipped **`16032-headnave`** (fullest 6DOF "room you move through" — the most literal realization of the provocation). The two non-winners were fully built, compliance-clean, own-folder-isolated, `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed. Bank with resurrect notes:

- **`16048-leanfield` — THE ROBUSTNESS-FIRST WEBCAM NAVIGATOR (2D centroid + depth, no 6DOF matrix).** The brief: graduate antiphon's HRTF nave into a webcam field using only the THREE most stable signals a face tracker produces — nose-centroid (index 1, x-flipped) → a 2D cursor across a constellation of 6 takes (each HRTF panner placed *relative to the cursor* so the faced node is in front); face bbox scale (landmarks 234/454) → lean/depth that shortens listening distance + tightens a focus gaussian + zooms the field; eye-line roll (33/263) → a subtle listener up-vector stereo tilt. Deliberately throws away `outputFacialTransformationMatrixes` (the fragile bit under bad lighting/glasses/cheap webcams). WebGL2 constellation, Ikeda B-W-R, pointer default (pointer=centroid, wheel=lean). Fully built at `16048-leanfield` this cycle (1057 lines, compliance-clean in isolation; not committed). **Why NOT shipped now:** it's the *safest* reading of the provocation — a field you point at rather than a room you walk through — so it under-delivers on the jury's explicit "lean and walk" 6DOF ask that headnave meets head-on. **Resurrect:** it's the ready SWAP if headnave's 6DOF feels jittery in Karel's review (this works on any webcam, any lighting); or ship as the robust production-path once the concept is validated. Deepen by fusing the depth axis with headnave's translation.

- **`16064-facingcompass` — TURN YOUR HEAD TO CHOOSE WHO SPEAKS (rotational call/answer).** The brief: 6 takes on a ring 60° apart; head YAW is a compass (±30° sweeps the full ring). Turning steers the `AudioListener` forward vector (real HRTF rotation) AND shapes per-voice gain by angular alignment — the faced take swells to the bone-white **CALL** (blooming with its analyser), the opposite take lingers as the oxblood **ANSWER**, a slow 6-second antiphonal cycle trading the front/back halves (antiphon's breath, now conducted by your head). WebGL2 radial ring-field + heading needle, Ikeda B-W-R, pointer/arrow default. Yaw from the 4×4 matrix `atan2(m[1],m[0])`, nose-offset fallback. Fully built at `16064-facingcompass` this cycle (1000 lines, compliance-clean in isolation; not committed). **Why NOT shipped now:** rotational-only — you barely move — so it's the most legible in a 30-second review but the narrowest slice of "lean AND walk"; headnave is the fuller graduation. **Resurrect:** ship the moment Karel wants the most instantly-graspable version ("just turn your head"); or FUSE — layer facingcompass's call/answer antiphonal breath onto headnave's walkable nave for the best of both (the natural cycle-2 deepen of the winner).


## 2026-08-25 (cycle 1196 — SHIPPED `16000-morphonate`, WIDE ×3) — his harmony grows a living GPU membrane; two strong arms banked

Cycle 1196 went **WIDE ×3** to break the five-cycle "human performs his catalog live" monoculture the 2026-08-23 jury named as its #1 concern. Three arms, three ways out of the vein, all cold/achromatic + WebGL2-primary (clearing every palette/output ban) + rule-10-clean. Shipped **`16000-morphonate`** (autonomous — leaves the performance vein entirely — AND genuinely *reads* his harmony, per rule 10's deeper ask). The two non-winners were fully built, compliance-clean, own-folder-isolated, `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed. Bank with resurrect notes:

- **`15984-seismscore` — THE PLANET'S LIVE SEISMIC PULSE PLAYS HIS PIANO (real USGS earthquake feed → grains of his take).** The brief: the public CORS-enabled **USGS earthquake GeoJSON** feed (`all_hour`/`all_day`/`2.5_day`) polled every 60s, diffed against a seen-set so only genuinely NEW quakes sound. Each new quake slices ONE grain from his decoded take via `createBufferSource` → per-grain envelope → `StereoPannerNode` → `safeMaster`. Mapping: **magnitude → grain length + gain, depth → playbackRate/pitch (deeper=lower), longitude → pan.** Output = raw **WebGL2** analytic shader globe (slow auto-rotation, cold graticule + limb glow), epicenters projected onto the front hemisphere as expanding white-hot additive rings (bigger/brighter for recent+larger), plus a cyan-white analyser-driven seismogram scrolling the bottom. Cold achromatic; RGBA8 (no float-extension dependency → very robust). Secondary human control: region filter + feed-window selector + track selector. Graceful degrade: synthetic *event stream* (metadata only — audio always his real grains) with a `text-destructive` notice; WebGL2 notice; reduced-motion freezes rotation. Ref: Ryoji Ikeda *data.matrix* / data-as-material + the seismic-sonification tradition. **Why promising:** the cleanest "real external data as the *carrier*" (the exact jury-blessed lane of `15440-spheres`/`14224-skyflock`), the most instantly legible concept for a 30-second morning review ("the Earth plays his piano"), and maximally diverse against the recent performance ships. Fully built at `15984-seismscore` this cycle (1022 lines, ESLint+tsc clean in isolation; not committed). **Why NOT shipped now:** his take is grain *material* triggered by unrelated external events rather than *read* — morphonate realizes rule 10's "make visuals genuinely read his music (harmony, melody, form)" more deeply, and shipping the harmony-reader over the event-trigger was the closer call decided on that axis. **Resurrect:** ship on any cycle wanting real-world-data sonification (it's the most rested lane and the most robust of the three); deepen by layering **multiple takes** by quake-cluster geography, or crossfading albums by global seismic energy.

- **`16016-inkstroke` — A CALLIGRAPHIC BRUSHSTROKE BOWS HIS RECORDING (Calliphony, reframed rule-10-clean).** The brief: reframe **Calliphony (arXiv:2608.03040, Aug 2026 — a calligraphy-driven real-time music-performance interface)** so the brush does NOT synthesize — it **bows** his real take. Pointer/pen stroke: horizontal position → playback position in his buffer; stroke **speed → grain density + ±6% playbackRate lean**; **pressure** (`PointerEvent.pressure`, else derived from slowness) + curvature → grain length + a lowpass "ink wetness" cutoff. Overlapping short `createBufferSource` grains (Hann envelopes, polyphony capped at 24) → shared lowpass → `safeMaster`. Output = **WebGL2** ping-pong ink-diffusion field (dark ink deposited along the stroke, bleeding/feathering into bone-white paper sumi-e style, whisper of cold indigo in wet cores, the bowing head breathing with the analyser). Idle auto-demo stroke keeps it alive on load; Canvas2D ink-bleed fallback; reduced-motion calms it. Ref: Calliphony + sumi-e ink-wash. **Why promising:** the **freshest research hook of the cycle** (strictly <30 days — jury #5's exact ask), a genuinely NEW input modality for the lab (pen/brush granular-bow, distinct from camera/MIDI/peer), and a lovely achromatic execution. Fully built at `16016-inkstroke` this cycle (1065 lines, ESLint+tsc clean in isolation; not committed). **Why NOT shipped now:** conceptually it is still a *human-performs-his-catalog* piece — the exact vein the jury flagged as a five-cycle monoculture — so even reframed it risks reading as "the vein again," and the WIDE mandate this cycle was to LEAVE the vein, which morphonate/seismscore do and inkstroke does not. **Resurrect:** ship the moment the performance-vein is rested again (its <30-day hook stays citable for a while); deepen into a **calligraphy DUET** — two strokes over `peerSync` (reuse 1195's transport), two brushes bowing one take together.


Cycle 1195 went **DEEP ×2** on the jury's #1 unclaimed 0× frontier — **WebRTC multi-user** (named across the last several JURY.md verdicts as the single most-cited never-run subsystem; provocation #2 on 2026-08-23 described it almost verbatim: "two people conducting ONE of his takes together — each drives a voice; the mix is the shared gesture… a genuine 4/5 that is NOT a fourth pair of solo hands"). I first built the hard shared infra myself — `_shared/peerSync.ts` (WebRTC `RTCDataChannel` with serverless copy-paste signaling + BroadcastChannel same-browser fallback + host-authoritative NTP-style shared clock + solo mode) — then raced two interaction models over it. Shipped **`15920-duetlink`** (two people each conduct one voice of a register-split take — the exact jury-named 4/5, cleanest concept, most legible for morning review, extends the blessed conduct/canon lineage from two-hands to two-people). Runner-up was fully built + compliance-clean + own-folder-isolated, `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed. Bank with resurrect notes:

- **`15936-commons` — SIT INSIDE HIS TAKE TOGETHER (shared presence field; casts = resonant grains of his own audio both peers see AND hear).** The brief: presence, not mixing — Karel's ONE real take (*All Together*) plays in sync for both peers over `peerSync`; both look into the SAME WebGL2 grain field (~4,200 additive point-sprites reacting to the live spectrum sampled in the vertex shader). Either peer taps/drags to **cast**: an expanding ripple propagates across BOTH screens at the same synced instant (`atSyncedTime = now()+130ms`, ripple age = `now()-at` so the ring is at the same radius on both screens at the same wall moment), and it re-triggers a short **grain sliced from the SAME `AudioBuffer`** (anchored near the shared playhead, snapped to a real note onset when analysis is present) through a swept high-Q bandpass — a resonant swell of HIS own audio, never a synth, both peers hearing every cast. Two presences are legible as ice-cyan "you" / cold-violet "other" cursors with trails; ripples inherit the caster's colour. Solo fallback: an "ambient other" drifts the far side and auto-casts, disabling itself the moment a real peer arrives. Canvas2D fallback; honours `prefers-reduced-motion`. Refs: The Hub (1980s computer-network band) + telematic-performance lineage; telepresence/social-presence research; the 2026 `RTCDataChannel` + NTP-clock-offset browser-audio consensus. **Why it was promising:** a genuinely DIFFERENT second vein of the same 0× frontier — presence/telematic-field rather than mix-a-voice — plus a real second technique (buffer-grain re-trigger through a resonant band), and the "another person is here, moving my sound" felt-goal is a strong surprise. **Why NOT shipped now:** duetlink is the *exact* concept the jury named as the next 4/5 and reads more instantly in a 30-second review ("you take one voice, your friend takes the other"); commons' "casts open a resonant band" is lovelier but a hair more abstract to grok cold; and neither could be runtime-verified headless, so the more legible/robust arm won the single ship. **Resurrect:** ship on a cycle wanting the presence/telematic register (it's the natural sibling once multi-user is proven); deepen with >2 peers (a shared room / conducted ensemble), a persistent "who's been here" trace, or casts that quantize to his onsets. Fully built at `15936-commons` this cycle (not committed).

## 2026-08-23 (cycle 1194 — SHIPPED `15872-answerpiano`, WIDE ×3) — the lab EMITS MIDI (his piano plays your synth); two banked

Cycle 1194 went **WIDE ×3** (ledger 1190 W · 1191 D · 1192 W · 1193 D → 1194 **W**; and jury 2026-08-22 #2: "if you go WIDE, hit a still-0× frontier — MIDI/OSC OUT or WebRTC multi-user — and do NOT ship a third-straight camera-primary piece"). Three DIFFERENT never-run/rare subsystems, all rule-10-clean, all dodging the jury bans (camera-monoculture · Canvas2D-flat · three.js-room · WebGL2-field · subtraction · grayscale · passive-primary). Shipped **`15872-answerpiano`** (Web MIDI **OUTPUT** = the jury's #2 named 0× frontier; the lab's first piece that EMITS MIDI — his recording drives a knowledge-based compiler that answers on YOUR hardware synth; freshest research hook of the cycle, MazzikaAI arXiv:2608.10360 Aug 2026 strictly <30d; 4–5/5 ambition). Both runners-up were fully built + `tsc`/ESLint-clean + compliance-clean + valid track IDs + full teardown, `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed. Bank with resurrect notes:

- **`15888-tiltpour` — TILT TO POUR HIS TAKE THROUGH THE ROOM (DeviceOrientation + Vibration haptic-OUT + WebGPU spatial grain cloud).** The brief: his real take is the only audio, routed through an HRTF `PannerNode` on a fixed ~1.7 m sphere; `beta`/`gamma` tilt steer azimuth/elevation so tilting physically moves his piano around your head (+ tilt-gated lowpass/gain, `setTargetAtTime`-smoothed); onsets (from `.notes[]` time, energy-flux fallback when analysis null) fire `navigator.vibrate` with length scaling to onset strength (haptic OUTPUT). Visual: a WGSL compute grain cloud (~16k grains seeded from his waveform, gathered toward the pour point — the conduct/canon engine adapted, jury #3 "compound the engine"), Canvas2D fallback on the same model; pointer-drag fallback pours the sound with no sensor + on-brand notices. Refs: Sound2Hap arXiv:2601.12245 + HaptoFlow (audio→vibrotactile) atop Web Audio HRTF. **Why it was promising:** two genuine lab-first subsystems (DeviceOrientation-as-control + Vibration-as-output) AND it compounds the WebGPU-compute engine per jury #3; a piece that comes alive *in the hand* is a strong phone-review surprise. **Why NOT shipped now:** it is the refinement of already-banked `15840-orbit` (1192) — a strong deepening (adds the WebGPU spatial cloud + HRTF so the "pour" reads visually even where iOS `navigator.vibrate` is absent), but WIDE this cycle wanted the frontier the jury named *first* (MIDI-out), and answerpiano's freshness + surprise edged it; also none of the three sensor paths (beta/gamma sign, vibrate timing, real WebGPU pipeline) could be exercised headless. **Resurrect:** ship on a phone-review / spatial-audio cycle; verify tilt-sign + HRTF orientation on a real device first; consider the Gamepad-rumble path for a cross-platform felt-output that lands on Karel's iPhone-less desktop too. Fully built at `15888-tiltpour` this cycle (not committed).
- **`15904-gamepad` — PLAY HIS TAKE LIKE A GRANULAR INSTRUMENT (Gamepad API + DOM/CSS controller panel).** The brief: a connected gamepad polled every frame (`navigator.getGamepads()`, `axes[]`/`buttons[]`, `gamepadconnected`/`disconnected`) drives a moving read-head through his one decoded take — left stick = scrub position + scatter width, right stick = time-base (0.6×→1.5×) + pitch spread, LT/RT triggers = a low/high crossover of a "bass voice" vs "treble voice" of HIS own signal, bumpers = wet/density, face buttons = jump to phrase anchors (from `.notes[]` onsets, even-spacing fallback); short windowed grains sliced straight from his buffer with equal-power ramps, all terminating at `createSafeMaster.input` (0 synth). Output: a stylized DOM/CSS controller map whose sticks/meters/buttons glow + scale with live input, + a small canvas grain ribbon. Full keyboard fallback (WASD/arrows/Q-E/Z-X/space/1–9, drawn on screen) so it's demoable with no controller. Refs: CataRT (Diemo Schwarz, IRCAM) corpus-based concatenative synthesis + controllerism. **Why it was promising:** genuine lab-first (Gamepad API, 0×), and it is exactly the cross-platform felt-instrument pivot that `15840-orbit`'s own resurrect note (1192) proposed; DOM-primary output is a healthy rare surface. **Why NOT shipped now:** controllerism/scrubbing is a slightly more familiar idea than "his recording performs on your synth," and it needs a controller to fully shine; answerpiano's frontier-novelty + fresh research + no-hardware demoability won. **Resurrect:** ship on a cycle wanting a playable-instrument surface; add the Gamepad haptic-actuators rumble (closes the `15840-orbit` felt-output gap cross-platform); consider polyphonic grain voices. Fully built at `15904-gamepad` this cycle (not committed).

## 2026-08-23 (cycle 1193 — SHIPPED `15824-canon`, DEEP ×3) — DEEPEN conduct into two-hand polyphonic conducting; two banked

Cycle 1193 went **DEEP ×3** (ledger 1189 D · 1190 W · 1191 D · 1192 W → 1193 D; AND jury 2026-08-22 #1 explicitly: "DEEPEN conduct — do NOT run away from your first 4/5 in a month"). ONE concept — **CANON: two-hand POLYPHONIC conducting, where each hand independently drives the time-base of a different voice of his ONE recording so his piano plays in counterpoint against itself, over TWO WebGPU grain clouds (conduct's engine reused/doubled)** — raced via three voice-derivation approaches. Shipped **`15824-canon`** (register crossover: bass/treble split, each hand one voice; the cleanest never-run subsystem = two decoupled time-bases from one take, most legible counterpoint, most robust). Both runners-up were fully built + `tsc`/ESLint-clean + compliance-grep-clean (0 synth, 0 direct destination, 0 font-serif/text-white chrome, 0 drug hits) + valid track IDs + full teardown, `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed. Bank with resurrect notes:

- **`15856-canon` — DUET WITH YOURSELF (record a conducting pass → replay it as a ghost second conductor).** The brief: you conduct his live head (height→`playbackRate` 0.72×…1.35×, distance→dynamics, openness→timbre); a `conducting → recording → duet` state machine (Record a pass / Stop & duet / Clear ghost / auto-freeze at 12s) captures each frame of your gesture into a time-stamped automation curve; on duet, a replay playhead loops through that curve and drives a SECOND looping head of his SAME recording (veiled, quieter, own lowpass + longer echo + a gate that only opens in duet), while you conduct the live head against your recorded self. Visual: conduct's WebGPU grain engine doubled — a full ember→gold LIVE cloud pushed by your live hands + a dimmer cooler-violet GHOST cloud pushed by the REPLAYED hand positions (a `ghost` flag in the shared Params), sharing one trail frame; dimmer violet replayed hand-markers over the live warm ones. Canvas2D two-cloud + pointer fallback (records/duets too). Refs: arXiv:2604.27957 (conducting=time), ReaLJam arXiv:2502.21267 (the "partner" is your own recorded pass, not a model). **Why it was promising:** the HIGHEST-novelty arm — gesture-automation record/replay is itself a never-run subsystem, and "duet with your past self" is the biggest conceptual leap of the three (extends the mididuet/anticipation lineage). **Why NOT shipped now:** the record→duet state machine is the fragile part (self-flagged: auto-transition fires from inside the render loop via a one-shot setPhase; ghost gate muted every non-duet frame) — riskier for a headless straight-to-prod ship than the crossover's stateless robustness. **Resurrect:** ship as the NEXT DEEP cycle (its own multi-cycle deepening: layer >2 ghosts = a conducted ensemble of your past passes; quantize the replay loop to his onsets; save/name passes). Fully built at `15856-canon` this cycle (not committed).
- **`15840-canon` — TEMPORAL ROUND (two playback heads of the same take, offset in time; conduct how tightly the round chases itself).** The brief: his one recording plays through a bright LEAD head (position 0) and a veiled FOLLOWER head starting `TRAIL_SECONDS=4s` behind (wrapped) — a round of his take against itself; left-hand height→lead `playbackRate`, right-hand→follower, independently, so the temporal gap between them breathes (surfaced as a signed `trail: N.N s` readout). Openness→per-head lowpass (lead ~12kHz, follower ~6.5kHz), distance→gains+shared feedback-delay space. Visual: conduct's grain engine via a per-grain `voice` flag (10k lead / 10k follower, STRIDE padded to 8), follower dimmed/violet-shifted/slower-damped/displaced so it visibly trails. Canvas2D + pointer fallback. Refs: arXiv:2604.27957, BachDuet (counterpoint). **Why it was promising:** clean and robust; the breathing-trail readout is a lovely legible mechanic. **Why NOT shipped now:** "two heads of the SAME full-band signal offset by 4s" reads closer to a conducted echo/delay than genuinely INDEPENDENT voices — the crossover's register split is the more legible "two voices of his piano"; and the trail readout is integrated from `playbackRate` not true source position, so it drifts slightly over long runs (self-flagged). **Resurrect:** ship on a cycle wanting a simpler robust conduct-sibling; fix the trail by reading true source position; consider N-voice rounds (a stretto). Fully built at `15840-canon` this cycle (not committed).

## 2026-08-22 (cycle 1192 — SHIPPED `15808-mididuet`, WIDE ×3) — you become a co-performer; his catalog answers ahead of your line; two banked

Cycle 1192 went **WIDE ×3** (ledger 1189 D · 1190 W · 1191 D → 1192 W) — three DIFFERENT never-run/rare subsystems, all rule-10-clean, all dodging the jury 08-21 bans (passive-primary · Canvas2D-flat · three.js-room · WebGL2-field · subtraction · grayscale). Shipped **`15808-mididuet`** (Web MIDI = the jury's #3 named never-run subsystem; the visitor plays and Karel's OWN recorded phrases answer, retrieved concatenatively and shown *ahead* of the line — ReaLJam's anticipation-made-visible on an inline-SVG conversation score; 4/5 ambition). Both runners-up were fully built + `tsc`/ESLint-clean + compliance-grep-clean + valid track IDs, `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed. Bank with resurrect notes:

- **`15824-shadow` — SING AND HIS PIANO SHADOWS YOU (mic + real-time YIN pitch tracking → re-pitched spotlights of his real notes).** The brief: hum/sing a slow line; a hand-written **YIN** f0 estimator (difference → CMND → absolute-threshold → parabolic interp, gated on RMS + periodicity, median+EMA smoothed with octave-snap) tracks your fundamental; the nearest of Karel's real notes is re-triggered from his recording with `playbackRate` nudged ±4 semitones toward your pitch, so his piano answers in unison a beat behind, in his timbre. Output: inline-SVG "pitch helix", two entwined ribbons + unison lock-glow + note/cents/confidence readout. Pointer-draw fallback (X=time, Y=pitch) when no mic — so always demoable. Refs: YIN (de Cheveigné & Kawahara 2002), CREPE (Kim 2018). **Why it was promising:** genuine lab-first (visitor's *sung pitch* as control, 0×), and the most intimate of the three — your voice literally conducts his hands. **Resurrect:** ship on a mic-secondary / cold-input cycle; deepen with polyphonic-aware selection (chase his chord under your melody), and a "duet memory" that learns which of his phrases you keep returning to. Fully built at `15824-shadow` in git history of this cycle's builders (not committed).
- **`15840-orbit` — HIS RECORDING IN YOUR HAND (device-tilt traversal + Vibration-API HAPTIC output).** The brief: phone-first; tilt gamma pans him across stereo, tilt beta opens a lowpass/gain (brightness); his note onsets (`.notes[]` time+velocity, walked against wrapped playback) fire `navigator.vibrate` taps scaled by velocity (~8ms soft → ~42ms hard), coalesced per-frame with a 55ms floor + visibility guard so dense passages never machine-gun; a warm SVG orb flares + recolours by `pitchClassHue` on each onset; spectral-flux fallback when a take has no analysis; pointer-drag fallback on desktop. **Why it was promising:** the haptic OUTPUT modality is a genuine lab-first, and a piece that comes alive *in the hand* is a strong phone-review surprise. **Why NOT shipped now:** `navigator.vibrate` is **Android-only** — on Karel's iPhone at 06:30 the palm-pulse (the whole point) won't fire; he'd get only the sight+sound+tilt version. **Resurrect** if/when Karel confirms an Android review device, OR pivot the felt-output to the **Gamepad haptic actuators API** (a connected controller's rumble, cross-platform) so the haptic lands regardless of phone OS. Fully built at `15840-orbit` this cycle (not committed).

## 2026-08-21 (cycle 1190 — SHIPPED `15696-leanin`, WIDE ×3) — three non-locomotion committing verbs raced; two banked

Cycle 1190 went **WIDE ×3** on the jury 2026-08-20's still-open provocations #2 ("the verb that isn't locomotion") + #4 ("commit to a register you've never held") + #5 ("extend the body-reading ceiling past `pulse`"). Three distinct non-locomotion committing verbs, each flat single-surface (no three.js, no WebGL2, no sim), each catalog-only. Shipped the **proximity/intimacy** arm (`15696-leanin`: lean physically closer → his take blooms; sit back → it recedes — face-area via webcam with pointer fallback). Both runners-up were fully built + build-clean (each self-reported `tsc --noEmit` + ESLint exit 0), rm-rf'd pre-build, never committed. Bank them with full resurrect notes:

- **`15664-letter` — "a letter written in his music" (writing as transport).** His take starts frozen/silent; TYPING is the only transport — each keystroke feeds a decaying `energy` that his single `AudioBufferSourceNode`'s `playbackRate` chases toward 1, so continuous writing holds ~real-time and stopping glides the piano to a freeze mid-phrase. Each keystroke snaps to his nearest real note onset (binary-searched `loadTrackAnalysis`) and drops a slate-teal ink glyph on a Canvas2D scoring ribbon; backspace erases the last word AND scrubs the playhead back to that word's onset (the only source re-seat). Cadence also drives a lowpass. Palette: carbon-copy bone paper + cold slate-teal ink (hsl ~195 42% 48%). Ref: Alvin Lucier *I Am Sitting in a Room* + Fluxus event scores. **Resurrect:** ship near-verbatim as its own cycle — it's the conceptual/critical + "score-following in reverse" lane, distinct from every shipped piece; deepen by replaying ONLY the landed notes as stitched exact slices (a `15600-keepsake`-style negative image of the letter). Strong ship candidate on a cycle that wants writing/text as the verb.
- **`15680-datamatics` — "lock onto his pulse or it dissolves into noise" (rhythmic entrainment).** His take rendered as a scrolling **Ikeda severe black/white datamatics** field (monospaced glyph grid, scan-lines, hex/coordinate HUD, hard NOW bar; pure black + white + ONE cold cyan `hsl(190 90% 60%)`). Bright structural columns land on his real onsets (clustered from the note-roll into a ≤3/s followable pulse, envelope-flux peak-pick fallback). Visitor TAPS in time (space/click); a phase-lock lock-meter Gaussian-scores each tap within ±130 ms; lock high → lowpass opens 700 Hz→14 kHz + gain 0.4→1.0 + matrix resolves; lock low → lowpass closes, gain ducks, field tears into Canvas-only scan-line noise. Lock decays so coasting is impossible. Safety-clean (spatial noise, stable mean luminance, ≤3 Hz, reduced-motion freezes). Ref: Ryoji Ikeda *datamatics / data.matrix / test pattern* + sensorimotor synchronization. **Resurrect:** this is the LITERAL answer to jury #4 (Ikeda register still 0× before this) — ship it on the next cycle where the cross-cycle contrast favors COLD/severe (this cycle shipped the warm ember `leanin`, so a cold-clinical piece back-to-back was deliberately deferred to avoid a mini-monoculture with this-morning's keepsake). Ready-to-ship, keyboard-only, robust. Highest-priority bank.

## 2026-08-21 (cycle 1189 — SHIPPED `15600-keepsake`, DEEP ×3) — the earmarked curation-by-loss piece, raced via three interaction models

Cycle 1189 cashed the 1188 earmark: a dedicated **DEEP** on *curation by loss* (jury 2026-08-20 provocation #3 — "build the banked keepsake as a dedicated DEEP; it is NOT a walk-a-room and NOT three.js"). One concept — his take plays once, you keep only what you reach for, on replay you hear only your keepsakes — attacked via three interaction models. Shipped the **river-reach** arm (`15600-keepsake`: real-time press-and-hold grab, Canvas2D river, exact-slice keepsake replay). Banked the two runners-up, both fully built + build-clean, rm-rf'd pre-build, never committed:

### `15616-keepsake` — SPEND-TO-KEEP: the scarcity-toll arm (inline-SVG ledger)
- **The one sentence:** his take plays once and you may save at most ~25 seconds of a ~3-minute take — every moment you keep is a moment you can never get back, and when the budget runs dry the rest streams past forever unkeepable.
- **Brief / what was built:** default take "Rolling" plays once via one `bufferSource → safeMaster.input`. Hold KEEP (button + spacebar) to record intervals while a **25s budget meter drains in real time** (cold ink → `text-destructive` red near empty); at zero the control goes dead, a red boundary marker drops, and the post-budget stream ghosts to erased grey. Surface = inline-SVG toll-receipt: draining meter + streaming note-roll strip (waveform-envelope fallback) + accumulating filmstrip of saved fragments, kept moments burned bone-white via sparing `feGaussianBlur`. Replay = exact kept slices, 8ms equal-power fades, "you paid Ns of 25." Full teardown; tsc/eslint clean; rule-10 clean.
- **Why banked, not shipped:** the toll-budget makes loss most *legible*, but it is conceptually adjacent to the existing `15344-toll` (a recording you pay to hear), and its own README leans on toll as the sibling — shipping it risked reading as "toll again." The river-reach arm is the fresher single concept the jury actually earmarked.
- **Resurrect:** fold into the shipped winner as the **variable-toll** deepening — price seconds non-uniformly (harmonically dense / climactic passages from the chord analysis cost more budget to keep), forcing a real economy of musical value; or a two-visitor shared 25s budget over one take, negotiating in real time what survives.

### `15632-keepsake` — ATTENTION-DECAY: the graded-memory arm (Canvas2D, ACT-R)
- **The one sentence:** keeping a moment works like real memory — a moment survives only if your attention was on it as it sounded, and even attended moments decay unless you return to them, so on replay the take is not a clean cut but a graded ghost, each fragment as loud as your memory of it actually was.
- **Brief / what was built:** default take "Isolation." The take is a field of quarter-second embers on a pitch/loudness contour; the pointer is an attention focus, each moment's ACT-R base-level activation rising (Gaussian falloff, hold to lean in) only while focus is on a *sounded* moment and decaying `A *= exp(-dt/τ)` (τ≈5s). On end, the whole `AudioBuffer` replays through ONE automated `GainNode` whose envelope traces each frame's final activation — full where you held, ghost where you brushed, silent where you let go — with a remembered/ghosts/let-go tally. Graceful waveform-field fallback; tsc/eslint clean; rule-10 clean (real buffer re-gained by memory, 0 synth).
- **Why banked, not shipped:** the freshest *technique* of the three (first "graded memory re-voicing" in the lab — memory as decay of *accessibility*, not deletion, per arXiv:2604.00131 *Oblivion*), but its verb — a pointer attention-*steer* — is the "easy load-bearing verb" the jury (2026-08-20 #2) explicitly warned against, and the feel-tuning (DECAY_TAU/BOOST_RATE/LENS_R) is unvalidated by ear. The winner's press-and-hold reach is the more committing verb.
- **Resurrect:** this IS the winner's deepening #1 (weighted decay, not a hard edge). Merge: keep the river-reach *verb* but make the replay gain *graded* by how firmly/repeatedly you reached, not binary — plus a "sleep/consolidation" pass between listen and replay where weak-but-repeated traces strengthen and lucky single hits fade.

## 2026-08-20 (cycle 1188 — SHIPPED `15536-antiphon`, WIDE ×3) — walk between his takes and stand inside his own counterpoint

Cycle 1188 went **WIDE** to break a four-DEEP streak (1184–1187) and directly serve diversity, but every brief was a *sharp one-sentence concept* in the jury 2026-08-19's ceiling lanes (conceptual/critical + catalog-as-corpus + load-bearing human verb) — so the WIDE was itself a ceiling hunt. Shipped **`15536-antiphon`** (spatial/installation: ≥6 real takes as HRTF voices in a cathedral, navigate between them, they call and answer antiphonally — jury #4 corpus-not-filter + #2 the-verb + the thin spatial lane + VISIONARY's boundless-immersive direction + Karel's loved cathedral/voice-cathedral/canon cluster). Banked the two runners-up, both fully built + build-clean, rm-rf'd pre-build, never committed:

### `15520-keepsake` — CONCEPTUAL/CRITICAL: curation-by-loss (the negative image of your own attention) — **the strongest bank; earmarked for a dedicated DEEP next cycle**
- **The one sentence:** one of Karel's takes plays through EXACTLY ONCE, and the only thing you can do is KEEP the moments worth saving; everything you don't reach for, once the passage flows past it, is gone for good — on replay you get only your keepsakes, a portrait in his own playing of what you chose to hold.
- **Brief / what was built:** single real take ("Bath") plays once via one `bufferSource → gain → safeMaster.input`. The sole verb is KEEP (big button + spacebar); each press grabs a ~1.6s window centred on the playhead, merges overlaps into a ledger, irreversible (no un-keep, no rewind, nothing auto-kept). On passage-end or "Let the rest go" → KEEPSAKE mode: a look-ahead scheduler stitches only the kept windows in time order with equal-power (`setValueCurveAtTime` sine/cosine) crossfades, looping with a wrap crossfade; keep-nothing → silence + "You kept nothing. It's gone." Visual = full-screen three.js `ShaderMaterial` river whose shape is the buffer's peak-envelope `DataTexture`, warm-gold kept bands crystallizing while the un-kept remainder dissolves to ember-black behind a sweeping playhead; DOM-fallback river; `text-destructive` audio-error path; full teardown + `ctx.close`. `tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0, rule-10 clean (0 osc/constantsource, 0 direct destination). ~single-file page.tsx + README.
- **Why it was banked, not shipped:** it is the sharpest *single* concept of the three and squarely the jury #5 "actual ceiling" lane (extend toll/negative — "the thing you can only hear by giving something up"), BUT the jury explicitly wants that ceiling piece as a **dedicated DEEP race**, not a WIDE arm — and shipping a THIRD consecutive conceptual-loss piece (toll→negative→keepsake) risks the exact "wearing costumes" rut the jury warned about. Antiphon serves the fresher #4/spatial lane now; keepsake gets its own DEEP.
- **Resurrect:** next cycle, DEEP ×2–3 on this concept. Deepening hooks: shape the kept-band *length* into the visual (a long-held moment reads bigger); a running "X% of his passage survives" that Karel can push to near-zero; use arXiv:2508.01571's shortest-path reduction to show a faint "skeleton" of what he'd have kept, so your choices contrast with the structural one; try it on a denser take than Bath to test the crossfade musicality by ear (the one honest unknown).

### `15552-graft` — LIVE-PERFORMANCE / concatenative retrieval: play a melody, his real recorded hands answer
- **The one sentence:** play any melody on your keyboard and Karel's own recorded hands answer in real time — the piece finds and sounds the nearest phrase he ACTUALLY recorded, so you improvise a live duet with a pianist who only ever speaks in things he really played.
- **Brief / what was built:** QWERTY = a two-octave keyboard (+ tappable ≥44px on-screen keys). After a start gesture all six verified tracks decode and their `loadTrackAnalysis().notes[]` group into phrases indexed by pitch-class/register/contour (audio-only ~1.2s-window RMS/ZCR fallback badged "no analysis"). Each key-press retrieves the nearest real phrase (weighted distance, top-4 random for liveliness) and windows that actual recording via `s.start(t, startSec, durSec)` with a click-free envelope — every triggered SOUND is a real slice, never a tone. Warm parchment/amber DOM "living score" blooms each answer as a fading card (played→matched note, contour sparkline, source title, chord root). `tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0, eslint clean (1 benign teardown warning), rule-10 clean.
- **Why banked:** genuinely strong verb + corpus, but the retrieval *lane* is already 2× recent (answerback/transcript) and the DOM score is the least immersive of the three; antiphon's spatial cathedral is the bigger surprise and the fresher lane.
- **Resurrect:** ship standalone when the audit wants a live-performance / keyboard-instrument register; or fold the CataRT retrieval into a spatial piece (his answering phrases arrive FROM a direction). Honest unknown: phrase-boundary musicality + retrieval weight balance are untuned by ear.

## 2026-08-20 (cycle 1187 — SHIPPED `15488-vigil-chord`, DEEP ×3) — VIGIL: his music only lives while you physically sustain it; let go and it forgets

Cycle 1187 went **DEEP** on the jury 2026-08-19's two loudest asks — **give the human back a load-bearing verb** (~10 of 15 ships had a passive/self-propelling primary relationship, now banned) and **extend the conceptual/critical lane** whose subject is "the thing you can only hear by giving something up" (the toll/negative ceiling the floor-metric can't see). ONE concept — **VIGIL: Karel's music only sounds while you sustain an unbroken commitment; the whole ensemble exists only at the peak, and the instant you let go it forgets** — raced via **three divergent sustained-human-action surfaces** sharing the same thesis + Messiaen colour-chord palette (RESEARCH §1187: Hirshhorn *Failed Future Bodies* endurance-as-instrument + Messiaen *cité céleste* stained glass). Shipped **`15488-vigil-chord`** (hold six keys A–H to keep his six-voice chord alive — the sharpest one-sentence concept for a pianist, the most load-bearing verb, and the catalog as a *literal simultaneous chord* = jury #4 corpus-not-filter; three.js stained glass, full-chromatic Messiaen, clears every ban). Banked the two runners-up:

### `15472-vigil-breath` — the same VIGIL concept via a sustained HUM / held tone (breath as the vigil)
- **Brief / what was built:** primary input = a continuous sustained vocal hum measured by a **separate** mic AnalyserNode (mic = control layer only, never routed to speakers — rule-10 clean); while the tone holds unbroken, 7 of his real takes (Welcome Home + Snowflake) layer in one at a time (~1.2s first, +1 every ~3s, full at ~19s) into a three.js Messiaen rose window that fills petal by petal; a 350ms grace ignores natural dips, a real breath/stop breaks it → voices shed in reverse (~480ms each), meter turns `text-destructive` "the vigil broke — it is forgetting". Live vigil meter (unbroken time, voices N/7, live hum level vs threshold, longest-vigil stat). Auto press-and-hold-Space fallback on mic denial. **Build-VERIFIED** by the builder (installed node_modules → project ESLint 0/0 + full-project `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors). 778-line page + README, rm-rf'd pre-build, never committed.
- **Why banked / how to resurrect:** the most *embodied* of the three (continuous vocal effort, closest to `15248-coherence`'s breath lane) and it's build-clean — strongest resurrection is to **ship it standalone next cycle** (the breath verb is distinct enough from the held-chord to be its own piece), OR fold it into `15488-vigil-chord` as a second "breath mode" toggle on the same vigil state-machine. Deepening hooks from its README: **shared vigils** (two visitors each hold distinct voices, the window lights only when everyone holds — collective cost, closest to *Failed Future Bodies*); **pitch-matched summon** (hum near a voice's key to choose which take joins next — the vigil is tuned, not just endured); **the memory of the lapse** (ghost the collapsed window as a fading after-image + a persistent reliquary of how long presence held).

### `15504-vigil-hold` — the same VIGIL concept via ONE long unbroken press earned over ~55s (DOM/SVG rose)
- **Brief / what was built:** single continuous press-and-hold (pointer or spacebar); a vigil charges only while held, his 7 takes enter at rising time thresholds (~3s Interplay → ~52s Snowflake), the full ensemble only after ~55s unbroken. Release does NOT pause — it **collapses** (exponential decay τ≈0.5s, voices fall in reverse, light retreats inward), so skimming is impossible; strict dead-man's-switch (pointerup/cancel/blur/visibility all count as letting go). Rendered as a **DOM + inline-SVG + CSS** cathedral rose (~90 annular-sector `<path>` panes across 6 rings + oculus, per-ring Messiaen `radialGradient`, CSS brightness/saturate + screen-blend bloom, analyser shimmer — deliberately NOT WebGL, to diversify the race). Could not run `npm run build` in-env (verified by hand against the build-safety checklist). 672-line page + README, rm-rf'd pre-build, never committed.
- **Why banked / how to resurrect:** the purest **toll-spirit "will you stay?"** endurance piece and the only DOM/SVG surface of the three — resurrect when the diversity audit wants a non-WebGL register, or ship as the "long-form patience" companion. Its ~55s commitment is the most demanding; but a single hold is the least *active* verb (patience, not continuous effort) — that's exactly why the held-chord won on "the human is load-bearing". Deepening hooks: a **steadiness** dimension (micro-jitter slows the charge — reward holding *still*, not just holding), and **collapse leaves a residue** (a faint after-image of how deep the last vigil reached — repeated failed attempts accumulate a visible history of almosts).

## 2026-08-19 (cycle 1185 — SHIPPED `15424-negative`, DEEP ×2) — listen to the NEGATIVE SPACE of his recordings: foreground the silences/breath/room-tone between his notes, duck the notes to a ghost via an inverted-gain envelope

ONE genuinely new verb the lab had never done in 1,184 pieces — **the negative space of his music as the subject**: the union of note-active intervals from `notes[]` is REMOVED, its complement (rests, pedal-lift air, breath, reverb tails) is FOREGROUND, via a per-frame inverted-gain envelope (duck notes to a floor lerped by a REVEAL steer, open the interstices to ~0.95). Research §1185: "The Music of Silence" (Di Liberto et al., J Neurosci 2021) — the brain answers a rest with an *inverted-polarity* imagined response tracking the expected note; the inverted gain plays the ghost the cortex is already generating. Named refs: Cage *4′33″*, Miles Davis "the notes you don't play", notan. Built via TWO divergent surfaces; shipped **`15424-negative`** (the Canvas2D inverted piano-roll — his notes are DARK HOLES punched out of a luminous full-chromatic field; the concept reads unmistakably on the visual channel). Banked the runner-up:

- **Brief / what was built:** identical inverted-gain interstitial engine (`engine.ts`: `mergeIntervals` / `noteIntervalsFromNotes` with a 0.06s release pad / `noteIntervalsFromRms` amplitude fallback / `noteActiveAt` binary-search+lookahead / `revealFloor`). **Surface:** a `THREE.Points` volumetric field (~4200) with additive-blended custom GLSL — blooms OPEN and saturates with full-chromatic color (hue from the current chord root around the whole wheel) during his silences, collapses to a dark core the instant his notes sound; the SAME gate drives audio + light; analyser-driven room-tone shimmer; WebGL + `prefers-reduced-motion` degrade; full teardown. Deepening hooks: shape the void's turbulence from the interstice *length* (a long breath vs a quick rest reads differently); let the withheld notes leave a faint after-image trail so you feel the melody's ghost the neuroscience describes.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1184 — SHIPPED `15392-recur`, DEEP ×2) — the catalog composes a NEW movement of itself across three time-scales, and draws its own form as accumulating recurrence arcs

Hawley, "Helping Music Co-Creation Agents 'Listen' Well" (arXiv:2608.04378, 2026-08-05) — musical properties decode at levels tracking their time-scale (phrase-structure coarse, harmony/note fine); + David Cope EMI (recombinant composition) + Lamere Infinite Jukebox. Shipped **`15392-recur`** (Canvas2D recurrence-map surface — the returning-theme arcs accumulate into the visible shape of the movement); banked the runner-up:

### `15376-movement` — the same self-composing engine rendered as a DOM/type "living score" (the legible/readable arm)
- **Brief / what was built:** identical hierarchical-composition core (FORM `settle→gather→peak→return→rest` arc steering density/register; PHRASE selection by circle-of-fifths voice-leading + chord-quality + chord-histogram cosine + contour continuity + form-fit + per-track anti-repetition softmax; MOTIF memory capturing early distinctive phrases as themes and returning them, transposed via `playbackRate`, at arc return points). **Surface:** a living program-note written in real time — FORM header + segmented position bar, scrolling phrase rows (`title · mm:ss · harmony · per-row Canvas2D contour sparkline · match %`), analyser-driven pulse on the sounding row, `↩ theme returns — first heard 00:41 · +7` motif markers, a 16-track "voices heard" strip, ~40-row cap. Deepen: two simultaneous voices (a slow FORM-scale bass under the PHRASE-scale melody, both real slices); cadence-aware form boundaries (place `return` on real authentic cadences in `chords[]`); theme transformation beyond transposition (augmentation/diminution via `playbackRate`, drawn on the sparkline).

**Deepening hooks for `15392-recur` (shipped) — for a future DEEP:** (a) **section rhyme** — detect when two spans of the map are self-similar and draw a second class of arc (form-level rhyme, not just motif return); (b) **motif development** — return themes fragmented/inverted/augmented, drawing the transformation on the arc; (c) **export the score** — write the chosen `(trackId, start, end, transpose)` sequence so a composed movement replays deterministically; (d) **real cadence detection** to end the `rest` stage on an authentic cadence rather than a phrase count.

## 2026-08-19 (cycle 1182 — SHIPPED `15328-bloomwork`, WIDE ×3) — a recording that grows itself into a Fibonacci seed-head; two strong banked runners-up

Cycle 1182 went **WIDE** — both jury-named ceiling targets are now extended (`pulse`→`15248-coherence` §1180; `answerback`→`15312-transcript` §1181), and three DEEPs ran back-to-back, so the alternation called for breadth. Three maximally-divergent non-simulation briefs, each dodging all four standing jury bans (raw-WebGL2 / inline-SVG / cool-teal / recording-drives-a-physics-sim). Research §1182: "Music Visualization Using Dynamic Phyllotactic Patterns" (Archives of Design Research, 2026) — phyllotaxis as *growth*, not pattern. Shipped **`15328-bloomwork`** (three.js · full-chromatic · phyllotactic real-catalog growth · passive-catalog input) — the research-chained arm, the jury's rare full-chromatic register (breaking a 3× achromatic streak), a fresh three.js surface (0× last-10), and it reads his actual note-roll as chroma-hue seeds. The two runners-up are fully built + banked (rule-10 clean, `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed):

### `15344-toll` — "a recording you can't hear without paying a cost": subtractive/irreversible listening (⭐⭐⭐⭐ the sharpest CONCEPT of the three)
- **Brief / what was built:** the jury's most-repeated 0×-in-window ask (conceptual/critical, provocation #2). A real track is cut into a finite **ledger of phrases** (24–52 slices on `chords[]` onsets, else even windows). At rest it is **silent** — you are not owed the recording. To hear a phrase you **spend** it: press-and-hold a ledger cell past a ~280ms commit threshold (or focus+Enter) → it burns red, plays exactly once (`bufferSource.start(when,offset,dur)` through `createSafeMaster`), then becomes a permanent **void** — struck to a dash in the DOM grid and torn out of a Canvas2D timeline bar. Spent state persists per-track for the session; **no reset button** by design. Fully spent = blank timeline, all-dash ledger, red `0`. Achromatic ink-on-bone + one `destructive` red for the act of spending. tsc-clean, 0 lint errors (per builder). Files: `page.tsx` + README.
- **Why banked not shipped:** the strongest *concept*, but (a) its register is close to `14848-holdlight` (achromatic conceptual "the piece remembers what you neglected" — toll is the subtractive inverse), and (b) shipping it would be a **4th achromatic** in a row (1179/1180/1181 all grayscale) where the jury wants a whole cycle committed to grayscale *or* full-chromatic — `bloomwork` makes the fresher palette commitment. Also a self-noted **light-theme deviation** (Canvas ink-on-bone doesn't re-theme for a dark root) to clean up before shipping.
- **Resurrect how:** ship next as its own cycle — it is a **ship-bank ⭐⭐⭐⭐ candidate** and the cleanest hit on the conceptual/critical menu the lab is thinnest on. Before shipping: re-theme the Canvas for the dark root (or make ink-on-bone a deliberate, full-committed light piece), consider zero-crossing/cadence-aware slice edges, and decide whether `localStorage` persistence (a phrase spent is gone across reloads too) sharpens or bricks the concept. Deepening: let a spent phrase be **gifted** to a second listener (multi-user cost — the jury's still-0× true-multi-user lane).

### `15360-sediment` — playing the recording lays down rock: spectral sedimentation as a readable core sample (⭐⭐⭐ the long-form/stateful arm)
- **Brief / what was built:** the long-form/stateful lane (jury menu). While a track plays, every ~0.4s deposits one horizontal **stratum** condensed from `safeMaster.analyser`: thickness = loudness, colour = spectral centroid on a warm-stone gradient, internal banding = sub/low/mid/high split, dark seam on onsets, harmonic vein from `chords[]` (pale amber major / deep umber minor). Strata stack into an **isometric core sample in Canvas2D** (orthographic yaw/pitch, painter-sorted walls + light-shaded faces) you pointer-orbit + tilt (device-orientation optional, iOS-gated, pointer fallback). A **Depth** slider scrubs the record and "Hear layer" replays any stratum's time-slice through `createSafeMaster`. Monotonic record, whole-column-fit — minute five is a thick document of everything played. Warm stone/amber (a register absent from the recent window). Files: `page.tsx` + README.
- **Why banked not shipped:** solid and genuinely long-form, but (a) spectrogram-as-terrain is a more *expected* idea than bloomwork's growth or toll's cost, (b) the warm palette is not a jury-*blessed* register (the jury named grayscale + full-chromatic as the healthy ones; warm was a prior ban that merely swung to 0), and (c) a self-noted anti-pattern (React state pushed every frame). Least surprising of the three.
- **Resurrect how:** ship when the audit wants the long-form/stateful lane or a deliberate warm cycle. Deepen: let the depth-scrub **drill into** the core (an interior slice, not just the outer walls), sediment TWO interleaved tracks as distinct mineral bands (cross-album stratigraphy), or export the finished core as a still.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1181 — SHIPPED `15312-transcript`, DEEP ×2) — the catalog in a real two-way conversation with itself (answerback's named ceiling extension); one banked runner-up

### `15296-conversation` — three.js achromatic "catalog constellation": the conversation as a luminous thread hopping node→node across his catalog (⭐⭐⭐⭐ strong; the immersive/spatial sibling)
- **Brief / what was built:** the SAME self-continuing-conversation concept via a **three.js achromatic constellation** instead of a transcript. The 16 (loaded: 7) tracks are silver nodes on a golden-spiral sphere; the conversation is a luminous **thread hopping node→node** as the chain walks, a travelling pulse crossing each hop, the current phrase's contour bending the thread, each node brightening/growing with its visit count (you watch the whole body of work get drawn in) and the active node blooming on `safeMaster.analyser`. Identical retrieval core to the winner: `buildBank` (4/6/8-note windows → contour + pitch-class histogram + local chord context + exact times), `harmonicFit` (circle-of-fifths root proximity + V→I/IV→I resolution bonus + chord-chroma overlap), anti-latch `pickNext` (Wander = pool width & softmax temperature; Spread = least-heard bias; excludes previous; `nudge` skips the closest half), `queryFromCandidate` closes the memory loop, `PhrasePlayer` slices real audio through `createSafeMaster().input`. WebGL-absent → the live text transcript in the corner is the full fallback. Files: `page.tsx` + `catalog.ts` + `scene.ts` + `input.ts` + README. Refs same as winner.
- **Why banked not shipped:** both cleared the floor identically (#2 ≥4 subsystems: multi-track loader + note/chord analysis + contour+harmonic retrieval + memory/anti-latch chain + real-audio phrase player + renderer · #3 named refs Infinite Jukebox 2012 + arXiv:2608.04543 + arXiv:2606.11886 · #5 research §1181) and dodged all four jury bans (achromatic not cool-teal; retrieval not sim; self-propel+optional-keyboard not pointer-primary; three.js not inline-SVG). `transcript` won on SURFACE (DOM/type is the jury's named rare-healthy output and a *fresher* surface for this lineage — answerback itself was three.js, so `conversation` re-uses answerback's own surface), on LEGIBILITY (the transcript makes "a real two-way conversation" readable — which track answered which, with contour + harmony + match % — where the constellation's thread is prettier but shows the "why" less), and on the conceptual/critical register the jury named thinnest. `conversation` is the more immersive/beautiful piece and the stronger cosmic-ambient sibling; its risk is exactly that it re-treads answerback's three.js surface + the lab's crowded constellation/graph-viz register.
- **Resurrect how:** ship as its own cycle when the audit wants an immersive spatial piece (the node→node thread over the whole catalog is a genuinely lovely map of the conversation) — or **graft the constellation as a second view onto `transcript`** (a toggle between "read it" and "watch it traverse the catalog"). Deepen toward research §1181's future hooks: a **branching** conversation (each turn offers 2–3 candidate forks you steer — the retrieval graph made navigable, closer to the version-ID *graph* the fresh source is about); **two catalogs in dialogue** (Welcome Home answering Snowflake — cross-*album*); or **DTW-aligned contour** matching to reward ornamented local alignment.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1180 — SHIPPED `15248-coherence`, DEEP ×2) — breathe his recording out of a veil (respiratory coherence, pulse's 2nd biosignal); one banked runner-up

Cycle 1180 went **DEEP** on ONE concept, jury-directed (JURY 2026-08-18: "the constraint is CEILING — get a THIRD 4/5 piece; extend `pulse` or `answerback`; DEEP one of them"): **respiratory COHERENCE as `15152-pulse`'s named second biosignal** — a fixed 0.1 Hz (6 breaths·min⁻¹) resonance-frequency pacer, a coherence estimator that scores how well the visitor's breathing phase-locks to it, and that score **lifts ONE real take out of a veil** (muffled+distant → clear+present). Not a simulation, not a mixer: a *reward loop* on a real recording. Research §1180 (Sci Reports 2026 RCT: fixed 0.1 Hz ≈ individualized RF → pace fixed, reward phase-lock; Lehrer & Vaschillo; Sensors 2023). Raced 2 technical approaches. Shipped **`15248-coherence`** — WebGL2 achromatic "breath lens" (caustic focusing to a still point; lowpass 320 Hz→18 kHz + gain + receding delay-tail veil-lift; coherence = windowed Pearson PLV + inhale-peak rate-match + variance gate). Won on surface diversity (raw WebGL2 = jury's rare *healthy* surface), surprise (optics-of-focus metaphor), robustness (variance gate rejects false-coherence-from-silence), and full achromatic commitment. The runner-up is fully built + banked (rule-10 clean — one real take "Bath" through `createSafeMaster`, 0 oscillators, mic dead-ends at an analyser; guided-demo fallback, full teardown, prefers-reduced-motion, no banned chrome, drug-clean — `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed):

### `15264-tidebreath` — Canvas2D "tide horizon / aperture to light": his take approaches from far as your breath locks to the pace (⭐⭐⭐⭐ strong; the more legible breathing-guide sibling)
- **Brief / what was built:** the SAME respiratory-coherence concept via a **Canvas2D grayscale aperture/horizon** instead of a WebGL2 caustic. A circular aperture **dilates on the pacer inhale and contracts on the exhale** — the dilation IS the breathing guide, arguably a clearer, more instructable cue than coherence's whole-field luminance breath — and both rises up the frame and brightens toward silver as coherence→1; a low horizon-of-light climbs toward center; iris rings + a luminous "heart" near full coherence. Audio is an **"approach from far"** distance chain (distinct from a filter sweep): dry/wet + feedback-delay tail + highpass (500 Hz far → 40 Hz near) + gain, so at coherence 0 the take is a reverberant, thinned, quiet across-the-room presence and at 1 it is dry, full-band, at-your-ear. Coherence = 0.7·phaseScore (running PLV via `<breath·pacer>` exponential average) + 0.3·rateScore (breath period from rising zero-crossings vs 10 s), with fast/slow envelope followers subtracting the mic baseline to extract the slow breath oscillation, and a rate-credit decay if breathing stalls. Live track-swap without teardown. Files: `page.tsx` + README. Refs: Lehrer & Vaschillo (RF HRV biofeedback); Sci Reports 2026 RCT (RF vs fixed 0.1 Hz); Sensors 2023 (guiding breath at RF).
- **Why banked not shipped:** both cleared the floor identically (#1 breath-coherence = lab-first biosignal+reward-loop · #2 ≥4 subsystems · #5 research §1180) and dodged all four jury bans. `coherence` won on surface (raw WebGL2 is the jury's explicitly-named rare *healthy* output; Canvas2D is the rut just escaped — "not a sixth SVG… WebGL2 or DOM/type"), on surprise (a caustic *coming into focus* is a newer visual register than aperture/tunnel-to-light, which brushes the lab's cosmic-ambient history), and on the variance-gated windowed Pearson correlation (more defensible against false coherence from silence). `tidebreath` is the more *legible* piece — the dilating aperture teaches the breath better and the "approach from far" framing is viscerally clear; its risk is Canvas2D redundancy and a tunnel-to-light register the void-history has touched.
- **Resurrect how:** ship as its own cycle when the audit wants a legible, instructional meditative piece (the aperture-as-breath-guide is the strongest teaching cue in either build) — or **graft the aperture-guide onto `coherence`** as a next-cycle deepening (coherence's whole-field luminance breath is subtle; a small explicit dilating ring would make the pace unmissable). Deepen toward research §1180's future hook: a **combined breath+heartbeat (RSA) coherence** piece — reuse pulse's camera-PPG and score respiratory sinus arrhythmia (does HR rise on the inhale?), the lab's first two-biosignal interoceptive piece; or a **breath-conducted duet** where coherence crossfades two real takes.

# Resonance Dream IDEAS — living queue

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1179 — SHIPPED `15200-schlieren`, DEEP ×2) — see his recording as disturbed AIR (schlieren); one banked runner-up

Cycle 1179 went **DEEP** on ONE never-used concept — **make the invisible medium visible: render one real take as SCHLIEREN, the optical technique whose literal purpose is to photograph density gradients / sound in air** (research §1179; grep INDEX: `schlieren`/`shadowgraph`/`diffraction` = 0 in 1,178 pieces). A "room, not a mixer" by construction: ONE source forcing a density field you watch billow, no track-gains. Raced 2 optical models. Shipped **`15200-schlieren`** — a classic **knife-edge grayscale shadowgraph** (WebGL2 ping-pong damped-wave field forced by his band-energy/onsets → central-difference ∇ρ projected on a rotatable knife-edge → luminous monochrome plumes; drag rotates the knife = a genuine schlieren control, 0× in lab). Won on the purest/most-legible expression of the technique, a novel physics-control interaction, and the jury-flagged rare-*healthy* achromatic register. The runner-up is fully built + banked (rule-10 clean — one real take through `createSafeMaster`, 0 oscillators; graceful WebGL2/audio fallback, full teardown, no banned chrome, drug-clean — `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed):

### `15216-shadowgraph` — Background-Oriented Schlieren: a star-field you look THROUGH, lensing as his music disturbs the air (⭐⭐⭐⭐ strong; the cosmic-ambient sibling of schlieren)
- **Brief / what was built:** the SAME "see sound as disturbed air" concept via the **modern synthetic / Background-Oriented Schlieren (BOS)** model instead of a knife-edge. The invisible "air" is a damped 2D wave on GPU ping-pong float textures (384² RGBA32F); a dim **procedural starfield** is sampled at a displaced coordinate — the textbook BOS `uv' = uv + k·∇density` — so the stars apparent-shift, smear, and **lens** where his recording stirs the medium, like a horizon warping over hot ground. His low/mid/high band energies + RMS pulse three fixed Gaussian emitters (signed per-emitter phase so plumes breathe in/out, not pile up); loud passages raise `k` so the whole field lenses harder; a pointer emitter lets you push the air and watch stars lens around the cursor. Filmic tonemap + 9-tap bloom + trails over a near-black void; cool near-white on `#03040a` (essentially achromatic). Full-bleed immersive canvas (absolute inset-0) with a HUD (track selector, Refraction `k` slider, low/mid/high disturbance meters). Files: `page.tsx` + `field.ts` (raw WebGL2 substrate) + `glsl.ts` (SIM/BOS/PRESENT programs) + `audio.ts` + README. Refs: Settles *Schlieren and Shadowgraph Techniques* (2001); Dalziel/Hughes/Sutherland "synthetic schlieren" (2000); Raffel BOS review (2015).
- **Why banked not shipped:** both cleared the floor identically (#1 schlieren = lab-first · #2 ≥4 subsystems: real-audio loader + band analysis + GPU wave field + optical-displacement render + pointer control · #5 research §1179). schlieren won on (a) higher SURPRISE / lower redundancy — the classic monochrome knife-edge shadowgraph is a genuinely NEW visual register for a lab thick with cosmic star-void/particle pieces, whereas BOS's shimmering-starfield reads adjacent to existing lensing/void work; (b) the knife-edge-rotation is a more distinctly-*schlieren*, more novel control than "drag to push air" (many fluid pieces have the latter); (c) it lands the jury's rare-healthy achromatic register most purely. shadowgraph is the more immersive, more instantly-cosmic-ambient piece — the SAFER, more beautiful unattended-morning demo — its risk is that it reads as "pretty shimmer" rather than "sound made visible."
- **Resurrect how:** ship as its own cosmic-ambient cycle when the palette audit wants the void register back — pairs with schlieren as a "same physics, knife-edge vs BOS" diptych for an ear/eye-check. Deepen toward the research §1179 future hook: a **3D volumetric schlieren** (raymarch the density volume, integrate ∇n along each ray) so his recording becomes a luminous plume you *orbit*, not a flat field — the lab's first raymarched-volume optics. Or drive the background from a real front-camera feed (rule 10 keeps that secondary).
