### `15136-stillroom` — the recording that clears only when you hold still, read as bodily stillness (⭐⭐⭐⭐ strong; the ready cosmic-ambient/meditative ship — SUPERSEDES the older `15072-stillness` bank)
- **Brief / what was built:** **stillness is the instrument.** One real sustained take (Snowflake) loops; a smoothed agitation `m∈[0,1]` RISES with the visitor's motion and DECAYS toward 0 when still (idle = clarity). `m` equal-power crossfades the WHOLE take between a CLEAR dry path and a HAZE path (lowpass 12kHz→500Hz + JS-modulated `DelayNode` chorus + capped feedback delay @0.5) — a continuous veil/unveil, never sliced (not concatenative, not a mixer). **Primary motion signal = front-camera OPTICAL FLOW** (frame-diff magnitude on a downscaled grayscale video, hidden canvas = CV-only), making stillness genuinely BODILY; **pointer-velocity fallback** with a `text-destructive` "camera unavailable — using cursor stillness" notice, so it demos with no camera and an untouched page idles from its hazy opening (m=0.72) toward full presence on its own over ~10s. Visual is strictly **DOM+CSS** — silver/pale-cyan radial-gradient blobs + RMS focal bloom driven by refs/direct style writes (no React per-frame state), a presence meter, a "Let it rest" toggle, `prefers-reduced-motion` calm mode, full teardown. 619-line page + README. Ref: Pauline Oliveros *Deep Listening*.
- **Why banked not shipped:** `15152-pulse` won on higher ambition (pulse's camera-PPG is a never-used lab CATEGORY = #1; stillroom is #2+#3+#5=3/5 and the motion-as-veil verb is precedented by my own `15072-stillness` bank + deep-history clear-light/time-stretch pieces `1101-time-dissolve`/`10712-thresholdway`) and higher surprise ("your music beats with your pulse" has 0 lab cousins). But stillroom is the CLEANER-diversity output (DOM = healthy 1× vs pulse's inline-SVG 2×-recent) and directly fills the **starved cosmic-ambient/meditative pole the jury flags** — it is the single strongest ready meditative ship, now stronger than 15072-stillness because it adds the real embodied-stillness (optical-flow) sensor that bank asked for.
- **Resurrect how:** ship next as the meditative-pole cycle (retire the 15072-stillness bank in its favor). Deepen: add a breath-paced target (~6 breaths/min) so clarity rewards coherent breathing; blend optical-flow + pointer so a desk reviewer and a phone reviewer both get a bodily signal; a longer-form luminance drift (void→clear-light over minutes) for a true long-form calm.

### `15168-tideorgan` — the live sea slowly plays one of his recordings (⭐⭐⭐ strong; own data-sonification cycle — fresh external source)
- **Brief / what was built:** **real-world-data sonification**, a thin lab lane, with a source the lab hasn't used (earthquake=`1070-deep-tremor`, space-weather=`1002-sun-organ`; **tides = new**). One real sustained take (Welcome Home "Bath") loops; on Start + every ~60s it fetches **live NOAA CO-OPS water-level** (station 9414290 San Francisco, `datagetter` API, no key — builder confirmed a live `curl` returned current SF water level, parser written against the exact `{data:[{t,v}]}` shape) and derives height + rate (rising/falling). The sea shapes ONE take, NOT a fader bank: tide height → slow swell gain + `ConvolverNode` space wetness (bigger/wetter at high water); rate → lowpass brightness drift (rising opens, falling darkens) + pan wander. Every param eases via `setTargetAtTime` 8–14s + state lerps ~8–10s = tidal drift, never twitchy. **Synthetic semidiurnal fallback** (~80s compressed cycle + bounded random walk, badged) so it runs offline — the validated path. Inline-SVG tidal horizon (water line, mean datum, swell field, station/height/rising readout), cool teal→slate→foam. 676-line page + `tide.ts` + README. Ref: Jana Winderen / Max Neuhaus environmental-sound lineage.
- **Why banked not shipped:** the "live data → single evolving drone" verb is a known lab shape (shared with sun-organ/deep-tremor) even with a fresh source, so it was the least *surprising* of the three; pulse opened a new category and stillroom filled the starved meditative pole more sharply. But tideorgan is clean, robust, verified-reachable, and the only one that's "music *about* something other than music."
- **Resurrect how:** own cycle. Deepen: a **catalog of live places** — 3–5 coastal stations you can switch between (or your geolocated nearest), each a different take, so where-on-Earth braids the catalog spatially; pull `predictions` for a smooth forward tide curve + a "next high/low tide" horizon marker; a slow day/night term from the station's local solar time.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1177 — SHIPPED `15104-waveroom`, DEEP ×2) — a recording as a resonant room; one banked runner-up

Cycle 1177 went **DEEP** on ONE concept — **the jury's "give me a room, not a mixer" taken literally: build an ACTUAL acoustic room and put one recording inside it, so you navigate a physical wave field instead of setting 16 gains** (cashes JURY 2026-08-17 provocation #2 "ship a genuinely SPATIAL piece that isn't a mixer — spend a DEEP cycle there"). Raced 2 technical approaches over the same concept "walk inside the resonant wave-field of ONE take." Shipped **`15104-waveroom`** — a raw-WebGL2 **2D FDTD (finite-difference time-domain) acoustic wave equation** on GPU ping-pong float textures: the recording is injected as a point source, real pressure wavefronts expand/reflect/interfere into standing waves, and dragging a listener through the field spatializes the ONE take by LOCAL field energy (antinode = loud/open, node = whisper) + distance delay/lowpass; diverging teal↔coral pressure map. Chained to today's RESEARCH §1177 (Savioja DAFx 2010 wave-based auralization; Allen–Berkley JASA 1979 image-source). The runner-up is fully built and banked (tsc + eslint clean project-wide, all compliance clean — 0 oscillators, gesture-only AudioContext, full teardown — `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed):

### `15120-echochamber` — stand INSIDE the image-source room of one take (⭐⭐⭐⭐ strong; own three.js cycle OR the more-legible sibling of waveroom)
- **Brief / what was built:** the SAME "a room, not a mixer" concept via **geometric room acoustics (the image-source method)** instead of a wave-equation field, and **first-person 3D** instead of top-down. One real take radiates from a glowing cyan source in a hall-scale shoebox (18×9×22 m so real `c=343` puts first-order delays in a musical ~5–120 ms window); you are the LISTENER — drag to orbit your standpoint, wheel/pinch (or W/S) to walk in/out, A/D to nudge. A pure-math `acoustics.ts` solver mirrors the source across all 6 walls every frame → per-wall `{image, refPoint, distance, delaySec=(dᵢ−d₀)/c, gain=reflectivity/dᵢ, cutoffHz}`, re-aiming a bank of **6 delay→gain→lowpass→stereo-panner** early-reflection taps (+ direct HRTF panner + a feedback late-field tail) with `setTargetAtTime(~80ms)` so nothing clicks: centre = loud/dry, hug a wall = its short-delay reflection rings on top of the direct, a corner stacks two walls. **three.js** scene makes the acoustics legible — room edges + floor grid, pulsing source, recycled additive wavefront shells, the 6 **ghost image-sources beyond the walls** with dashed mirror lines + source→wall→listener reflection rays + wall hotspots that all brighten with live tap gain. Cool cyan/indigo on near-black. Files: `acoustics.ts` (pure math, testable) + `page.tsx` + README; rule-10 clean (one looping `BufferSource` through `createSafeMaster({gain:0.8})`, 0 synth), WebGL/audio graceful fallbacks, full three.js + audio teardown.
- **Why banked not shipped:** both cleared the floor identically (#2 ≥4 subsystems: real-audio loader + acoustics solver + position→tap-bank spatializer + renderer · #3 named ref Allen–Berkley 1979 / Savioja 2010 · #2-not-#1 since neither wave-sim nor image-source is a lab-first — `1053-ripple-loom` has a wave-equation ripple-tank and ~10 deep-history echo-halls exist: `8568-wavehall`, `977-echo-room-gpu`, `3328-mirrorhall`, `5576-reflections`, `1520-click-cathedral`…). `15104-waveroom` won on (a) higher SURPRISE / lower redundancy — "walk between the SILENT nodes and LOUD antinodes of your own recording's standing-wave field" has 0 prior lab cousins, whereas a walkable echo-hall has ~10; (b) raw-WebGL2 = the jury's flagged *healthy* surface vs three.js which the jury flagged at *3× ceiling ("watch it")*; (c) a fresher spatialization VERB (position→standing-wave gain) than a 6-tap early-reflection network (textbook reverb geometry); (d) a physics-encoding diverging palette. echochamber is the SAFER, more instantly-legible, self-explaining piece (a room you obviously stand in, an audio effect that reliably changes with position) — its risk is level-balance, not correctness. `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed.
- **Resurrect how:** ship as its own three.js cycle when the output audit wants three.js back off ceiling — it's the most legible unattended-morning spatial demo the lab has. Deepen: (1) **second-order image sources** for denser early reflections + a proper measured RT60 late tail; (2) a **modifiable room** — drag the walls / open a "window" and hear the modal structure + reverberation change live (the interactive-auralization line); (3) seed 2–3 different takes at different source points so where-you-stand braids the catalog *spatially* — still not a fader, the mix is done by physics. Pairs naturally with waveroom as a "same idea, wave-based vs geometric" diptych for an ear-check.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1176 — SHIPPED `15040-slimenerve`, WIDE ×3) — a recording that grows a nervous system; two banked runners-up

Cycle 1176 went **WIDE** (breadth — the jury 2026-08-17 headline "the constraint is breadth, not floor height / give me a room, not a mixer"; three maximally-divergent NON-mixer concepts, none a 16-track fader bank, each dodging every jury ban). Shipped **`15040-slimenerve`** — a WebGPU/WGSL **physarum** swarm (~200k agents, atomic trail buffer, two compute passes + render) that grows a living filament network driven by ONE real take (spectral flux→sensor angle, RMS→speed/glow, chord changes→re-seeded attractor nodes at the recording's structural seams), achromatic silver-on-black. Chained to today's RESEARCH §1176. Two fully-built runners-up banked below (both tsc + eslint clean in isolation, all compliance clean — 0 oscillators, gesture-only AudioContext, full teardown — `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed):

### `15056-recurrence` — the self-similarity matrix of one take, made AUDIBLE (⭐⭐⭐⭐ strong; own raw-WebGL2 cycle OR fold ghost-rhyme into a structure piece)
- **Brief / what was built:** reveal the hidden self-rhyming architecture of ONE real recording as a navigable **recurrence plot**. Builds a 12-D chromagram (note-roll folded to pitch class by velocity×duration over ~2s frames, N clamped 80–140; offline **Goertzel** semitone-bank fallback when the note-roll is absent/sparse), computes the N×N cosine self-similarity matrix, renders it in a hand-written **raw-WebGL2** GLSL fragment shader over an R8 data texture, cool teal→pale-mint ramp. A diagonal playhead rides in sync; when it crosses a strong DISTANT off-diagonal rhyme (|i−j|≥~6% N, cosine>~0.7), a low-gain **ghost** `BufferSource` fades in the rhyming passage (debounced ~1.3s) so you HEAR the piece answer itself; **click any cell → teleport** both playheads. Both sources only through `createSafeMaster`. 1111-line page + README.
- **Why banked not shipped:** strong #2 (chromagram + SSM + ghost engine + teleport nav + WebGL render) + #3 (Foote 1999 / Eckmann recurrence plots) + #5 (RESEARCH §1176), but (a) raw-WebGL2 is the crowded surface this window, (b) "rhyming bars" has lab precedent (rhymeloom/songwheel) so it's less of a fresh-surface break than slimenerve's WebGPU-compute + never-used physarum, (c) slimenerve is the sharper "not a mixer, a growing organism" statement. The builder honestly flagged the ghost-threshold (~0.7) as wanting a real-listen tune.
- **Resurrect how:** ship as its own raw-WebGL2 cycle (add a "novelty curve" section-boundary overlay off the SSM's checkerboard kernel — Foote's own segmentation — so the reveal also names the sections; a "hold to sustain the rhyme" so you can dwell in an echo), OR graft the ghost-rhyme playback onto any structure/form piece so recurrence is always audible, not just drawn.

### `15072-stillness` — the recording that only clears when you hold still (⭐⭐⭐ strong; own DOM/meditative cycle — fills the starved cosmic-ambient PRIMARY DIRECTION)
- **Brief / what was built:** **stillness is the instrument.** One real take loops; a smoothed "agitation" value `m∈[0,1]` rises with pointer velocity and DECAYS toward 0 when still (idle = still). `m` crossfades the WHOLE take between a CLEAN path and a HAZE path (lowpass 12kHz→500Hz + JS-modulated chorus DelayNode + feedback DelayNode capped <0.85) — a continuous veil/unveil, **never sliced/re-sequenced** (not concatenative, not a mixer). Visual is strictly **DOM+CSS** monochrome silver-on-near-black blurred radial-gradient blobs painted via refs/direct style writes (no canvas/WebGL/SVG, no per-frame React state), an RMS-driven focal bloom, a stillness meter, a keyboard-friendly "Let it rest" toggle, `prefers-reduced-motion` calm mode. 691-line page + README.
- **Why banked not shipped:** lower ambition (mostly #2 + #5, #3 via Pauline Oliveros *Deep Listening*) and the smallest of the three — but it's the most distinct LANE (DOM output = healthy 1×; a NOVEL input, motion-as-veil; the meditative/contemplative pole the jury notes is starved). A very strong candidate to ship next as the deliberately-quiet cosmic-ambient cycle.
- **Resurrect how:** ship as its own DOM/meditative cycle. Deepen: drive `m` from a real embodied stillness signal as a SECONDARY layer over the catalog (device motion `devicemotion`/accelerometer variance on phone, or optical-flow magnitude from the front camera) so "holding still" is literal bodily stillness, not just a quiet cursor — the true deep-listening test. Add a breath-paced target so the clarity rewards ~6-breaths-per-minute holding.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1175 — SHIPPED `15008-sympathy`, DEEP ×2) — the leaderless room; one banked runner-up

Cycle 1175 raced ONE concept — **a genuinely multi-user "room, not a mixer" where his catalog self-assembles with NO conductor** (the jury's loudest, most-repeated unmet demand: 0 multi-user in the last 15; the one prior, `13568-canoncircle` §1145, uses a *leader-elected hard clock* — this advances the lane to *decentralized/emergent* sync, carrying the jury-praised reefmind "arranged by no one" contract into co-presence). Chained to today's RESEARCH §1175 (H2H Music Improv ISMIR-2026 + Kuramoto 1975 / Huygens 1665 / Strogatz *Sync*). Shipped **`15008-sympathy`** (Kuramoto phase-coupling: each voice pulls toward the group's mean phase, order-parameter *r* rising 0→1 as the loops entrain; raw-WebGL2 firefly-field; ghost voices so a solo tab hears the full round). The runner-up is fully built and banked:

### `15024-incommon` — Terry Riley's *In C* over his catalog, leaderless (⭐⭐⭐⭐ strong; own inline-SVG cycle OR a sympathy deepening)
- **Brief / what was built:** the SAME leaderless-room concept via the **In C additive-module process** instead of Kuramoto coupling. A fixed ordered score of ~28 one-bar phrase-cells sliced (at real note onsets) from 4 real tracks, each folded toward one shared tonic by a deterministic per-track `detune` (so every tab builds the *identical* score with no handshake). Each present player (tab) has an index in the score and auto-advances at its own pace, but a **decentralized consensus window** clamps it to never exceed `groupMin + K` (K=3) — In C's core "don't stray far from the others" rule, enforced with no leader. The SOUNDING result is the **union** of every player's current cell, retriggered on a shared **wall-clock pulse** (`Date.now`, tempo from analysis), equal-length crossfades at loop seams, all through one `createSafeMaster` (0 synth). Clean modular code (`modules.ts` score-builder / `room.ts` BroadcastChannel co-presence + consensus + ghost top-up / `engine.ts` lookahead union-scheduler / `page.tsx`). Inline-SVG **module-ladder** (score bottom→top, each player a cool-hue token climbing, consensus window a dashed band, sounding cells lit + labelled), cool-only ramp. Ghosts top up to 3 and retire as real tabs arrive. 4 files + README, tsc + eslint clean, all compliance clean (0 oscillators, no banned chrome, gesture-only AudioContext, full teardown).
- **Why banked not shipped:** both cleared the floor identically (3/5: #2 ≥4 subsystems + #3 named refs + #5 RESEARCH §1175); `15008-sympathy` won on (a) a dramatic self-organizing payoff legible SOLO in <40s for the sound-on review the jury flags #5-highest-leverage (In C's additive drift is authentic but, by the builder's own honest flag, ~8–20s/advance = slow to reveal), (b) the sharper statement of the emergent/no-conductor reframe, (c) raw-WebGL2 = the healthiest surface (In C used inline-SVG, legal but 2×-recent), (d) Kuramoto/Huygens a fresher framing than *In C* (240× in-lab). `rm -rf`'d pre-build, never committed.
- **Resurrect how:** ship as its own inline-SVG cycle (deepen: harmonic-consensus auto-transpose like canoncircle so the union stays consonant as players spread; a gentle "the group has finished — begin again" bloom) OR fold the consensus-window + wall-clock-pulse into a `sympathy` v2 that offers both sync modes (emergent-coupled vs additive-process) in one room.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1174 — SHIPPED `14944-phantomline`, DEEP ×2) — dichotic illusion opened as a new lab category; one banked runner-up

Cycle 1174 raced ONE new concept — a **dichotic auditory illusion** (the phantom melody your two ears assemble that is in neither channel; Diana Deutsch's *scale illusion* 1973 + Bregman ASA) — via two divergent technical splits, and shipped **`14944-phantomline`** (note-gated pitch-height dichotic panner; each melody note hard-pans by Deutsch's exact high→right / low→left mapping; inline-SVG three-lane physical-vs-phantom score). It is the lab's first illusion piece — every prior piece maps the *physical* signal; this one is about the divergence between physical and *perceived*. Chained to today's RESEARCH §1174. The runner-up is fully built and banked:

### `14960-earsplit` — the same take torn spectrally between your two ears (⭐⭐⭐ strong; own cycle OR phantomline v2 deepening)
- **Brief / what was built:** the SAME dichotic-illusion concept via **spectral fission** instead of note-gating. TWO sample-aligned `BufferSource`s of one real take → complementary band filters (`lowpass`/`highpass` sharing one `crossoverHz`) → hard-opposite `StereoPanner`s → one `createSafeMaster`. Because both ears carry the SAME time-aligned source, the brain **fuses the spectrum** back into one instrument (spectral fusion wins) — yet the spatial image is uncanny and unstable. The crossover sweeps ~300–2000 Hz on a log-space sine LFO (nudged higher by live spectral centroid → busier passages tear higher), and every ~10 s the two bands **swap ears**, cross-fading through center via `setTargetAtTime` so the swap glides. Canvas2D split-field: the spectrum is drawn torn to whichever side currently holds each band, a moving tear-line at the crossover, a soft RMS-driven center "fusion haze," additive `"lighter"` glow + motion-trail, one cool `hsl(205…)` blue→white hue family (halves distinguished by side + motion, not two colors). Null-analysis-safe. 622-line page + README, tsc + eslint clean, all compliance clean (0 oscillators, no banned chrome).
- **Why it's promising:** it is the *truer* dichotic condition — genuinely-simultaneous different content in each ear at once, where phantomline (one BufferSource) can only *alternate* ears per note. The spectral-fusion-vs-spatial-segregation tension is a distinct, subtler perceptual payoff.
- **Why it didn't win:** the perceptual payoff is quieter and the torn-spectrum canvas is more abstract than phantomline's three-lane "here is the phantom melody the ears don't contain" — for an unattended/quick review, legibility + Deutsch-faithfulness beat it. Not a last-10 tag collision, so it's clean to ship as its own cycle.
- **Resurrect:** ship as its own spectral-fission cycle (Canvas2D output, 0× dichotic-spectral), OR fold its two-simultaneous-source split into a phantomline v2 so the phantom line is carried by genuinely-simultaneous ear content, not per-note alternation. Deepen with a per-voice source-separation (route detected melody vs bass to opposite ears — closer to true Deutsch two-stream) if a stems/HPSS path becomes cheap. Files: `page.tsx`(622) + `README.md`, self-reported tsc/eslint-clean.

### `14944-phantomline` deepenings (extend the winner)
- **Truer two-stream dichotic:** the single BufferSource can only put the *whole* mix in one ear at a time; a light HPSS or melody/bass split (or the earsplit two-source trick) would let high content sit in one ear WHILE low content sits in the other simultaneously — the actual Deutsch two-sequence condition, a materially stronger illusion.
- **Perceived-line honesty:** the centre phantom contour currently threads all notes in pitch order; a real ASA grouping model (proximity + temporal-coherence thresholds) would show *where* the brain would actually split into two streams vs fuse — the illusion has a boundary, and drawing it would teach the phenomenon.
- **A/B the illusion:** a "reveal" toggle that collapses the hard-pan to mono (or plays the true physical L/R) so the listener hears the jagged reality vs the phantom — the before/after is the whole point and would make it self-teaching.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1173 — SHIPPED the banked `14928-livescore`, SHIP-BANKED N=1) — text-only register cashed; chordlattice still ready

Cycle 1173 shipped **`14928-livescore`** (the ⭐⭐⭐ "ship a near-future cycle" resurrect banked in §1172 below) essentially as specced — the self-writing kinetic-typography score, the lab's first piece whose visualization IS pure type. It cashes **jury 2026-08-17 provocation #4** (the deliberately text-only / no-color register nothing else fills) and re-chains cleanly to today's RESEARCH §1173 dive (Visual Lyrics, ACM IUI 2026 — animated text as the music's own surface; case c). No non-winners this fire (N=1 delegated build, not a WIDE/DEEP race), so nothing new to bank — but two fresh deepenings for livescore worth a later polish cycle:

### `livescore` deepenings (extend `14928-livescore`)
- **Onset-tight chord sync:** the published chord onsets can lag where the ear feels the change; snap the hero re-write to a lightweight live onset detector off `master.analyser` (spectral-flux threshold) so the word lands ON the attack, not a beat late. The single highest-value polish — it's the one thing a sound-on review will notice.
- **Real section timestamps:** sections currently map evenly across the buffer duration (reads as an arc, not exact boundaries). If the analysis ever carries per-section times, key the caption to them; until then, consider deriving rough boundaries from chord-density / novelty (Foote-style) so the caption changes where the music actually turns.
- **Typographic voice-leading:** let the hero word's *horizontal position* drift with the chord root around the circle of fifths (left=flat side, right=sharp side) so the eye reads harmonic motion as spatial motion — type as a Tonnetz without the lattice.

## 2026-08-17 (cycle 1172 — SHIPPED `14896-chladnichord`, WIDE) — two banked divergent explorers

Cycle 1172 went WIDE (3 divergent non-mixer verbs, all reading Karel's real catalog, all dodging the last-10 bans). Winner **`14896-chladnichord`** (cymatics — sand migrates to the nodal lines of a square plate whose mode `(m,n)` is driven by the live chord root + spectral brightness; research-chained to this cycle's ChladniSonify dive). The two non-winners below were built to demoable, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed — resurrect either as its own cycle.

### `14912-chordlattice` — his chords walk the neo-Riemannian Tonnetz as a glowing path (⭐⭐⭐ strong; own cycle)
- **Brief / what was built:** Karel's real take loops through `createSafeMaster`; `loadTrackAnalysis().chords[]` is walked against playback position and each chord lights its triad triangle on an authentic inline-SVG **Tonnetz** (pitch-class `pc = 7p+4q mod 12`), tracing a **cyan path** whose edges are classified as neo-Riemannian **P/L/R** moves (nearest-instance cell selection so the path walks the tiling smoothly rather than teleporting). `notes[]` pulse individual pitch-class nodes under the chord path; a chroma-fold-of-FFT fallback keeps the lattice alive when analysis is null/sparse. Palette near-black + **magenta (current triad) ↔ cyan (path/nodes)**; live readout = chord symbol + detected move + key; 946-line page + README, tsc+eslint clean, all compliance clean.
- **Why it was promising:** it reads the ACTUAL composition (progression + note roll) and renders real voice-leading geometry — the P/L/R letters make the harmonic motion legible, which is a genuinely different verb from a mixer.
- **Why it didn't win this cycle:** Tonnetz is heavily represented in the lab all-time (many prior walks — 797/1019/538/896/942…); the WIDE winner had to be the research-chained piece (research-first rule case a → chladnichord), and this is the least *surprising* of the three even though it's the most technically built-out. Not a last-10 tag collision, so it's clean to ship as its own cycle.
- **Resurrect:** ship as a standalone cycle; deepen the chord parser (currently root + maj/min, extensions collapsed to nearest triad — add explicit aug/quartal handling instead of nearest-triad approximation), and consider a hex-tiling variant or seventh-chord tetrahedra so it isn't "another triad Tonnetz."

### `14928-livescore` — a self-writing kinetic-typography score (⭐⭐⭐ — ✅ SHIPPED cycle 1173, 2026-08-18)
- **Brief / what was built:** a deliberately un-pretty, TEXT-ONLY piece (no shader field, no particles, no geometry — the opposite register from the lab norm). Karel's real take loops through `createSafeMaster`; the entire image is **Geist type**: the current chord symbol is a large violet "now" word that swaps via a Web-Animations blur/scale transition on every chord change and **breathes** (font-weight 220–860 + scale) with smoothed RMS from `safeMaster.analyser`; a formal-**section** caption advances over the timeline; melody **note-names** scatter and ghost at the frame edges from `notes[]`; a fixed mono key/tempo/meter/dB/time readout. Analysis-null fallback = live spectral descriptor (BRIGHT/WARM/DARK) + strongest-bin note-name, flagged "spectral fallback." 655-line page + README, tsc+eslint clean, all compliance clean.
- **Why it was promising:** it is the **most distinct register** of the three and the literal answer to jury 2026-08-17 **provocation #4** ("a deliberately text-only / no-color piece"); cheap, robust, trivially solo-reviewable sound-on. A palette-diversity release valve the lab is starved of.
- **Why it didn't win this cycle:** the WIDE winner had to be the research-chained cymatics piece (research-first case a); and of the two remaining, chordlattice reads harmony more richly. But livescore is the higher-priority resurrect — it fills a register nothing else does.
- **Resurrect:** ship next-available non-research-forced cycle to cash provocation #4; deepen by tightening chord-onset sync (the published analysis onsets can lag where the ear feels the change) and giving sections real timestamps if the data ever carries them (currently spaced evenly → reads as an arc, not exact boundaries).

## 2026-08-17 (cycle 1171 — SHIPPED `14864-betweenus`, DEEP) — one banked sibling + the relational-co-presence deepenings

Cycle 1171 went DEEP on jury 2026-08-17 **provocation #2** ("give me a room / a genuinely multi-user piece, not a mixer" — the lab has 0 recent-window multi-user pieces) with the fresh relational verb **distance = harmony between two listeners**. Winner **`14864-betweenus`** (two `BroadcastChannel` presences; near → a just major third + a convolution "between-bloom"; Lissajous ghost fallback for solo review; The Hub lineage). Raced against one same-concept sibling below — built demoable, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed.

### `14880-conflux` — proximity FUSES the two takes into one grain texture (the granular-confluence sibling — resurrect as its own DEEP cycle)
- **Brief / what was built:** the same two-presence `BroadcastChannel` + ghost substrate, but the relational engine is **granular interleave** instead of tuning-lock. Two real takes ("Welcome Home" + "Rebound") loop, one per presence; a look-ahead scheduler (`setInterval` ~25ms, ~100ms lookahead on `ctx.currentTime`) slices short Hann-windowed grains (40–120ms) out of BOTH real buffers and alternates their source; as `d→0` grain density climbs (~2.5→~34 grains/s) and A/B alternation tightens toward strict, so the two recordings **braid into one "between" texture** that only exists when both are near. Canvas2D duotone (cyan `hsl(190…)` + rose `hsl(340…)`) with a braiding particle current at the seam. 100% real catalog (grains are slices of his buffers), one `createSafeMaster`, 0 oscillators; tsc+eslint clean. Helper file `conflux.ts` (seeded RNG, Hann envelope, Lissajous, distance→proximity, id).
- **Why banked not shipped:** lost to `betweenus` on **legibility + robustness for an unattended morning review** — betweenus's "two lights meet → a chord resolves + a bloom appears at the midpoint" is a sharper single-image concept and its audio graph (detune glide + one convolution bus) has a far smaller runtime-glitch surface than a live grain scheduler. The builder self-reported two honest weaknesses: near-fusion reads as a *shimmer wash* (grains at random offsets, not beat/pitch-aligned), and a brief **voice-flip** when the lower-id peer changes on partner swap.
- **How to resurrect:** its own DEEP cycle with the two fixes as the deepening — (1) **transient-align / phase-lock** the grain offsets (onset-detect both loops, snap grains to shared beats) so the fusion lands rhythmically instead of as a wash; (2) make role/hue assignment **survive a partner swap** (stable pairing token, hysteresis) so no voice flip. The granular-confluence engine is a genuinely different musical hypothesis from tuning-lock and deserves its own build budget + Karel's ears. Slug `14880-conflux` free to reuse.

### Relational-co-presence deepenings (extend `14864-betweenus`)
- **Negotiated consonance:** let the pair settle on *which* interval (unison / third / fifth) they lock into based on HOW they approach — e.g. approach angle or approach speed picks the interval — so two people can "choose the chord" together, not just trigger a fixed third.
- **True two-device presence:** BroadcastChannel is same-origin/same-browser only; a tiny WebRTC/WebSocket signaling relay would let two *phones* be the two presences (the still-open cross-device multi-user lane the jury keeps flagging). Deliberately out of scope for the server-less version — flag for a Karel decision (needs infra outside the scope fence).
- **N>2 presences:** a shared field of 3–5 lights where every pairwise distance contributes a partial — the catalog as a *constellation of listeners* whose collective geometry is the chord. (Careful: keep it relational, not a fader bank.)

## 2026-08-17 (cycle 1170 — SHIPPED the ⭐⭐⭐⭐ banked `14848-holdlight`) — two fresh seeds from its research

Cycle 1170 shipped **`14848-holdlight`** (the ⭐⭐⭐⭐ "ship next" idea banked in §1169 below) essentially as specced, plus the two deepenings (permanent per-session loss of a fully-neglected thread; afterimage-on-release). Research-first §1170 = "attention itself as the primary creative tool / listener as composer-performer" (ACM Creativity & Cognition 2026 + Oliveros *Deep Listening*). Two adjacent hooks worth building later, both extending the attention-as-scarce-material verb:

### `holdlight-gaze` — attention that wanders is claimed back (webcam presence as a SECONDARY layer)
- **Brief:** the holdlight mechanic, but a filament you're holding is quietly *reclaimed* if your attention drifts — a webcam face-presence / idle-timeout check (MediaPipe FaceMesh or a cheap motion/blink heuristic) that, after N seconds of no gaze or no micro-movement toward the held thread, begins to release it on its own even while your finger stays down. Makes "holding" require *continuous embodied attention*, not just a resting finger. Webcam is a SECONDARY control layer over catalog audio (rule 10 clean; 100% audible sound stays his takes). Palette/austerity as holdlight.
- **Why:** deepens the thesis (attention is effortful and *interruptible*) and finally uses a sensor as a *withholding* force rather than a mixer controller. Novel verb still intact.
- **Watch:** don't let it become creepy-surveillance; frame it as "the room notices you've drifted." Keep the webcam strictly local, control-only, never recorded/shown.

### `holdlight-duet` — a thread that only sounds while TWO people hold it (the still-0× multi-user lane)
- **Brief:** a WebRTC / shared-room version where a filament sustains only while *both* listeners are pressing it at the same time — presence requires shared, simultaneous attention. Neglect and loss are shared state. Directly attacks jury provocation #2 (a genuinely multi-user piece — still 0× in the lab across 15+ cycles) and #the-social-contract without being a mixer at all.
- **Why:** the multi-user lane the jury keeps flagging, expressed through holdlight's already-proven verb instead of "conduct the mix together." Two-person co-attention as the composition.
- **Watch:** multi-user is hard to demo unattended (needs 2 clients) — pair a single-user "ghost partner" auto-hold fallback so a solo morning-review still shows the idea. This is a DEEP multi-cycle candidate, not a one-fire build.

## 2026-08-17 (cycle 1169 WIDE "the rare achromatic register + not-a-mixer") — two banked explorers (built demoable, curated out, removed unbuilt; resurrect from this seed)

Cycle 1169 raced 3 divergent briefs, ALL committing to the achromatic/grayscale register (jury provocation #4: full-chromatic is now 4× in the last-10 window and hard-audit-banned, warm + cool-violet already banned — grayscale is the genuinely rare palette), each via a different input verb × technique × output. ### `14848-holdlight` — a recording you can only keep present by holding your attention on it (⭐⭐⭐⭐ SHIP NEXT — freshest verb, cheapest to run)
- **Brief/what was built:** 5 of Karel's real tracks as thin vertical **filaments of light** on near-black inline **SVG** (achromatic white→graphite). **Press-and-HOLD** a filament → its looping track fades in + the thread brightens/tautens/quivers to its own per-track analyser; **release → sound + light decay**. There is no play button, no latch — sound needs continuous, effortful attention, you cannot set-and-forget. **Finite attention:** at most 2 held at once. **Memory of neglect:** a per-filament `neglect` value rises while unheld, dims/thins the thread and scales its revive-rate by `(1−0.72·neglect)`, so the piece is materially different after 5 min of tending a few while others go dark; "Let it all rest" wipes it. Each voice → own GainNode presence-envelope → one `createSafeMaster`; lazy-loaded on first hold; 0 oscillators. Ref: Pauline Oliveros *Deep Listening*.
- **Why it's strong:** the single most anti-mixer piece in the fan-out (the *opposite* of a fader bank — presence is scarce and enacted, never set), a genuinely new INPUT verb (sustained-hold, 0× as primary), freshest OUTPUT axis (inline-SVG, austere), and the clearest hit on the jury's "conceptual/critical" menu item. Highest surprise-per-line.
- **How to resurrect:** ship as its own cycle essentially as-is. Deepening: let a fully-neglected filament eventually go permanently dark for the session (true loss); add a faint "afterimage" that lingers a beat after release; consider a breath/idle-timeout that gently claims a held thread back if you stop moving (attention wanders).

### `14816-afterglow` — one take heard from inside a blooming architecture, tunnel-toward-light in pure grayscale (⭐⭐⭐ HOLD until the "architecture you move through" theme cools)
- **Brief/what was built:** ONE real take (dropdown) → dry path + **four synthesized-IR `ConvolverNode` rooms** (`makeRoomIR`: exp-decaying, one-pole-lowpass-colored, peak-normalized noise; RT60 0.15s slap → 1.2s room → 3.5s cathedral → 8s infinite bloom). A single **depth** affordance `t∈[0,1]` (pointer-drag up / wheel / touch) crossfades the four rooms via overlapping Gaussian windows (centres 0/0.34/0.67/1.0, σ=0.26), fades dry→0 (fully wet at the light), opens a depth lowpass 3.5k→18kHz. Raw WebGL2 fragment-shader raymarched cylindrical bore (72 steps, `gl_VertexID` triangle) widening to a vanishing-point glow that floods to white at the threshold. **ACHROMATIC pure-luminance grayscale→white** (`vec3(luminance)` throughout) — the rare register provocation #4 asked for. Sub-0.13Hz brightness drift, no strobe. Ref: near-death tunnel-toward-light phenomenology + Bilbao synthesized reverberation.
- **Why held, not shipped:** it is the safest/most-legible of the three, BUT its "ONE take through a modeled architecture you move through" concept is thematically adjacent to cycle 1168's just-shipped `14784-nave` (walkable cathedral) — shipping back-to-back would be exactly the "too similar" trap Karel/jury keep flagging. Its unique lever is the grayscale palette.
- **How to resurrect:** ship on a cycle when a pure-grayscale palette swing is wanted AND `14784-nave` is ≥3 cycles back so the "architecture" theme has cooled. Deepening: add a return-to-dry "birth canal" reversal, or make depth auto-advance for a hands-free NDE arc.

A single **depth** affordance `t∈[0,1]` (pointer-drag up / wheel) crossfades the four rooms with overlapping Gaussian windows, fades dry→0 (fully wet at the light), and opens a depth lowpass ~3.5k→18kHz. Raw WebGL2 fragment-shader raymarched cylinder bore (72 steps, full-screen triangle via `gl_VertexID`); bore opens + vanishing-point glow floods to white at the threshold. **ACHROMATIC pure-luminance grayscale→white** — the rare register the jury's provocation #4 explicitly asked for. ### `14800-sittingroom` — Alvin Lucier's *I Am Sitting in a Room* (1969) rebuilt as a place you inhabit
- **Brief/what was built:** ONE real take seeds a **modal-resonance void**: 10 low-order room eigenmodes → parallel high-Q bandpass `BiquadFilter`s tuned to `f=(c/2)·√((nx/Lx)²+(ny/Ly)²+(nz/Lz)²)`; the resonated sum is compressed, delayed ~0.24s (one "pass"), gain-scaled and **re-injected** into the resonator input. Over ~150s the dry recedes, wet advances, feedback climbs 0.15→0.57, Q sharpens 8→34 — a **long-form stateful** piece that is audibly different at minute 3 than minute 0, evolving from "the recording" toward the room's own ringing. Pointer x/y → Lx/Ly/Lz retunes every mode live (setTargetAtTime). Raw WebGL2 summed-Chladni nodal field on a boundless deep-violet void boundary, higher modes weighted by progress. Feedback provably bounded (<1 loop gain + in-loop compressor + safeMaster limiter). 0 synth music.
- **Why resurrect:** the most conceptually distinctive of the three and the strongest named-reference pedigree (Lucier). Its own honest rough edge: the safety-conservative loop makes the "room takeover" read as an evolving resonant *wash* rather than a dramatic collapse to a single tone. Resurrection move: give it an explicit, clearly-labeled "let it ring" intensity slider (still capped <1) so a consenting listener can push the takeover further, and/or a second variant that convolves the take through its OWN measured resonance IR à la Lucier's tape passes rather than a continuous loop.
- **Slug free to reuse:** `14800-sittingroom`.

### `14736-phasepiano` — Steve Reich *Piano Phase* (1967) from HIS own take → live moiré interference
- **Brief/what shipped in the race:** one decoded `AudioBuffer` played through TWO `BufferSource`s — copy A at `playbackRate=1` (pan L), copy B at `1+ε` (pan R, ε≈0.002–0.02, keyboard `↑↓`/`[]`); the accumulating offset `∫ε·dt` is tracked on the audio clock and fed to a **raw WebGL2 `#version 300 es` fragment shader** as `uPhase`, driving two detuned grating systems whose difference term paints slow **crimson/bone/black** moiré rings; `R` re-aligns to unison, analyser energy pumps contrast. 100% real audio through one `createSafeMaster`, 0 synth.
- **Why promising:** first Reich-style dual-rate phasing of his recording in the lab; named ref (Reich); a genuinely graphic, un-pretty palette (off the warm + cool-violet ruts) and WebGL2 (off Canvas2D). Clean, correct, demoable (typecheck clean).

Reich phasing on his rubato is a strong idea; the phasing is real and works.

- **Brief/what shipped in the race:** a from-scratch **Paulstretch** engine (8192-pt windowed STFT, `pow(1−x²,1.25)` window, analysis pointer creeping ×8–×50 slower than synthesis @ 50% overlap, **per-bin random phase** Hermitian-mirrored, independent L/R phase for free stereo width, offline-pre-rendered into a seamless self-crossfaded loop so the audio thread never starves). A **long-form scheduler** crossfades a new stretched track in every ~40–90s over ~12s; retiring tracks drop to ~0.05 residue and linger under a ~24s tail → minute-5 ≠ minute-1, real memory. **three.js** boreal green→cyan→teal aurora curtain undulates with the smoothed energy envelope. Autonomous-primary + keyboard nudges. 971 lines, lint+typecheck clean.

Refs: Pauline Oliveros *Deep Listening* + YIN. **ambition #2 (≥3 subsystems: 16-track loader + YIN pitch/RMS mic-analysis + nearest-key choir-swell engine + WebGL2 constellation) + #3 (Oliveros + YIN) + #5-adjacent.** **Why it lost:** the gentlest of the three concepts and mic-call-response is conceptually near prior call/response pieces (`13696-callback`); strongest of the three on *playability* (jury #1) but the window is already 12/15 perform, so a perform piece was the least-diverse slot this WIDE. **Resurrect by:** ship as the mic-secondary review piece (Karel's own instrument-adjacent input, 0× mic in the window); or fuse with a real-key-aware voice-leading bias so the answering swell resolves into the sung key. Files: `page.tsx`+`audio.ts`+`pitch.ts`+`gl.ts`+`README.md`, tsc/eslint-clean.

## §1162 banked runners-up (DEEP — ONE concept "conduct & self-layer your WHOLE catalog with bare-body/hand gestures in the air — 'Ghost Hands', record a motion pass so a translucent clone holds that mix while you layer another" raced via 3 technical approaches; winner = `14480-bodychoir`, whole-BODY MediaPipe-Pose conductor. Both losers fully built demoable + compliance-clean (rule-10 real catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer`→one `createSafeMaster`, 0 synth, three.js, on-brand chrome, full teardown, autonomous no-camera fallback), banked here, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed. Both dodge the same bans the winner does: camera/hand input off the pointer/keyboard rut, three.js off the Canvas2D 5× rut, full 16-hue chromatic spectrum off both the warm≈4× audit-ban and the jury cool-violet ban.)

- **`14464-ghostmix` (⭐⭐⭐⭐, MediaPipe HANDS → dome of 16 orbs)** — the richest gesture VOCABULARY of the three and the most literal cash of the research: two webcam hands are the mixing desk in the air over a two-row three.js dome of 16 full-spectrum orbs. Reach → nearest orb focuses (Gaussian reach-lobe: louder/brighter, lowpass opens); hand height → cutoff; finger splay (index-tip↔pinky-tip / hand-size) → gain swell; two-hand distance → stereo width + a feedback-delay "space" send; pinch (thumb↔index) → grab the focused orb and pull it to centre. Ghost hands: records ~8s of hand-control frames → a translucent wireframe ghost hand (cyan L1 / magenta L2) replays it forever and SUMS into the mix; 2 clearable layers. **Why it lost:** the dome-of-orbs render sits closest to last cycle's `14400-orbitcompass` ring-of-orbs — strong but the least visually *new* of the three. **Resurrect by:** it's the cleanest desktop-friendly demo (hands at a desk, no stepping back) — ship as the hands-scale sibling to bodychoir, or fold its feedback-delay "space" send + pinch-grab-to-centre into bodychoir cycle-2. 1289 lines, build-clean.
- **`14496-handnebula` (⭐⭐⭐⭐, MediaPipe HANDS → gravity-well particle NEBULA)** — the most novel interaction MODEL + most organic render (Anadol-lineage): 16 tracks are 16 `THREE.Points` clusters (~4,800 pts) drifting in 3D, each a full-spectrum hue. Two hands are gravity wells — a hand near a cluster pulls it forward (spring-home + inverse-distance attraction + damping) → louder/brighter, particles condense; draw two attended clusters within a blend radius → pans converge for an emergent DUET; spread/release → clusters spring back to an ambient lowpassed wash. PINCH (or Freeze btn) snapshots each audible cluster's gain/pan/filter into independent looping "ghost" voices + a frozen shimmer copy; 2 ghosts, oldest recycled, Clear btn. **Why it lost:** its ghost = a *state snapshot*, not a replayed *motion* loop, so it's the weakest cash of the "record-your-movement-as-a-clone" research hook (bodychoir/ghostmix both replay actual recorded gesture motion). **Resurrect by:** the "pull two clouds together to blend two recordings into a duet" verb is the freshest single idea in the race — build a piece around *blending* (a mixer whose only gesture is proximity-merge), or graft the nebula render onto bodychoir. 1154 lines, build-clean.

## §1161 banked runners-up (WIDE — 3 divergent briefs, each dodging all four fresh-jury bans [pointer-primary input / WebGL2-full-field output / single-recording-DSP-toy template / cool-violet-on-near-black] AND my fresh diversity finding [the last 5 winners are ALL Canvas2D — a new output monoculture] on DIFFERENT axes; winner = `14400-orbitcompass`, a three.js dawn-ring you turn your body inside of to live-mix the whole catalog by compass heading. Both losers built demoable + compliance-clean (rule-10 real catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer`→one `createSafeMaster`, 0 synth, on-brand chrome, full teardown), banked here, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed.)

- **`14416-stillpoint`** (⭐⭐⭐⭐, resurrect as the next cosmic-ambient turn — a genuinely NEW posture for the lab: the anti-screen piece) — **a near-screenless, eyes-closed meditation: your whole catalog dissolved into a slow orbiting spatial field, paced to your breath.** Fully built + ESLint-clean. Press Begin and it plays itself: `REAL_TRACKS`/`loadRealTrackBuffer` → looping BufferSources, each `source → HRTF PannerNode → lowpass → gain → field gain → createSafeMaster` (never `ctx.destination`); **3 voices audible at once**, orbiting on slow signed angular velocities (~2–6 min/rev), retiring the oldest + spawning a fresh un-duplicated track every ~26 s over a ~16 s cross-fade so the mix always moves (long-form, minute-5 ≠ minute-1). A **per-frame-accumulated breath clock** (default 11 s ≈ 5.5 breaths/min; ↑/↓ retune continuously so brightness never jumps) gently swells field gain + opens each voice's lowpass. The ONE visual is a single warm-ember DOM glow on near-black (`#050506`), breathing scale+opacity **only** (max ~0.16 Hz — SAFE, nothing audio-reactive on luminance); chrome fades after 5 s, returns on activity. Optional SECONDARY mic layer (`useMicAnalyser`, heavily-smoothed amplitude → soft swell; denial/absence degrades silently). Refs: 2026 brainwave-entrainment lit (audiovisual > audio-only, but 5.5-breaths/min parasympathetic calm) + arXiv:2605.30940 streaming-spatial-audio (RESEARCH §1161). **Lost to orbitcompass** on diversity-vs-immediate-window ONLY: `14336-tidalstrata` (cycle 1160) JUST shipped an autonomous cosmic-ambient whole-catalog "press one button and let it drift" piece — shipping stillpoint one cycle later gives two consecutive autonomous-ambient-drift pieces; orbitcompass's compass-heading INSTRUMENT verb was the sharper break. **To resurrect:** it's the strongest banked seed and complete — ship it the next cosmic-ambient turn (it's the lab's only true audio-primary/anti-screen piece, and it rewards exactly the sound-on/headphones review the jury flags #5-highest-leverage); or deepen with a subtle harmonic-common-tone bias on which track spawns next so the slow crossfades voice-lead rather than collide.

- **`14432-typescore`** (⭐⭐⭐, resurrect when the concatenative window has cooled) — **type a word or a line of poetry and hear it in Karel's own piano: each letter selects a real note-slice from his catalog, so language becomes a playable score, rendered as inline-SVG kinetic typography.** Fully built + ESLint-clean; engine in a separate `typescore.ts`. Decodes one real track, builds a **pitch-tagged note-slice index** from `loadTrackAnalysis` (each note = a buffer region `[time,time+duration]` tagged by MIDI + velocity; even golden-ratio region fallback when analysis is sparse/absent → always makes sound); each typed char maps **deterministically** to a target pitch (alphabet position → pitch class spread around the circle of fifths; **vowels aim high / consonants low**; spaces + punctuation = rests), nearest real slice played back `playbackRate`-nudged ≤±2 st (clamp [0.5,2]) with a click-free A/R env through `createSafeMaster`, 12-voice cap. Output = animated SVG `<text>` glyphs placed by triggered pitch, sized by velocity, sepia (vowels)/graphite (consonants) on warm cream paper, swept by a violet `<line>` playhead that re-triggers the line. Refs: The Concatenator arXiv:2411.04366 + CataRT + concrete-poetry/Cardew *Treatise* (RESEARCH §1161). **Lost to orbitcompass** on: (a) its TECHNIQUE (concatenative note-slices) is the immediate window's MOST-repeated — `14256-mididuet` (1159) + `14240-inkscore` (1158) + `14192-notemirror` (1156) all shipped concatenative-corpus engines in the last four cycles; (b) it decodes a SINGLE track, leaning toward the jury-#3-banned single-recording template, where orbitcompass spatialises all 16. Its INPUT (typed language, genuinely ~0× as a primary verb) and OUTPUT (SVG kinetic typography) are the fresh parts. **To resurrect:** ship once concatenative has cooled a few cycles; or make it whole-catalog (draw slices from ALL 16, letting the *word* pick the source recording) so it sheds the single-recording lean and cashes jury #3 too.

## §1160 banked runner-up (DEEP — ONE concept: "the lab's first true LONG-FORM flagship — your whole 16-track catalog as a ~10-min self-evolving piece with REAL memory, minute-5 audibly ≠ minute-1, yet coherent" — raced via 2 different-in-kind long-form engines: granular concatenative vs multi-stratum spectral crossfade; winner = `14336-tidalstrata` (the spectral-layer engine — whole recordings held as strata, reshaped by migrating filter-prisms, with a residue-that-resurfaces memory + an accreting geological cross-section that makes the 10-min FORM visible). Loser built demoable + compliance-clean (rule-10 real catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer`→one `createSafeMaster`, 0 synth), banked here, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed.)

- **`14320-driftmass`** (⭐⭐⭐⭐, resurrect as the granular long-form sibling — now a **fully-built, reviewed** implementation, not a mere brief) — **a ~10-minute drift-choir that reassembles your catalog into a NEW evolving texture with real long-range memory.** `drift.ts` is a genuine two-timescale engine: a SLOW process (one move every ~40–90s) walks a `harmonicCenter` around the circle of fifths on a *weighted* table (prefer stay / ±5 / ±7 over random jumps), migrates a 2–4-track `sourcePool` across all 16 recordings, and runs a density/register ARC (sparse→swell→dense→recede) across the 10-min form; a FAST look-ahead grain scheduler (60ms tick, 0.28s horizon) plays REAL catalog slices taken at note onsets (via `loadTrackAnalysis`, with a fixed-interval raw-buffer fallback), each pitch-nudged ≤±5 semitones toward the center and Hann-windowed for overlap-add legato; **novelty suppression** (recently-used grain keys avoided ~4s) keeps the texture moving. **The jewel is the MEMORY reservoir** (`captureMotif`/`maybeRecall`): it captures short grain-SEQUENCES as motifs, and the slow process re-injects an *older* motif (>60s old) transposed to the current center — recall grows likelier as the arc matures (`0.15 + 0.4·smoothstep(0.25,0.85,progress)`), so **minute-1 material literally returns at minute-8**, and each recall fires a visible tick on the timeline ribbon. Canvas2D warm high-key daybreak aurora (`render.ts`): slow hue-bands read the harmonic tide (`pitchClassHue`), fast motes read the grain cloud + analyser. Keyboard steering (↑↓ density, ←→/[ ] tide brighter/darker, Space pause via `ctx.suspend()`), full teardown. **Lost to tidalstrata** on best-PIECE grounds only: (a) perceptual risk — a granular cloud of pitch-nudged slices reads less obviously as "his piano" than tidalstrata's *whole looped recordings*; (b) diversity-vs-window — concatenative catalog grains were just shipped one cycle prior in `14256-mididuet`, and warm-gold aurora is adjacent to skyflock/mididuet. **To resurrect:** ship standalone as the granular long-form sibling (it's complete + build-clean), OR — the higher-value move — **fold its transposed-motif recall reservoir into `14336-tidalstrata`** so a resurfacing stratum can also carry a faint *melodic* echo of an earlier moment, not just a filtered wash: that grafts driftmass's deeper *musical* memory onto tidalstrata's more-legible *textural* memory. The motif-reservoir-with-arc-weighted-recall is the reusable jewel here.

## §1159 banked runners-up (WIDE — 3 divergent briefs dodging the fresh jury bans [pointer-primary input / WebGL2-full-field output / single-recording-DSP-toy template / cool-violet-on-near-black] on DIFFERENT axes; winner = `14256-mididuet`, the first Web MIDI piece in the lab: play a live duet with yourself, your keyboard voiced by concatenative grains of your OWN catalog over a chosen bed take. Both losers built demoable + compliance-clean (rule-10 real catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer`→`createSafeMaster`, 0 synth), banked here, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed.)

- **`14288-tiltpour`** (⭐⭐⭐⭐, resurrect near-verbatim — the strongest banked seed of the window; the ONLY full-chromatic piece and a clean "whole catalog as ONE corpus" build) — **your whole catalog is a bowl of colored sand; TILT to pour it.** All ~16 verified tracks load lazily (`pour.ts`: first batch of 5, rest stream in) as one bowl of grain-pools, each recording a pool of 80–250ms real-audio grains at a distinct hue around the FULL color wheel, placed on a ring. `DeviceOrientation` beta/gamma is a gravity vector: pools "downhill" pour (rate up to ~14 grains/s + gain weighted by tilt·ring alignment × magnitude), 24-voice cap so it never muds; near-flat = even shimmer from the whole corpus, steep = a few recordings dominate. Canvas2D additive particle field rains colored sand downhill — a rainbow, a deliberate break from the violet monoculture. Arrow-key virtual-tilt fallback (NOT pointer-primary), iOS `requestPermission()` behind an "Enable tilt" button, full teardown. **Lost to mididuet** on: (a) tilt is NOT a lab-first (83-kids-tilt-rain used it) → ambition ~2/5 vs mididuet's 4/5; (b) mididuet cashes the explicit jury #2 demand ("touch a MIDI keyboard — 0×, literally Karel's instrument") which is the single highest-value 0× lane. **To resurrect:** ship as-is (it's a rare full-chromatic + tilt + whole-corpus piece, all three under-represented) OR give the pour a *record/loop* layer — capture a tilt path and let the bowl replay it, so it authors a mix-over-time rather than only live-blending (differentiates it from the §1157 `14256-tidecorpus` tide seed).

- **`14320-driftmass`** (⭐⭐⭐⭐, resurrect as the lab's long-form flagship — thin category, genuine state/memory) — **a 10+ minute cosmic-ambient drift that reharmonizes your own piano into a slow choir, provably different at minute 5 than minute 1; you steer only the harmonic weather.** `engine.ts`: three coupled state variables evolve continuously — a **harmonic center** (root PC + mode) migrates every 20–40s around the circle of fifths (biased by a histogram of the tracks' real `chords[]`, occasional major/minor flips); a **register target** (slow ~70s sine + per-migration random walk); a **density** (3-min swell, then a 47s breath). **Memory-biased grain selection** draws 82% from grains whose pitch already sits in the current chord tones, picks the one nearest the register target, and micro-tunes ≤±3 semitones onto the nearest chord tone → his solo improvisations become a consonant drift-choir. 15-voice cap, raised-cosine envelopes, a hand-built Schroeder reverb (4 lowpassed combs + 2 diffusers + one long echo, no external IR), all through safeMaster. Deterministic-but-non-periodic in elapsed time (a `migrations` counter proves it moved) so it never repeats a bar (no bars). Keyboard steering: `1–8` pull the center toward 8 harmonic regions (pull persists ~2 migrations), `Space/D` deepen, `R` rest. Warm high-key aurora (golds/ambers/rose-gold on dark warm ground; luminance drifts on multi-second sines, nothing strobes). **Lost to mididuet** on: (a) ambition ~2–3/5 (long-form + whole-catalog, but no lab-first technique + no dated-research chain this cycle) vs mididuet's 4/5 + jury #2 alignment; (b) it's a watch-lean piece (steer the weather, don't play notes) where mididuet is a true instrument in Karel's hands. **To resurrect:** the lab genuinely lacks a long-form flagship — ship it next WIDE/DEEP that isn't the MIDI lane; deepen with common-tone voice-leading between migrations, granular overlap-add legato, an envelope→reverb-size feedback, and a multi-minute "form/movements" layer (build → still coda) so the 10-min arc has shape.

## §1158 banked runner-up (DEEP — ONE concept: "compose a NEW coherent piece by weaving phrases from the WHOLE 16-track catalog onto a paper-white ink graphic score, harmony-aware" — raced via 2 interaction models: horizontal melodic time-score vs vertical polyphonic weave; winner = `14240-inkscore` (the resurrected ⭐⭐⭐⭐ §1157 seed, now with the queued key-profile selection + diatonic snap + consonance slider). Loser built demoable + compliance-clean (rule-10 real catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer`→`createSafeMaster`, 0 synth), banked here, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed.)

- **`14256-inkloom`** (⭐⭐⭐, resurrect as inkscore's POLYPHONIC "loom mode" sibling) — the VERTICAL / chordal model. Time is the warp (left→right); each key adds a phrase as a horizontal **thread** (weft) that loops, so several of his phrases sound at once and the engine's job is **vertical consonance**: `weave.ts` holds the union chroma of all sounding threads, an interval-consonance weight table, and `bestTransposition` picks each new thread's `playbackRate` shift (±≤4 semitones, mapped from a consonance slider) to maximize overlap with that union → a raw stacked collage (0) resolves into a voice-led chord (1); "Weave a chord" builds a consonant 3-thread weave in one click, "Re-weave" re-optimizes all. Woven ink threads on cream paper (position = time×register, weight = velocity), faint warp lines, one terracotta accent. All audio = real-catalog phrase loops through one safeMaster (0 synth), lazy-decoded/cached. Refs: Xenakis UPIC density-fields + textile scores (2025 ZKM book) · Cardew *Treatise* · The Concatenator arXiv:2411.04366. **Lost to inkscore** on: (a) inkscore is the honored resurrection of the queued ⭐⭐⭐⭐ seed with exactly the spec Karel's STATE queued (key-profile selection + diatonic snap + consonance); (b) a horizontal left→right time-score reads more canonically as Cardew/UPIC than a textile weave; (c) tighter AV coupling — inkscore's swept playhead RE-TRIGGERS the ink marks (the score IS the instrument), where inkloom self-disclosed its playhead is a **visual scrubber decoupled from the audio scheduler** (looping voices run free) and polyphonic stacking of his rubato piano risks mud. **To resurrect:** fold `weave.ts` into inkscore as a "loom mode" toggle — keep the swept-playhead audio scheduler (so the weave is beat/loop-locked to the score, not free-running) but adopt inkloom's **vertical union-chroma voice-leading** for simultaneous marks, so stacking in-key becomes possible. The vertical-consonance engine is the reusable jewel here.

## §1157 banked runners-up (WIDE — 3 divergent "whole catalog as ONE corpus" briefs, each dodging all three fresh-jury bans (pointer-primary input / WebGL2-full-field output / cool-violet-on-near-black) on a DIFFERENT axis; winner = `14224-skyflock`, the real-solar generative orchestration painted as a full-daylight-spectrum sky. First 8 load immediately, rest lazy-load as the tide reaches them; full three.js teardown. ## §1156 banked runner-up (DEEP "ONE concept — improvise a live MIDI duet OVER your own recording, voiced in your own timbre, on a full-chromatic Scriabin color organ — raced via 2 timbre engines"; winner = `14192-notemirror`, the analysis-driven note-slice sampler that replays his ACTUAL recorded notes. ## §1155 banked runners-up (WIDE — 3 divergent "let Karel PLAY his catalog" briefs on fresh input×output×technique×palette axes; winner = `14128-choirtabs`, the multi-tab BroadcastChannel leader-clocked canon of his real piano. Both losers banked here, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed.)

- **`14144-latticeharp`** (⭐⭐⭐, resurrect near-verbatim) — reach INTO his recording and PLUCK its overtones on a **Tonnetz** (neo-Riemannian harmonic lattice: fifth = +7, major-third = +4 diagonals). His looping take feeds a pool of ~30 high-Q band-pass **resonators**; touch a lattice node and that pitch rings OUT of his playing (constant-peak band-pass ≈ unity at centre, so loudness tracks his actual energy at that pitch — filtering, never synthesizing). Sweep a triangle → the triad blooms from his overtones. WebGL2 glowing lattice + tappable SVG fallback, multi-touch pointer-capture. Refs: Euler 1739 / Riemann / Cohn; band-pass as spectral excitation. **Lost to choirtabs** only on lower ambition (2/5 vs choirtabs' first-in-lab multi-user) + no research-chain this cycle + the resonator-reach technique sits near banked `13936-stringbath`. **To resurrect:** tune the third→5/4 and fifth→3/2 (just intonation) so it beats less against his overtones; verify the ringing reads on a real device; it's a gorgeous standalone touch instrument. Beat-quantized to a 16th grid anchored to `AudioContext.currentTime` (Chris Wilson two-clocks lookahead), a 2-bar step-memory record/replay loop layers a re-performance over a gapless equal-power bed. 3-tier degrade: note-roll → onset-novelty slices → even slices. WebGL2 pad wall + playhead, Canvas2D fallback. Refs: Linn/Akai MPC + Maschine/Simpler slice-to-pad; Chris Wilson "A Tale of Two Clocks." **Lost to choirtabs** on redundancy-vs-window — the MPC/pad/beat-quantize territory is warm from `13968-flipdeck` (1152) + banked `13952-flipgrid`. **To resurrect:** ship once the rhythm axis cools; it's the strongest "re-perform your own melody" instrument and a clean use of `loadTrackAnalysis` to PLAY (not draw) — exactly what jury #1 wants.

## §1154 banked runners-up (DEEP "ONE concept — a HIERARCHICAL, beat-locked, section-aware playable map of his own song — raced via 3 geometries"; winner = `14096-songwheel`, the radial song-form wheel. All 3 shared the SAME rigorous pipeline that fixes `14048-rhymeloom`'s flagged rough edge: hand-rolled radix-2-FFT spectral-flux onset novelty → autocorrelation tempo (median-onset fallback + "loose" badge) → phase-aligned 4/4 beat/downbeat grid → Goertzel beat-synchronous chroma → bar cosine self-similarity → **Foote checkerboard-kernel novelty segmentation** (Foote ICME 2000) + mean-chroma cluster lettering A/B/C → gapless ~100ms-lookahead bar-quantized leap scheduler (equal-power crossfades, never `source.loop`, all through `createSafeMaster`, 0 synth). Both losers built compliance-clean, banked here, `rm -rf`'d PRE-build, never committed.)

- **`14112-branchwalk`** (⭐⭐⭐, resurrect near-verbatim) — the STRONGEST "compose your own re-telling" realization and the cleanest cash of jury #1's *compose* verb: sections are NODES in an SVG arc-diagram graph, strongest cross-section rhymes are EDGES, and a walker travels the graph — at each boundary the beat-aligned branches light up and you pick one (or auto-wander/coherence-slider picks) to splice an endless in-time re-telling, with a **breadcrumb of the path so far**. Lost to songwheel only on (a) per-track graph-legibility variance (builder-flagged — dense-harmony tracks can hairball) and (b) branches land on a section's *first* downbeat, not the single best-matching interior bar. **To resurrect:** target the best-matching interior downbeat per branch + a stable deterministic layout (timeline-baseline arc diagram) tuned so node count stays legible on a phone; then it becomes the definitive "DJ the FORM of your song" piece. SVG, zero GPU risk.
- **`14080-formfold`** (⭐⭐⭐, resurrect if Karel wants the dense recurrence texture) — the RIGOROUS full-matrix realization: keeps rhymeloom's WebGL2 R8 self-similarity heat-map but adds the beat/downbeat grid + Foote section boundaries drawn as bands ON the matrix, plus a clickable Canvas2D **form ribbon** strip; most controls of the three (matrix-cell leap + ribbon-section loop + solo + J + wander + coherence + BPM±/×2÷2/meter). Lost to songwheel on being rhymeloom's *visual twin* (same WebGL2 SSM heat-map → risks the jury's "extend became repeat"), where songwheel's radial form-wheel is a genuinely new way to see his song's shape. **To resurrect:** ship it as the "analyst's companion" to songwheel — the dense pairwise recurrence texture (all echoes at once) that the wheel deliberately simplifies to only the strongest chords.

# Resonance Dream IDEAS — living queue

Each idea has a status (`queued` / `in-progress` / `demoable` / `polished` / `dead`),
a slug (becomes the route `/dream/<n>-<slug>`), and a spec terse enough for
the Dream Agent to build from. Add new ideas to the bottom; promote
items by editing status in place.

## §1153 banked runners-up (WIDE "three divergent rule-10 briefs on fresh input×output×technique×vibe axes"; winner = `14048-rhymeloom`, the chroma-self-similarity rhyme-jump instrument)

Both built demoable + fully compliant (rule-10 catalog audio via `loadRealTrackBuffer`→`createSafeMaster`, 0 synth, semantic-token chrome, notes modal, no api route). `rm -rf`'d after banking, before the authoritative build. Resurrect near-verbatim.

- **`14032-skyscore`** (⭐⭐⭐ — the most beautiful of the three; *your music, played by the sky right now*). Real environmental data conducts his real catalog: **NOAA solar-position math** (computed locally in UTC — no network, no timezone data; geolocation with a 6s-timeout SF fallback) gives sun altitude/azimuth + dawn/day/dusk/night phase + season + a smooth 0–1 "sky energy"; **open-meteo** current weather (wind/cloud/temp, CORS-open, no key, try/catch → solar-only fallback) adds live conditions. Mapping: altitude → gain + lowpass; **azimuth → StereoPanner so the music orbits with the sun** (east→L, west→R); cloud → convolution-reverb wet + high-shelf darkening; wind → per-layer detune vibrato; time-of-day → how densely 1–3 real tracks layer (equal-power crossfades, `mulberry32`-seeded LFO scheduler) — a genuine long-form generative bed that's texturally different every hour and evolves over minutes without looping. Full-viewport WebGL fragment-shader sky (altitude-sampled gradient, sun/moon disc placed by real alt/az, audio-reactive aurora bands, cloud-dimmed stars) + Canvas2D fallback; live mono readout of the sky data. Refs: Brian Eno generative ambient / Jem Finer *Longplayer*. **Why it lost:** it's press-Play-and-*watch* — the sky conducts, you don't DO anything — exactly the passive-playback posture jury #1 flagged, and the cosmic-ambient vibe + full-screen frag-shader brush two lingering jury bans. **Resurrect** when the lab next wants a genuinely long-form/ambient piece or an external-data cycle — the solar-math + data-arrangement scheduler is reusable shared-infra (first real-world-data conductor in the recent window); giving the listener even one steering verb (a "time-of-day scrubber", or letting them pick which sky-input maps to what) would lift it off the watch-only floor. ambition 3/5 (#2 ≥3 subsystems: solar/weather ingestion + generative arrangement + spatial/filter chain + WebGL sky · #3 Eno/Longplayer · long-form-generative).
- **`14064-tiltpour`** (⭐⭐⭐ — the most embodied; *pour your own music around the room*). Device tilt (`DeviceOrientationEvent` β/γ, iOS `requestPermission()` on the primary tap; **pointer-drag gravity fallback** so laptops are first-class; idle drift keeps it alive) becomes a gravity vector over a **96×96 Stam "Stable Fluids" semi-Lagrangian sim** (self-advection → Gauss–Seidel pressure projection → dye advection, per-frame hard clamps + NaN guards so it never blows up). His live level+bass (safeMaster analyser) inject dye+velocity at a source hung on the raised side, so the glowing fluid pours downhill; the dye's **brightness centroid** drives a StereoPanner (x→pan — his piano moves to where the liquid gathers) and a lowpass (vertical pooling→cutoff — pour it down to darken/thicken). Full-viewport WebGL2 R8 dye field (violet ramp) + Canvas2D fallback. Signal chain strictly buffer→lowpass→panner→safeMaster (no synth). Ref: Jos Stam *Stable Fluids* (SIGGRAPH 1999). **Why it lost:** highest build/GPU render-risk of the three (builder couldn't verify locally), the "interaction" is spatialization of a full looped track rather than musical re-composition (shallower than rhymeloom's playable navigation), and it extends the already-*loved* `84-wave-fluid` direction rather than opening new ground. **Resurrect** as a phone/installation piece once tilt+GPU are device-verifiable — it's the strongest live-performance/Tauri candidate of the batch (care #3/#5), and a per-device neutral-tilt calibration + true multi-dye (one hue per frequency band) would deepen it. ambition 2–3/5 (#2 ≥3 subsystems: fluid sim + tilt input + centroid spatialization + WebGL2 · #3 Stam 1999).

Touch/pluck a string and that pitch **rings out of his playing** — filtering, never synthesizing; hold several to build a chord of resonances. three.js string-rack visual shows standing-wave vibration (amplitude + ice→violet color from each resonator's live post-gain energy), full teardown, graceful WebGL notice. Seeded auto-arpeggio self-demo. **Why it lost:** conceptually the least novel of the three — "pluck resonances out of his piano" sits near `13488-striketemple`'s strike-a-duet and the lab's long piano/duet lineage; visible oscillation rate is an aesthetic approximation (documented). **Resurrect** as a filter-harp instrument; the parallel-resonator-bank-over-his-catalog engine is clean reusable infra. ambition #2 (decode + resonator bank + string-physics visual + renderer) + #3 (Karplus-Strong / modal resonance).

## §1147 banked runners-up (WIDE "three ways to PLAY his own recordings, not watch them"; winner = `13696-callback`, the call-and-response grain duet)

- **`tapewarp` — perform his recording like tape (⭐⭐⭐, strongest banked; resurrect near-verbatim).** `status: queued`. A solo turntablist instrument: lay one of his real recordings out as a 3D **tape ribbon** (three.js) and **grab it with your hand** — a real-time **granular time-stretch** engine (Hann-windowed overlap-add grains, ~90ms/50%, each `AudioBufferSource` at `playbackRate=1` so time & pitch decouple) means holding the head still **freezes a chord into a shimmer**, sweeping slowly **time-stretches** at pitch, dragging back **reverses** (from a pre-built reversed buffer), and trapping the head **stutters**. Drag = scrub (horizontal) + rate (vertical); buttons Play/Freeze/Reverse/Stutter; Rate + Grain sliders; 16-track selector; violet read-head cursor + analyser-driven glow on ink ground. Builder shipped it fully working this fire (page.tsx + grainEngine.ts + ribbonScene.ts + README) with clean tsc/eslint. **Why banked not shipped:** its time-manipulation verb is close to the §1144 DEEP winner `conductorwell` (same granular-OLA-hand-conducts-his-recording lineage) — a diversity call, not a quality one; it is the strongest banked piece and the purest "his real sound, always" instrument. **Resurrect as a DEEP:** add a 2nd read-head for two-hand multitouch chords (grab two moments, cross-fade by hand), move the grain cloud to an **AudioWorklet** for sample-accurate timing + per-grain pitch/pan spray, and a record-a-gesture-pass overdub loop. Ambition: ≥3-subsystems + never-quite-this-technique (scrub-turntablist vs conduct).
- **`canontabs` — multi-tab canon of his real piano (⭐⭐; lower resurrection value — see overlap).** `status: queued`. Opening his recording in several tabs turns each into a **voice in a canon**: a `BroadcastChannel("resonance-13712-canontabs")` **leader-elected shared clock** (lowest-id = conductor, self-healing) owns the master transport + shared track id + tempo + canon delay; followers slave off `performance.now()` interpolation; voice N reads the buffer `N·canonDelay` behind voice 0 so his piece chases itself in a round. Conduct: drag pad = tempo×0.75–1.25 / canon-delay 0.5–6s; "Cue next voice" builds the round; **single-tab fallback** layers 2–4 delayed copies locally (√count trim, ≤4 cap) so a lone reviewer still hears it. Builder shipped it working (page.tsx + canon.ts + engine.ts + README, clean checks). **Why banked (and lower priority):** JURY #4 asked to resurrect `11888-chorusrooms` on his catalog, but the §1145 WIDE winner **`canoncircle` already shipped a BroadcastChannel shared-clock canon of his real recorded phrases two cycles ago** — canontabs largely re-treads it (its fresh delta is per-tab=per-voice presence + conductor build-up). **Resurrect only if extending past canoncircle:** onset-quantised entrances landing on his phrase downbeats; broadcast a compact spectral fingerprint so remote nodes pulse with timbre not just amplitude; optional WebRTC data-channel for cross-device presence over the same message schema.

## §1146 banked runners-up (DEEP "Dream between your OWN recordings, and STEER it" — rule-10 resurrect of the 5/5 `12976-dreammedley`; 3 real-audio morph engines raced, 2 not shipped; winner = `13664-dreambetween`, the reservoir-navigated granular one)

The §1146 DEEP concept: the 5/5 dreammedley wandered a latent space between his songs beautifully but made sound by re-synthesizing pitch contours through an **FM voice** (a rule-10 gap) and painted it warm-ember Canvas2D (now doubly jury-banned). This cycle rebuilt it three ways so **every sound is his real piano** and Karel **steers** it (jury #1 interaction + jury #3 dreammedley-on-real-audio). Chained from RESEARCH §1146 (Music-JEPA arXiv:2607.22000, Jul 24 2026 — sound as an *action-conditioned* world model). Both banked were built demoable + full compliance sweep (`"use client"` L1 · 0 `Math.random`/`Date.now`/`new Date` · 0 oscillators/FM/synth · every node → `safe.input`, 0 `ctx.destination` · `loadRealTrackBuffer` real catalog · 0 banned classNames/hues · Link back to `/dream` · no api route) before removal — resurrect either near-verbatim.

- **⭐⭐⭐ `13632-betweensongs` (resurrect-strong — the concatenative/spatial slot)** — *the point cloud IS the corpus.* Real-time **corpus-based concatenative synthesis / audio mosaicing** (CataRT/Schwarz lineage): on load, each of 4 real tracks (Welcome Home/Isolation/Snowflake/Ghost) is sliced into ~120 ms overlapping grains with RMS + log-spectral-centroid (hand-rolled radix-2 FFT) + ZCR features; a **three.js point-cloud scatter** lays every grain at `anchor+feature-offset` so each recording is a cluster with internal bright/dark structure. Drag the roving cursor → proximity weights pick *which* recording (weighted-random, so the between-space alternates corpora), in-cluster position sets a target feature vector → k-NN picks *which* grain; equal-power sine-window overlap-add, `playbackRate=1` (his tone, never pitch-shifted). Files: `page.tsx` (745L, custom additive ShaderMaterial), `mosaic.ts` (FFT + `buildCorpus` + `MosaicEngine` lookahead scheduler), `readme-text.ts`, README. Refs: CataRT/Schwarz, The Concatenator (arXiv:2411.04366), Music-JEPA. **Why banked not shipped:** concatenative/mosaic is already well-trodden in-lab (48+ INDEX hits) so it's not first-in-lab, and dreambetween is the *faithful* dreammedley resurrect (keeps the actual reservoir) the jury named. **Resurrect:** ship near-verbatim when a spatial/three.js slot rotates in; strongest standalone of the two.

- **⭐⭐⭐ `13648-spectralmorph` (resurrect — the spectral-DSP slot; most technically demanding)** — *two of your recordings become one.* Hand-rolled real-time **STFT / phase-vocoder cross-synthesis morph**: N=2048, hop=512 (75% Hann OLA, analysis-hop=synthesis-hop so no time-stretch), per-frame magnitude interpolation along a steered `t∈[0,1]` — **perceptual/geometric** `|A|^(1-t)·|B|^t` by default (the accessible core of SoundMorpher's SPDP, a genuine hybrid midpoint, not a crossfade) or linear; coherent phase reused from the dominant source per bin so `t=0`→A exactly and `t=1`→B. Output = **WebGL2 "spectral curtain"** (indigo-A / magenta-B / violet-morph line curves over a scrolling waterfall). Drag the curtain to steer; hands-off auto-sweeps 0→1→0; swap source B; toggle perceptual vs linear. Files: `page.tsx` (768L), `morph.ts` (radix-2 FFT + streaming `MorphEngine` + `Visualizer` + `mulberry32`), `readme-text.ts`, README. Refs: SoundMorpher (arXiv:2410.02144), phase-vocoder cross-synthesis (Dolson/Moorer), Music-JEPA. **Why banked not shipped:** builder flagged a real reviewer risk — ~250 ms lookahead latency + main-thread STFT jank could stutter on his hardware (untested headless); and it's the least dreammedley-faithful of the three. **Resurrect:** move the STFT to an `AudioWorklet` to kill the jank/latency risk, then it's a strong DEEP headliner on its own; or fold as a "spectral-morph view" on a two-track piece.
