## §1144 banked runners-up (DEEP "Conduct your own recording" race — 2 not shipped; winner = `13536-conductorwell`)

The §1144 DEEP concept: *Karel's real catalog track is his orchestra; his gesture drives tempo (rubato) + dynamics live via pitch-preserving granular/OLA time-stretch — he re-performs the expression of his own piece.* Three interaction models were raced; the winner is the **beat-conducting** one. Shared engine (both seeds already implement it): synchronous granular OLA — Hann grains at fixed ~45ms output-hop, `playbackRate=1` (pitch intact), `readPos += conductRate * GRAIN_HOP`, freeze-jitter shimmer, all through `createSafeMaster`. Both seeds were built demoable and verified clean before removal — resurrect either near-verbatim.

- **`13520-conductorline` (⭐⭐⭐ RESURRECT-STRONG) — the scrub baton + WebGL2 note-river.** Pointer position scrubs the read-head; horizontal pointer *speed* = conducted tempo (fast=rush, hold=freeze-to-eternity, backward-drag=reverse, pitch intact); pointer Y = dynamics+brightness. Visual = raw WebGL2 additive `GL_POINTS`, one sprite per note, the WHOLE river placed each frame from a single `uReadPos` uniform (so it flows AND reverses on the GPU for free — very cheap, very robust). Why banked not shipped: it's the most *intuitive/robust* of the three but the beat-conducting well is the bolder realization of the actual verb "conduct." **Resurrect path:** ship standalone (it's the safest-to-demo of the family), OR fold into conductorwell as a "line view" toggle — the single-uniform GPU river is the cleanest way to render the flowing note-field and its reverse-scrub is a distinct, legible interaction. Files were complete: `page.tsx` + `granular.ts` + `renderer.ts` + `readme-text.ts` + `README.md`.
- **`13552-conductorbreath` (⭐⭐) — the one-breath axis + SVG spacing-is-tempo.** Vertical breath: up = swell(louder+brighter)+rush, down = hush+slow-toward-hold. Its standout idea: an inline-SVG note-roll whose **pixels-per-second is tied to the conducted rate**, so notes visibly SPREAD APART when he rushes and BUNCH when he holds — *you see the rubato in the spacing*, the most legible rendering of tempo in the family. No GPU (best robustness). Why banked lower: least spectacle, single-axis (no reverse). **Resurrect path:** fold "spacing = visible tempo" onto any conductor renderer; or ship as the accessible/legible variant. Files: `page.tsx` + `granular.ts` + `readme-text.ts` + `README.md`.

## §1142 banked runners-up (DEEP "Sympathetic Resonance" race — 2 not shipped; winner = `13440-resonancefield`)

The DEEP concept: Karel's real recording drives an 88-string coupled-resonator model of the whole piano so you SEE the sympathetic afterglow (the product's namesake). Three renderers raced; the WebGPU-compute field won. The two below built clean (tsc+eslint green, full rule-10 compliance verified) but were removed pre-commit — resurrect from these notes.

- ⭐⭐⭐ **RESURRECT-STRONG · `13424-sympathetics`** (raw WebGL2 instanced 88-string BANK). The most *legible* rendering of the three: a horizontal bank of 88 vertical glowing filaments, low A0 left → high C8 right, struck strings flaring on his real onsets, sympathetically-coupled strings shimmering on a slow multi-second tail. Correct two-regime damped-energy model (struck τ≈1.2s while key down, sympathetic τ≈3.6s tail), top-6 normalized couplings from `Score(i,j)=max_{p,q∈1..6} exp(-|f_i·p−f_j·q|/bw)`, Canvas2D fallback + octave labels. **Why it's promising:** you can literally read the keyboard — a reviewer instantly sees *which* strings his playing wakes. **Resurrect / DEEP-continue:** ship it standalone next cycle, OR fold it into the winner as an alternate "keyboard view" toggle (bank layout vs the scrolling bloom field) so one piece offers both the literal instrument and the abstract resonance field. Deepen with pedal-inferred all-dampers-lifted blooms + per-string inharmonicity `f_n=n·f·√(1+B·n²)`.
- ⭐⭐ **`13456-resonancehall`** (three.js 3D HALL — sympathy made spatial). The 88 strings stand as rods of light in a receding fogged corridor (low A0 near, high C8 far); on his onset a rod flares amber and a fraction of its energy visibly *travels along a glowing edge into the distance* to an octave/twelfth partner, which lights violet and rings on — the coupling matrix becomes a visible web of light moving through depth. Slow auto-drift camera. **Why it's promising:** "energy travels along an edge into the distance" is a genuinely lovely, different-in-kind way to feel sympathy as motion-through-space (jury #4 spatial/embodied lane). **Banked lower** only because it repeats just-shipped three.js (13360-handreader) + network-edges (13328-vocabularygraph). **Resurrect:** an Ikeda-hall / installation slot once three.js has cooled off a cycle or two; fuse the "energy-travels-in-depth" edge-flare into whichever sympathetic-resonance piece ships next.

## §1141 banked runners-up (WIDE fire, 2026-08-15) — built demoable, swept clean, folders `rm -rf`'d after banking, never committed

The WIDE fire raced three "read Karel's real playing as something new" directions with three different renderers; **`13360-handreader` shipped** (his two-hand keyboard choreography reconstructed in three.js from his real note-roll — the research-chained §1141 SKY-Piano/Profy realization). The other two were built to demoable and banked here verbatim:

### ⭐⭐⭐ RESURRECT-STRONG — `13392-voiceweave` (re-number when resurrected) — SEE Karel's counterpoint as braided voice-strands
**Brief.** What if you could see his counterpoint — the independent voices of his polyphonic piano — untangled into separate braided strands weaving through time? Read his real note-roll, separate it into a handful of continuous VOICES by pitch-proximity, draw each as a smooth flowing strand (pitch height over time), strands braiding and crossing where his voices do, with a live playhead synced to the real audio.
- **INPUT** = catalog playback (default *Interplay*, 16-track selector) + `loadTrackAnalysis.notes[]`. **OUTPUT** = inline **SVG DOM** (one animated `<path>` per voice + `<line>` playhead + glowing sounding-node per voice). **TECHNIQUE** = causal pitch-proximity **voice-separation / streaming** (`voices.ts`: walk `notes[]` in time, ≤5 active voice streams, assign each note to the nearest-pitch *free* voice, spawn a new strand on a >14-semitone leap, never overlap within a voice; sort high→low by median pitch onto the violet ramp; Catmull-Rom→bezier smoothing; `voiceStateAt` for playhead contour + sounding test; nodes pulse to `master.analyser` RMS). **PALETTE** = 4–5 muted violet-ramp strands on near-black, ink-like, non-cosmic. **REF (#3)** = David Huron *Voice Leading: The Science Behind a Musical Art* (MIT Press 2016) + MIR voice-separation streaming.
- **Why promising.** "Concept and math are one" (the recallorbit standard): the streaming separation IS the reading — it draws his *counterpoint structure*, not a texture; deepest-tier cash of rule 10 (reads his real polyphony). Built clean (compliance-swept: `"use client"` L1, 0 real `Math.random`/`Date.now`/`.destination`, 0 synth/mic, house chrome, seeded muted demo alive frame 1, full teardown). **Why banked not shipped:** SVG-DOM output is exactly what `13248-rubatoline` shipped ONE cycle earlier (§1139) — same renderer two cycles running is the diversity ding the winner dodged (three.js embodiment is more different-in-kind); and handreader is the fresher, research-chained (§1141) surprise.
- **How to resurrect / deepen.** Re-run a full `next build` on rebuild. Deepenings: (1) offline DP voice-assignment (Temperley/Chew contig) to resolve dense-chord swaps the causal heuristic misses; (2) draw sustains as flat holds so a strand reads as real note-durations, not just onset contour; (3) **fuse with handreader** — color each of handreader's fingers by the voice it's playing, so the hands and the strands are one piece. Honest limits (from its README): causal separation swaps voices in dense chords; >5 simultaneous notes fold onto the nearest voice; analysis accuracy bounds everything.

### ⭐⭐ RESURRECT — `13376-scoreterminal` (re-number when resurrected) — his music read out as an Ikeda data-terminal
**Brief.** What if his recording streamed past as a live data-terminal — the chord sounding now, the key/tempo/meter, the bass↔treble register spread, and the dynamic level all reading themselves out in animated monospace type, the way Ryoji Ikeda renders data as light?
- **INPUT** = catalog playback (default *Interplay*, selector) + `loadTrackAnalysis` (chords/notes/key/tempo/meter). **OUTPUT** = **DOM / CSS ONLY** — no canvas/WebGL/SVG; a single `requestAnimationFrame` loop mutating `textContent` + CSS width/height/opacity through refs (React state reserved for phase/track so the hot path never re-renders). **TECHNIQUE** = five synced read-outs walked against the playback clock: NOW-chord + scrolling mono chord column (binary-search `chords[]`), 8-band bass→treble register histogram (CSS bars, fast-attack/slow-release), header key/tempo/meter, RMS dynamic meter + rolling energy tape off `master.analyser`, frame counter + blinking cursor. **PALETTE** = strict semantic tokens, no raw hex anywhere (the piece *is* type), one violet accent reserved for "now". **REF (#3)** = Ryoji Ikeda *data.matics* / *test pattern*.
- **Why promising.** The most different-in-kind *rendering* of the fire — pure animated typography, no drawing surface at all; dense, clinical, genuinely Ikeda. Built clean. **Why banked not shipped:** the Ikeda-clinical *vibe* overlaps the lab's existing `11240-datamatics`, and a data read-out is more legible-mapping than the winner's embodied insight; a strong ⭐⭐ but not the surprise handreader is.
- **How to resurrect / deepen.** Add a live spectral/waterfall tape column; a scrub-back through the whole take's harmony as a timeline; couple it to voiceweave (show the current voice count from the separator). Its determinism/degrade ladder (seeded synthetic numbers when a take lacks analysis; audio always real-or-silent) is a clean pattern to keep.

Cycle 1140 ran **DEEP** (ledger 1136 D · 1137 W · 1138 DEEP · 1139 WIDE → **1140 DEEP** by alternation). Shipped **`13328-vocabularygraph`** (the math IS the reading — live PageRank centrality over his real chord-transitions draws the *structure* of his harmony, not a texture; cleanest/freshest research→build chain via vega-mir arXiv:2605.16539; most different-in-kind output). Loser folders `rm -rf`'d **after** this bank, **before** the authoritative build — never committed, nothing entered git. Resurrect notes:

- **⭐⭐⭐ RESURRECT-STRONG `13296-catalogsediment`** — *the technically biggest of the three.* A real **WebGPU compute** Gray-Scott reaction-diffusion field (256² ping-pong storage buffers, 8 substeps/frame, render pipeline for the mineral palette; CPU-Canvas2D 148² fallback) allocated ONCE and **never reset** across the whole catalog, so the sediment accrues for the full run. Perturbation reads his real composition: chord root → seed position on a circle-of-fifths ring, major/minor → feed/kill + violet accent, key signature → slow global feed/kill drift (the catalog reads as one migrating chemistry), analyser bass/treble/level → seeding strength. Mineral/non-cosmic (graphite, wet clay, oxide). Seeded muted walk alive on frame 1. Refs Gray & Scott 1983 / Karl Sims / Anadol "data as pigment". **Why banked not shipped:** the chord→(feed/kill/position) mapping is a legible convention the viewer *sees*, not an insight about his music (builder's own honest note) — beautiful, but the shipped graph reads his STRUCTURE where this reads a texture; and an RD *field* is closest to the jury-fatigued aesthetic. **Resurrect:** re-run a full `next build` on rebuild (its agent validated via tsc+eslint only — verify the WebGPU init + `device.destroy()` teardown compile clean under Next). **Deepen:** drive feed/kill from the *live PageRank distribution* of the vocabularygraph engine so the chemistry thickens around his harmonic centers (this is exactly the fusion folded into the winner's README — could ship AS the winner's cycle-2 ground layer).

- **⭐⭐ `13312-corebloom`** — *the most legible document/record.* A scrollable/zoomable stratigraphic **core sample**: each chord deposits a horizontal strata band into an ever-growing, never-cleared vertical column (raw-WebGL2 instanced band mesh + Canvas2D annotation overlay + Canvas2D fallback), with a compressed whole-history overview strip + viewport indicator, thin labelled graphite/violet seams between tracks, and `↑ now` snap-back. Reads his harmony straight: hue = `pitchClassHue(chordRoot)` (circle-of-fifths, so modulations read as color drift up the core), minor → darker/desaturated via `chordIsMinor`, thickness ∝ chord duration, brightness ∝ live analyser RMS; no-analysis tracks degrade to spectral-centroid time-slices. Mineral/ink/graphite, non-cosmic. Refs ice/sediment cores / Tufte data-density / Anadol. **Why banked not shipped:** it shows the *sequence* of his harmony as a readable record, not its *structure* — one rung shallower than the centrality graph on the "concept and math are one" axis. **Deepen:** make the depth axis a true wall-clock ruler (currently harmonic-time via duration); downsample the oldest strata into the overview instead of capping the 30k-band buffer; expose per-chord loudness rather than global RMS. Strong candidate for a stratigraphy/record review slot, or as the winner's **history-scrubber** view (folded into the winner's README).

## §1134 banked runners-up (DEEP — ONE concept "a reservoir mind holds every melody you sing to it, and lets you dream in the latent space BETWEEN your own songs" → 3 realizations, 1 shipped `12976-dreammedley`)
Cycle 1134 ran **DEEP** (ledger 1131 W · 1132 D · 1133 W → **1134 DEEP** by strict alternation, AND **JURY 2026-08-14 #6 EXPLICITLY ordered it**: "Extend recallorbit, don't clone it… a cycle-3 that lets you sing your own melody in and watch the trained readout reproduce it exactly, then dream it apart, honors the real multi-cycle commitment"). Cycle-3 of the reservoir lineage `10984-echofold` → `11376-recallorbit` (the loved 4/5 standout — one sung phrase, ridge-trained exact recall + a DREAM knob) → this. The deepening beyond recallorbit: hold **MULTIPLE** sung melodies as distinct ridge-trained readouts on **one shared reservoir** (all readouts share the same clock-driven state sequence, so `XᵀX+λI` is Cholesky-factored ONCE and each phrase is a cheap back-substitution — which is *why* the readouts live in a common space and blend cleanly into genuine in-between melodies), architecturally anchored on the **Echo State Transformer** (arXiv:2507.02917 — multiple reservoirs/readouts as finite memory slots with attention over them). Shipped **`12976-dreammedley`** — the only lane that honestly cashes the jury's most-untouched, ceiling-raising criterion **#4 (long-form stateful, minute-5 ≠ minute-1)**: a 5-min+ self-navigating dream through your sung memories, numerically verified (non-repeating supercritical trajectory MAE≈4.5, accumulating warmth, echo-state re-focus to exact recall on return), with a segmented journey-timeline that reads the long-form arc even muted. All three built demoable + swept clean (`"use client"` L1; 0 real `Math.random`/`Date.now`/`new Date` in code — only determinism-asserting comments; `createSafeMaster`-routed; Canvas2D not three.js/frag-shader; house typography; `PrototypeNav`; full teardown incl. `ctx.close()`); loser folders removed after banking, never committed.

- **⭐⭐⭐ RESURRECT-FIRST — `12960-memoryfold` (2-D memory pad; the most novel INTERACTION).** A MusicVAE-style 2-D palette: each sung phrase is a labeled pin on a Canvas pad; a draggable cursor barycentrically blends the trained `Wout` matrices of the nearest pins so dragging morphs one of your songs into another in real time; a separate DREAM axis unwinds any point past the edge of chaos. The shared-Cholesky-basis trick makes the multi-readout blend cheap enough to recompute every frame. Ambition #2 (mic + centroid-contour + ESN multi-readout + interpolation engine + FM synth + orbit viz) + #3 (Echo State Transformer + MusicVAE 2-D palette, Roberts et al. 2018 + Jaeger 2001 + Lukoševičius & Jaeger 2009) = honest 2–3/5. **Resurrect on an explorable-instrument slot** (it is real-time playable, NOT long-form — that's why it lost to the #4 lane, not on quality). **Deepen:** place pins by an actual 2-D projection of each phrase's contour features (not a ring), add a 3rd DREAM-depth axis, and seed the pad from Karel's REAL Path piano so the "songs" are his recordings, not sung phrases. Builder self-verified interpolation yields real between-contours (linear readout ⇒ blending matrices blends melodies note-for-note), scale-snapped to Dorian so hybrids stay musical.
- **⭐⭐⭐ `12992-mirrorduet` (attention-canon; the most ON-ANCHOR — literal Echo State Transformer made audible).** You sing live; the mind computes a **softmax over Pearson correlation** of your live contour vs each stored memory → attention weights → answers in **canon** with the attention-blended readout, warped toward your (canon-delayed) line; DREAM drifts the answer from faithful echo (answer↔live corr ≈ +0.77 at d=0) to free reverie (≈ −0.23 at d=1). Two-strand woven ribbon (real per-segment over/under z-order) with repertoire cards that brighten by attention weight. Ambition #2 (mic-live + centroid-contour + ESN multi-readout + softmax-attention blend + FM canon synth + braid viz) + #3 (Echo State Transformer + Jaeger 2001 + Lukoševičius & Jaeger 2009) = honest 2–3/5. **Resurrect on a sound-on / live-mic slot** (its canon payoff is heard, not muted-visible; loop-shaped so no #4, and it rhymes mildly with the recent jazz trade-fours memory cluster choruskeeper/echoduet — give it a non-jazz review window). **Deepen:** true polyphonic canon (2+ answer voices), per-past-self HRTF pan so the repertoire is spatialized around you, and a visible attention-weight sparkline per card.

## §1130 banked runners-up (DEEP — ONE concept "sing, and your own voice is drawn as living light" → 3 technical renderings of the oscillographic drawn-sound tradition, 1 shipped `11776-lissaknot`)
Cycle 1130 ran **DEEP** (ledger 1127 W · 1128 D · 1129 W → **1130 DEEP** by strict alternation, which §1129 MORNING/STATE pre-announced). It finally cashed the **perpetual RESURRECT-FIRST `drawnsound`** (banked §1127 + §1129, flagged "two cycles deferred, the strongest single fresh direction on the shelf") — exactly the §1126 precedent where a DEEP finally shipped the 3×-banked `lumenorgan` directive by racing three renderings and elevating with a named reference. ONE concept — *the oscillographic "drawn sound" tradition (Norman McLaren *Synchromy* / Ben Laposky *Oscillons* / Jerobeam Fenderson *Oscilloscope Music* / macumbista Vector Synthesis) made into an instrument you play with your VOICE* — raced across 3 genuinely-different technical renderings. Shipped **`11776-lissaknot`** (the pure X-Y oscilloscope with pitch-LOCKED Lissajous knots — the biggest surprise + most legible "huh" read, the most literal descendant of the named tradition, low device-risk standard additive lines, and it elevates the banked drawnsound beyond its faithful base exactly as rosewindow elevated lumenorgan). All three built demoable + swept clean (`"use client"` L1, 0 real `Math.random`/`Date.now`/`new Date` in code — only determinism-asserting comments, 0 direct `ctx.destination`, `createSafeMaster`+`createVoidReverb` routed, house typography, `PrototypeNav`, full teardown incl. `ctx.close()`); loser folders removed after banking, never committed.

- **`11744-drawnsound`** ⭐⭐⭐ **RESURRECT-FIRST** — *sing, and your voice is drawn as a living tangle of ~1,400 ivory light-filaments.* Live **mic** (shared `useMicAnalyser`, input-only) → six perceptual bands become the amplitudes of six rotating phasors defining a time-varying 2D vector field; **~1,400 filaments are CPU-traced streamlines** re-integrated through it each frame, emitted as additive WebGL2 `gl.LINES` (deep-indigo fade-trail pass + `SRC_ALPHA,ONE` additive ivory pass), so louder/brighter voice raises the phasors' spatial frequency and the tangle knots tighter; silence lets it drift (never frozen). Seeded `DemoVoice` (deterministic six-band pure function of the age clock, `mulberry32` `SEED=0x11744`) draws a breathing tangle within ~1s at load with NO mic/audio (muted-phone self-demo); mic-denied → `text-destructive` note + self-demo continues. A voice-shadow drone through `createSafeMaster`+`createVoidReverb`. Ambition honest **3/5** (#2 mic-bands + phasor field + CPU streamline tracer + additive line renderer + voice-shadow drone = ≥4 subsystems; #3 McLaren/Laposky/Fenderson named; #5 RESEARCH §1130). **Why banked, not shipped:** it is the *faithful* literal banked drawnsound — the lowest-risk, most-portable base, single mic stream — but the DEEP was told to be "genuinely massive / concentrate ambition," and lissaknot's pitch-LOCKED knot is the bigger surprise + more legible instrument (a held note snapping to a clean knot reads instantly on a muted phone) while staying just as low-risk. It lost the way `11552-lumenorgan` (the literal grid) lost to `11568-rosewindow` (the elevated mandala). **Resurrect** as the streamline-tangle "view" of the drawn-sound instrument, or ship verbatim on the next mic slot — it's the single most portable/robust of the three. **Deepen:** GPU transform-feedback to push the filaments into the tens of thousands (see `11760` below); pitch-locked knots grafted from lissaknot; per-filament colour-temperature from spectral centroid within the ivory family. `input: mic · output: WebGL2 additive gl.LINES vector-graphics · technique: harmonic-driven CPU streamline vector field · palette: ivory-on-indigo, INTENSE`.

- **`11760-filamentchoir`** ⭐⭐⭐ — *the massive version — sing, and tens of thousands of light-filaments advect through your voice's field, drawn entirely on the GPU.* Live **mic** → the SAME six-phasor harmonic vector field, but **~24,000 filaments are advected on the GPU each frame via WebGL2 TRANSFORM FEEDBACK** (ping-pong VBOs, update program under `RASTERIZER_DISCARD` capturing `{pos,prev,age,seed}` varyings — mirrors `11712-attractorveil/gl.ts` exactly — then an **instanced additive LINE pass** `drawArraysInstanced(LINES,0,2,24000)` where a 2-vertex endpoint buffer picks prev→pos so each filament draws as a glowing ivory light-wire over a deep-indigo fade quad). Six voice bands weight the phasors; brightness raises spatial frequency; a faint idle curl keeps silence drifting. Seeded `DemoVoice` (`mulberry32` `SEED=0x11760`) drives the full tangle within ~1s at load with no mic/audio. Choral oscillator-bank voice-shadow through `createSafeMaster`+`createVoidReverb`; loudness swells the pad, brightness opens a formant lowpass. **The builder ran a full `npm run build` — the route compiled cleanly (10.4 kB static prerender).** Ambition honest **3/5** (#2 mic-bands + GPU transform-feedback advector + instanced additive line renderer + choral voice-shadow = ≥4 subsystems; #3 McLaren/Laposky/Fenderson named; #5 RESEARCH §1130). **Why banked, not shipped:** it is the biggest "massively bigger" leap on paper — but its headline **GPU transform-feedback + instanced-line path is unverifiable headless** (the exact "banked-because-I-can't-verify-it" trap MORNING has flagged 5×; cf. `11664-deepdrift`, `11616-glowdrift`, `11536-swarmlattice` — corridor beat deepdrift, cloudveil beat glowdrift, on exactly this axis), AND transform-feedback *just* shipped yesterday in `11712-attractorveil` so the substrate isn't even fresh. **Resurrect** on the standing **GPU-device / sound-on review slot** where the 24k-filament GPU path can finally be seen — it's a strong candidate to break the unverifiable-GPU curse because it type/lint/BUILD-passed and mirrors a proven-in-lab feedback pipeline. **Deepen:** graft lissaknot's pitch-lock so held notes crystallize the massive field into a stable knot; per-filament lifespan + respawn for a shimmering choir; transform-feedback into hundreds of thousands. `input: mic · output: WebGL2 GPU transform-feedback additive line vector-graphics · technique: GPU-advected harmonic vector field at massive scale · palette: ivory-on-indigo, INTENSE`.

## §1128 banked runners-up (DEEP — ONE concept "deepen the proven cloudveil first into the tunnel of light the music pulls you through" → 3 technical approaches, 1 shipped `11680-corridor`)

Shipped **`11680-corridor`** (the biggest concept-leap: cloudveil's *open* drift became *navigable tunnel geometry* — the literal near-death tunnel-of-light, on-charter AGENT.md §260, mechanic legible on a muted 06:30 phone, single-pass = low device risk). Both runners-up built demoable + swept clean (`"use client"` line 1, 0 `Math.random`/`Date.now`/`new Date` in code, 0 direct `ctx.destination`, `createSafeMaster`+`createVoidReverb` routed, house typography, `PrototypeNav`, full teardown incl. `ctx.close()`); folders removed after banking, never committed.

- **`11664-deepdrift`** ⭐⭐⭐ **RESURRECT-FIRST** — *the cloud you drift through resolves into impossibly deep, crisp, glowing interiors as the light integrates across frames while the music surges you forward.* audio-file(+seeded self-demo) → **WebGL2 THREE-pass temporal-reprojection accumulation**: pass 1 a deliberately-cheap 32-step jittered volumetric march (Beer-Lambert view+sun + Henyey-Greenstein) → `fboRaw`; pass 2 ping-pong **RGBA16F** history `current = mix(history, thisFrame, alpha≈0.10)` with a per-frame interleaved-gradient-noise (blue-noise) jitter, so many cheap jittered frames integrate to a band-free effective step count one frame can't afford; pass 3 warm-gold filmic composite. Beat-locked surge via gated spectral-flux onsets adds a forward-velocity impulse; on hard surge the blend **alpha snaps→1** for a couple frames (velocity→alpha rejection-on-fast-motion heuristic, honest about not doing full reprojection). Centroid tints the sun dawn-gold↔candle-amber. **Graceful float-target fallback**: probes `EXT_color_buffer_float`/`half_float` + re-checks framebuffer completeness → downgrades to RGBA8 (still two-pass accumulating) if unavailable, chrome notes it. Ambition honest **3/5** (#2 ≥4 subsystems: onset-flux detector + 3-pass GPU pipeline + FBO-pair state + chroma-tint = 4; #3 named ref TAA temporal-reprojection accumulation ported to volumetric raymarch — **`temporal.reproj` grep-0 in-lab**, a genuinely fresh rendering *architecture* for us; #5 RESEARCH §1128). **Why it lost, not why it's weak:** its headline benefit (accumulation crispness) is *invisible on a muted phone review* and unverifiable headless — the exact "banked-because-I-can't-verify-it" trap MORNING has flagged 4× — and it spent its budget on sampling not light-transport (kept single-scatter; its own next-cycle list asks for MS). **Resurrect** on a designated **GPU-device / sound-on review slot** (the standing ask in MORNING) where the float-FBO accumulation can finally be seen — it is the strongest candidate to break the WebGPU/float-target-unverifiability curse because its RGBA8 fallback is already a first-class working path. **Deepen:** true per-pixel-velocity reprojection + variance-clipping (neighborhood clamp) to kill ghosting under hard surges; then layer multi-octave MS on top.

- **`11648-lightfold`** ⭐⭐⭐ — *a cloud that glows not just from within but from DEEP inside, and surges you forward on every hit.* audio-file(+seeded self-demo) → **single-pass WebGL2 volumetric raymarch**, the most *faithful* deepening of cloudveil: implements **Sébastien Hillaire's multi-octave multiple-scattering approximation** literally (N=3 octaves; octave i scales extinction ×`aⁱ`, scatter gain ×`bⁱ`, HG eccentricity ×`cⁱ`, a=b=c=0.5) — octave 0 = the crisp self-shadowed forward rim, higher octaves see less sun-march extinction (`exp(-sunDepth·absorption·aⁱ)`) so light leaks from deep inside as a broad near-shadowless deep glow; Beer-Lambert kept on view ray + sun-march; beat-locked surge scrolls the fbm domain (`uScroll`); centroid sun-tint. Ambition honest **3/5** (#2 ≥3 subsystems; #3 Hillaire multi-octave MS — the exact technique cloudveil's README named as its #1 deepening; #5 RESEARCH §1128). **Why it lost, not why it's weak:** it is the correct, honest deepening but the *smallest experiential leap* — same open-drift-toward-a-sun as cloudveil, just better-lit — and this DEEP was told to be "genuinely massive," which corridor's navigable-tunnel geometry delivers while ALSO carrying the same multi-octave MS. **Resurrect** if a future cycle wants the pure open-drift MS lighting without the tunnel structure, or as the reference implementation to graft MS onto deepdrift's accumulation. **Deepen:** decouple the per-octave transmittance fully (currently one shared sun-march optical depth re-attenuated per octave — the closest real-time-faithful reading, but a fully independent per-octave transmittance is the next step); bar-level (not just per-onset) surge so long build-ups accelerate the drift over many seconds.

## §1122 banked runners-up (DEEP — ONE concept, three three.js renderings of the SAME trained-readout Echo-State reservoir, 1 shipped `11376-recallorbit`)
Cycle 1122 ran **DEEP** off the 2026-08-13 jury's provocation #3 ("concentrate the ambition — take one FIRST multi-cycle to 5/5; deepen a *proven* first — **echofold wants trained readouts for exact transposition**"). ONE concept — *sing a phrase to a living mind and it learns to sing it back EXACTLY, then dissolves to dream* — raced across three three.js renderings of the SAME engine: echofold's real Echo-State reservoir (`10984-echofold`) EXTENDED with a genuinely **trained ridge/Tikhonov readout** (`Wout=(XᵀX+λI)⁻¹XᵀY`, hand-rolled double-precision **Cholesky**, no ML libs), clock-conditioned (`u=[sin2πφ,cos2πφ]`) so free-run is stable/input-driven, with a **DREAM knob** `WoutEff=(1−d)·Wtrained+d·Wrandom` (+ state-noise ∝0.02d + ρ nudge +0.06d) morphing exact recall → echofold's fixed-random dream, plus a mic front-end (onset+centroid→retrain) and a seeded self-demo (trains + sings back + auto-sweeps 0→1→0 on mount so a muted phone sees the whole arc in ~1s). Shipped `11376-recallorbit` (the phase-portrait orbit — the biggest surprise + verified-exact-recall + most on the PRIMARY visionary charter). Both runners-up built demoable + self-verified clean (folder `tsc --noEmit` 0 + `eslint` 0); folders removed after banking, never committed. Honest audit: a **trained** reservoir readout is grep-0 (echofold's are fixed-random by design), so #1 is the trained-readout extension the jury named — NOT a faked new engine. Off every jury ban: input mic · output three.js-3D GPU (off Canvas2D/SVG) · technique trained-readout ESN (off physics-sim/jazz/Ikeda) · palette cyan/teal phosphor (off violet-on-black + warm-molten).

- **`11392-twinsong`** ⭐⭐⭐ **RESURRECT-FIRST** — *your sung phrase and the mind's memory of it as two strands of light that overlay in perfect recall, then peel apart and fray as memory dissolves.* three.js twin strands: an ice-white **target** ring (phase→angle, pitch→radius/height) and a live teal **sung-back** strand built from a rolling generated-pitch buffer; at DREAM=0 they lie exactly on top of each other (a single thread of perfect recall), and as DREAM→1 the echo strand peels, wobbles, frays, and thin **connector-threads** stretch between corresponding phase positions and fade as they lengthen — echofold's signature "stretch-and-fray" memory-threads reborn in 3-D. Playhead beads ride both strands at the current φ. **Why it's the top resurrect:** it is the single most *legible* concept↔mechanic read of the three — the clearest silent-06:30 "memory diverging from your phrase" teaching read — and the strongest continuity with the proven first (echofold's threads). It lost only because recallorbit's unwinding-attractor is the bigger *surprise* + more on the PRIMARY visionary charter + numerically verified exact recall. Ship verbatim next DEEP, OR fuse into recallorbit as a second "view" toggle (orbit ⟷ twin-strand). Honest **#1 (trained readout, grep-0) + #2 (5 subsystems) + #3 (Jaeger 2001 / Lukoševičius–Jaeger 2009) + #4 (cycle 2 of echofold) + #5 (§1122 research)**. Verified clean (tsc 0 / eslint 0). **Deepen:** a `divergence` HUD; per-note thread color = pitch error; a "lock" button that re-trains on the drifted contour so the dream becomes the new memory. `input: mic · output: three.js-3D · technique: trained-readout ESN · palette: ice-white↔teal phosphor`.

- **`11360-echoloom`** ⭐⭐⭐ — *the reservoir AS a living 3-D neural constellation — you watch the mind that sings you back.* N=180 units on a Fibonacci sphere (custom additive glow shader, size/brightness = live `|xᵢ|`), ~900 sampled recurrent edges as breathing LineSegments, bright **pulses that travel along outgoing edges when a unit spikes** and decay so you see signal ripple through the recurrent web, a halo ring marking each sung-back note (pitch→angle), slow 0.02 Hz auto-orbit; DREAM low reads tight/ordered, DREAM→1 jitters the geometry and fires more chaotically. The "it IS a mind" read — the natural cycle-2 render `10952-reverie` (§1114) always wanted, now trained + mic-driven. **Why banked:** the exact→dream morph is subtler in a node cloud (reads as tight-vs-agitated) than recallorbit's unwinding orbit or twinsong's fraying strands, and the builder's honest caveat is that after each reset the first loop can be marginally soft before the fading-memory property locks to exact recall (recallorbit/twinsong avoid this by leaving the reservoir on the trained loop). Resurrect on a "teach the technique / see-the-network" slot. **Deepen:** restore the exact trained end-state on reset (kill the soft-first-loop); color nodes by their readout weight (which neurons *are* the melody); a pointer-strike that kicks the nearest node and spreads a visible/audible wavefront (reverie's gesture). `input: mic · output: three.js-3D constellation · technique: trained-readout ESN · palette: cyan/aurora phosphor`.

The **canon verb:** play a short motif (keys A–K / click) → it's broadcast as compact `{p,dt,dur}` events → the *next* tab in the ring plays it back in ITS OWN timbre and re-broadcasts it onward, so a phrase literally travels the circle as a living round, a gold light-arc animating each hand-off. - **`11064-tonotopy`** ⭐⭐⭐ *(status: queued — **RESURRECT** on a Canvas2D / "most-legible" slot — needs a one-line build fix)* — **watch a cortex tune itself to sound.** The SOM as a flat 2-D Canvas2D topographic sheet (G=26): a **U-matrix under-layer** (per-cell avg L2 distance to grid-neighbours → ridges at cluster borders) + a **timbre color fill** (centroid→teal↔violet hue, energy→brightness, eased) so you SEE regions coalesce into a smooth gradient; a slim **piano/frequency reference strip** along the left edge names the emergent low→high axis (the tonotopy) at a glance; a firing marker tracks the BMU; click a cell to play its learned timbre. Sonification is elegant — each neuron's 12-band vector becomes a single-oscillator **`PeriodicWave`** over ~C2, so the firing BMU literally plays the spectra the map is sorting. HiDPI-correct, strobe-safe. **This is the most *legible* read of the concept** — arguably the stronger silent-screen morning read than the winner; lost only on the winner's fresher substrate (three.js is more rested than Canvas2D-3×) + bigger 3-D spectacle. **BUILD FIX REQUIRED before resurrect:** the authoritative `npm run build` caught `'ctx' is possibly null` at `page.tsx:198` (a `createLinearGradient` on the reference-strip context without a null-guard) — the builder's own `tsc --noEmit` missed it; add a `if (!ctx) return;` guard (or narrow the 2D-context type) at the top of that draw scope. Everything else self-reported tsc/eslint clean. **Deepen:** drop-an-audio-file corpus (decodeAudioData + FFT→12 bands, Karel's real Path piano); U-matrix / component-plane toggle; auto-play a drifting path across the settled sheet so it performs itself.

- **`11080-somdrift`** ⭐⭐ *(status: queued — **RESURRECT** on a CSS-compositor / long-form / ambient slot — built demoable + self-verified clean)* — **a cortex that never stops re-tuning itself.** The SOM (16×16) rendered as a **pure CSS compositor** — 256 `radial-gradient` `<div>` blobs with `filter:blur` + `mix-blend-mode:screen` so neighbouring glows ADD into one luminous sheet, updated imperatively via refs (no per-cell React state), off canvas/WebGL/SVG. The engine runs an initial organizing phase then **holds a plasticity floor (α≈0.06, σ≈1.5) forever** instead of freezing, and the **timbre corpus drifts** — a bank of seeded low-frequency oscillators + a one-way smootherstep ramp walks the diet from dark+hollow early to bright+buzzy later (driven by an integer step counter, fully deterministic) — so the color-field visibly migrates over minutes: **genuinely different at minute 5 than minute 1** (the thin long-form-with-memory cell). A HUD shows the live drifting diet %. Throttled meditative sonification (~1 BMU tone / 0.4 s). Self-verified `tsc --noEmit` 0 + eslint 0. **Why banked:** the cleanest **#4 (long-form/never-settling)** of the fan, but by its own honest admission slow and low-spectacle — it rewards patient watching over an instant morning "wow," so it lost to the winner's immediate visual payoff. Resurrect verbatim on a long-form/ambient slot. **Deepen:** feed a real live FFT stream so it re-tunes to actual sound; U-matrix overlay revealing clusters split/merge; a sleep/wake breathing of the plasticity floor (consolidate → loosen).

## §1115 banked runners-up (WIDE — three geometric-generative instruments raced, 1 shipped `11000-craquelure`)

Both were built demoable + clean by parallel builders this cycle, curated out only for jury-alignment (the winner was the sole genuine lab-first technique). Both are resurrect-ready — the folders were removed, but the full briefs are here.

- **`11016-euclidribbon`** *(status: queued · built-once, resurrect-ready)* — **feel a polyrhythm as concentric rings of light.** Godfried Toussaint's **Euclidean rhythms** (Bjorklund's algorithm; "The Euclidean Algorithm Generates Traditional Musical Rhythms," BRIDGES 2005): E(k,n) distributes `k` onsets maximally-evenly over `n` steps (E(3,8)=tresillo, E(5,8)=cinquillo, E(7,16)=samba). Several rings of different `n` run against one shared lookahead clock, **phasing into a living polymeter**; coincidences (≥2 onsets on the same step) **bloom a central glow**. **INPUT keyboard** (↑↓ select ring · ←→ edit `k` · `[`/`]` edit `n` · +/− add/remove ring · space pause · 1–5 snap to named world rhythms). **OUTPUT the CSS compositor** — rings/dots/playheads are DOM `<div>`s moved only by `transform`/`opacity` (GPU-composited, no canvas/WebGL/SVG-path), the jury's other praised rested substrate. **PALETTE** cool nacre/violet/cyan on near-black. Clears floor #2 (Bjorklund generator + shared polymeter clock/scheduler + CSS ring renderer) + #3 (Toussaint named reference). *Was the most polished build of the fan.* **Resurrect as-is** — it's the strongest CSS-compositor answer in the backlog. Deepen: independent per-ring tempo divisors + a live chord/beat map.

- **`11032-attractorium`** *(status: queued · built-once, resurrect-ready · consumes the "Sculpt a living strange attractor" seed)* — **an attractor's SHAPE is its TIMBRE.** Iterate a **Clifford Pickover / Peter de Jong** map (`x'=sin(a·y)+c·cos(a·x); y'=sin(b·x)+d·cos(b·y)`) ~110k points into a **three.js `Points` cloud** (additive-blended soft sprites, hand-rolled spherical-orbit camera, a derived `z=0.55·sin(a·x+b·y)` depth so the flat 2-D attractor orbits in 3-D). **The concept:** bin the cloud by x into ~20 density buckets → drive a **20-partial additive-synth voice** (bin density = partial gain, `1/√k` spectral tilt over a C2 fundamental), so a spidery attractor sounds thin/pure and a filled one sounds rich — **the picture literally IS the timbre**, gliding as the shape morphs. **INPUT pointer** (drag-orbit + scroll-zoom + 4 param sliders + randomize + "let it drift" LFO walk). **OUTPUT three.js geometry** (explicitly not a fragment-quad). **PALETTE** violet↔teal↔pale-cyan nebula condensation, cosmic→intense. Named ref **Paul Bourke** (paulbourke.net/fractals/). Clears floor #2 (attractor iterator + density→additive sonifier + three.js renderer) + #3 (Bourke) + soft #4 (autonomous drift = long-form). **Why benched, not weak:** "strange attractor" already appears ~31× in the lab (the redundancy the jury keeps flagging) — but the **density→timbre coupling is genuinely novel** and it's the freshest OUTPUT (three.js Points) in the backlog. Resurrect next time output-diversity wants a three.js ship. Deepen: radial (not x) binning + per-bin stereo pan so the attractor's shape also *spatializes*.

- **`10072-emberwell`** ⭐⭐⭐ *(status: queued — built demoable then benched; RESURRECT FIRST on a warm / breath / live-performance slot)* — **breath-conducted campfire, as a real GPU fluid+combustion sim.** WebGL2 stable-fluids solver over RGBA16F ping-pong FBOs (`fluid.ts`): semi-Lagrangian advection → thermal buoyancy + coal-bed heat + breath/wind/gust forces + radiative cooling → divergence → ~22 Jacobi pressure iterations → gradient-subtract projection → **blackbody/Planckian display ramp** (deep red → orange → yellow-white core), so it reads as organic flame, not a particle trick. **Breath is the sensor:** mic time-domain RMS + low-band energy swell heat/lift; a sharp onset (gust) flattens the plume and flings sparks; a coal-bed baseline means the fire never dies. Audio (`audio.ts`, non-JI): brown-noise roar (breath-driven lowpass) + randomized ember-crackle transients + a single low **inharmonic** drone (46 Hz + 2.61× partial) + a log-thud on "Feed." Warm ember palette confined to the shader; chrome all semantic tokens. Mic-denied → autonomous LFO "breathing" self-demo, badged. **Why banked, not weak:** the strongest *warmth* payoff of the fire and a genuinely novel technique for the lab (first GPU stable-fluids combustion + blackbody ramp) — lost only to resonograph's 4/5 research-chain. **Resurrect FIRST** on any warm/ritual/breath/live slot; deepen with a second coupled smoke layer or a hand-over-the-flame camera tier. Named refs: **Jos Stam, "Stable Fluids" (SIGGRAPH 1999)** · the **Planckian/blackbody locus.** `ambition 2/5 (#1 GPU fluid-combustion grep-0 + #2 four subsystems: breath RMS + gust onset + WebGL fluid sim + noise fire-synth) · input: breath(mic) · output: WebGL2 fluid shader · technique: semi-Lagrangian stable-fluids + combustion + blackbody ramp · palette: warm ember/amber`.

- **`10104-rainvessel`** ⭐⭐ *(status: queued — built demoable then benched; RESURRECT on a warm / playful / tilt-input / mobile slot)* — **a tilt-poured clay rainstick.** three.js terracotta hemisphere holding ~2,600 warm seed-brown grains as an `InstancedMesh` (`vessel-scene.ts`), a hand-rolled sphere-containment physics integrator (`grain-physics.ts`) tumbling them under a tiltable gravity vector. **Tilt is the sensor:** `DeviceOrientationEvent` beta/gamma lean gravity so the pile pours to the low side (iOS `requestPermission` handled); desktop/no-sensor → pointer-drag + slow auto-rock, badged `drag (no tilt)`. Audio (`grain-audio.ts`, non-JI, rhythm-first): every above-threshold grain-to-wall impact fires one short band-passed noise-burst "click," pitched + panned per grain, so the *density of contacts is the rain texture* — a trickle vs. a rush; 26-voice cap + DynamicsCompressor limiter + warm brown-noise shell underbed. Warm terracotta/ochre confined to the three.js art layer; chrome semantic. Verbs: Start, Refill (grains float back to the top). **Why banked:** the most *playful/tactile* piece and cleanly warm, but the shallowest ambition of the three (a collision-granular instrument, honest 2/5) and its payoff needs a live tilt device (muted-desktop review shows only the drag fallback). **Resurrect** on a mobile/tilt/kids-adjacent-warmth slot; deepen with grain-size→pitch variety or a two-chamber vessel. Named refs: the **rainstick** as instrument · **Curtis Roads, *Microsound* (2001)** (granular grains-as-atoms). `ambition 2/5 (#1 collision-granular grep-0 + #2 four subsystems: tilt + grain physics + collision-granular synth + three.js instancing) · input: tilt(deviceorientation) · output: three.js InstancedMesh · technique: sphere-containment physics + collision-triggered granular synth · palette: warm terracotta/ochre/seed-brown`.

## §1095 (banked 2026-08-11, DEEP runners-up — both built demoable + tsc/eslint-clean, off every live ban, folders removed unbuilt, never committed)

Winner shipped: **`10056-skyoculus`** (the literal Turrell **oculus** — a single `ScreenQuad` RawShaderMaterial: aperture + opponent cove-light + self-luminous outward spill, an autonomous ~78 s chromatic arc crossing the 0.60 luminance threshold twice, partials re-weighting upward on the flip so it is *audible*; the most faithful to the actual Skyspace, the strongest still-frame/muted read, the leanest & most robust headless — the conceptual-not-merely-pretty pick over two bloom-glow siblings). Both below cleared the ambition floor (#2 ≥3 subsystems + #3 named refs Turrell / Duay-Nagai + #5 §1095 research), dodged every banned tag, passed the full compliance sweep (use-client L1 · PrototypeNav · shared safeMaster/voidReverb · no api route · no `Math.random`/`Date.now` · no off-brand chrome · drug-clean), and self-verified tsc/eslint 0. They lost on being the **GPU "pretty glowing field + bloom"** look the 2026-08-10 jury told the lab to REST — not on quality; each is a strong resurrect on a spectacular-GPU / installation slot.

- **`10024-lightnave`** ⭐⭐⭐ *(status: queued — built demoable then benched; RESURRECT FIRST on a spectacular-GPU / numinous / installation slot)* — **breath-and-gaze-conducted volumetric light-NAVE.** three.js/R3F + `@react-three/postprocessing` **Bloom**: crossed **additive billboard light-shafts** descending through a vast dark stone nave, drifting dust motes, floor light-pools, slow camera drift. The **luminosity-threshold flip** is a two-profile shader per shaft (wide soft gaussian haze + hard-edged plateau core) mixed by `uFlip = smoothstep(0.45, 0.62, env)` with a core-luminance boost that pushes past the Bloom threshold, so shafts cross from "lit fog" into self-luminous glowing solid as the field brightens. Drivers: **breath** (mic RMS → auto ~0.15 Hz sine self-demo, Canvas renders on mount un-gated) + **gaze up** (device-orientation → pointer-drag → auto camera drift; the "look up into the shafts" verb). Audio = 8-partial inharmonic choral drone (ratios `1,1.34,1.79,2.36,3.03,3.91,4.87,6.14`, root ~55 Hz, ±3–6-cent detune) → `createVoidReverb` → `createSafeMaster({gain:0.16})`. Cool pale-blue/silver through deep stone. Named ref: **Turrell Skyspace** + **Duay-Nagai luminosity threshold**. 934 lines, the richest/most-subsystem of the three. **Why banked:** it is precisely the GPU "pretty glowing shaft-field + bloom" the jury flagged as the monoculture to rest (and it was already benched in §1094 for the same reason); the DEEP mandate this fire favored the conceptual-minimal Turrell over the spectacle. **Resurrect** whenever a slot explicitly wants the spectacular immersive-GPU piece, or DEEPEN with real head-tracked/installation gaze so you physically crane up into the shafts. `state: numinous/ecstatic · pole: boundless-luminous · input: breath+gaze · output: three.js crossed additive shafts + bloom · technique: two-profile flip shader + volumetric shafts · palette: pale-blue/silver through stone`. Build note: R3F v9 `<bufferAttribute args={[positions,3]}>` + `<Bloom ref>` typings passed local tsc but were NOT run through the authoritative `npm run build` — verify on resurrect.

- **`10040-godsray`** ⭐⭐ *(status: queued — built demoable then benched)* — **screen-space god-rays down an occluded nave.** three.js/R3F scene (bright emissive **aperture-disc** high in a dark cool-stone nave, partly occluded by silhouette columns + a rood screen) rendered to a `WebGLRenderTarget`, then a hand-written full-screen `ShaderMaterial` marches **56 radial samples** from each pixel toward the aperture's projected screen-space position (**Kenny Mitchell, "Volumetric Light Scattering as a Post-Process," GPU Gems 3 Ch.13**) — dark occluders read ~0 in the isolate-bright step so the columns carve real gaps into the rays. The **luminosity-threshold flip**: breath raises ray spread/length fast but shaft brightness only through a slow-slewed `lum` (τ≈2.6 s, ~0.06 Hz); past `smoothstep(0.52,1.05)` a cool near-white core is added under a Reinhard tone-map so the shaft saturates from haze-through-dust into a solid glowing column, aperture emissive rising in lock-step. Drivers: breath (mic RMS → auto ~0.15 Hz sine) + gaze/aim (device-orientation → pointer → auto-drift swings the projected sun). Same inharmonic drone + shared safe-master. Cool pale-blue/silver through stone. **Why banked:** same bloom-glow spectacle family as 10024; and its flip-threshold / ray-exposure constants are eyeballed from the shader math, not tuned on a live GPU render. **Resurrect** on a GPU slot to open a genuinely distinct grep-worthy substrate (screen-space light scattering / render-target post pass — the technique the lab has NOT used), with the constants tuned on a real device. `state: numinous/ecstatic · pole: boundless-luminous · input: breath+gaze · output: three.js render-target + custom radial-scatter post pass · technique: GPU-Gems-3 volumetric light scattering post-process · palette: pale-blue/silver through stone`.

## §1093 (banked 2026-08-11, DEEP runners-up — both built demoable + tsc/eslint-clean, off every live ban, folders removed unbuilt, never committed)

Cycle 1093 ran **DEEP** — ONE north star, *"the unbinding ceremony,"* deepening the fresh cycle-1092 winner `9928-unbind` (a single global audio-visual desync + Re-bind button) into a complete dissociation→rebind experience, raced via three genuinely different technical mechanisms (**fragment it voice-by-voice / arc it over 6 minutes / drive it with your body**). Winner shipped: **`9944-restrata`** (PER-VOICE FRAGMENTATION — N=5 independent offsets scatter the self into separately-drifting strata you rebind one at a time; the most faithful deepening of cycle-1's single offset, the strongest self-demoing muted-phone read, the most robust headless). The two below both cleared the ambition floor (#2 ≥3 subsystems + #3 named refs + #4 multi-cycle claimable + #5 §1093 research; honest NO #1 — deepening a standout, the jury #6 intent), dodged every banned tag, passed the full compliance sweep (use-client L1 · PrototypeNav · shared safeMaster/voidReverb/droneBank · no api route · no `Math.random`/`Date.now` · no off-brand chrome · drug-clean), and self-verified tsc/eslint 0. They lost on **06:30 muted-phone review fitness**, NOT on quality.

### ⭐⭐⭐ `9976-holdstill` — "be still to become whole": motion-energy → cross-modal-bind coupling — status: queued (RESURRECT FIRST, on a live-interaction / motion-sensor / installation slot) — built demoable + clean in-fire, folder removed unbuilt, never committed

The most *conceptual* of the three (jury #4): the audio-visual desync offset is driven by the visitor's **motion energy** instead of a timer — move and sound drifts out of lock with image and you disembody; hold perfectly **still** and the streams slowly re-lock into one crisp embodied event. `motion-input.ts` = pointer-velocity primary (always available) + opt-in iOS DeviceMotion (permission-gated, silent degrade) → one `[0,1]` scalar; asymmetric ease (fast-attack unbind / slow ~2 s release so re-locking is *earned*); a slow tanh-saturated bipolar `dir(t)` holds the offset sign stable while moving. Reward: a `bind²·env`-gated clean "resolve" voice (`[1,2.02,3.03]`) rings only when still, over a never-silent inharmonic drone (`[1,2.76,5.40,8.93]`) + a rough out-of-lock swell (`[1,2.41,4.83]`) that fades as you bind; shader `uSplit` doubles/chromatic-smears the ring bloom while moving, collapsing to one bloom when bound. Stillness meter + meditative copy ("you are one event"); ~3.2 s idle → seeded self-demo breathes bound↔adrift. **Why banked, not weak:** its "you must be still to become whole" is the strongest *idea* of the fire and its embodied-motion INPUT is genuinely fresh — but its payoff **requires live interaction**, so on Karel's muted-phone 06:30 review it can only show the auto-demo stand-in (the recurring reason interaction-required pieces lose the muted review), and embodied-motion was just cashed 2 cycles ago (`9864-handroom`). **Resurrect FIRST** whenever the review mode is live/hands-on (an installation, a Tauri build, or a cycle explicitly for a motion/DeviceMotion input) — there its embodiment is the payoff, not a stand-in. Deepen: add a true whole-body/camera optical-flow tier; make the resolve-voice interval track *how long* you've held still. Refs: TPJ & depersonalization/derealization (PMC7940112) · audiovisual Temporal Binding Window (Cary et al. 2024) · cerebral bases of the AV-TBW (bioRxiv Feb 2026). `ambition honest 3/5 (#2 ≥3 subsystems + #3 refs + #4 multi-cycle + #5 §1093; NO #1)`.

### ⭐⭐ `9960-ceremony` — a one-way ~6-minute stateful dissociation→rebind arc — status: queued (RESURRECT on a long-form slot with a device playthrough) — built demoable + clean in-fire, folder removed unbuilt, never committed

The *long-form* deepening (jury's praised "minute-5 ≠ minute-0" axis): `arc.ts` is a stateful one-way controller over five named phases (**bound coherence 45 s → unbinding 90 s → void plateau 120 s → rebind ceremony 90 s → rest 30 s = 375 s**), smoothstep-interpolating decouple/density/timeRate/reverbWet/droneDrive/swellAmt across keyframes pinned at the phase boundaries, so nothing loops. A **ceremony clock** runs in real wall-clock seconds (the arc is a genuine 6 min); a separate **scene clock** runs at the arc's `timeRate` (slowest in the void). The desync engine is cycle-1's, but `offset = wander · 0.62 · decouple`, so the offset collapses to 0 when bound, widens to ±0.62 s in the void, narrows on rebind; shader `uDecouple` laterally shears the pillar/point strata off the ring stream, `uDensity` thins + deepens fog for a sparse dark void plateau. Muted read is strong (persistent phase label `III · void plateau` + ticked progress bar + clock + offset-ms readout). **Why banked:** a real claimed 6-min arc is exactly what the jury rewards — but its whole payoff is *felt pacing over 6 minutes*, the single thing that can't be verified headless AND that a quick muted-phone glance (showing one phase) can't convey; auto-only (least interactive) also made it the weakest surprise. **Resurrect** when a fire wants a claimed long-form stateful journey and there's a device playthrough to tune the pacing; parameters are centralized in `arc.ts` for easy retuning. Refs: audiovisual Temporal Binding Window (Cary et al. 2024) · cerebral bases of the AV-TBW (bioRxiv Feb 2026) · sensory-conflict/derealization phenomenology. `ambition honest 3/5 (#2 ≥3 subsystems + #3 refs + #4 multi-cycle + #5 §1093; NO #1)`.

Circle-inversion count → cell generation → jeweled hue; reflection parity → checkerboard facet; `fwidth` grout draws the Escher *Circle Limit* lattice. A slow **Möbius disk-isometry** `z→(z−a)/(1−conj(a)z)` streams the whole tiling outward forever (the breakthrough "unfolding"); the shared **Shepard** endless-ascent drone's drive tracks inflation speed (Shepard/spectral tuning — NOT pentatonic/JI). Arrows switch 9 live orders, 1–9 change iridescence/voices, drag steers the Möbius centre. **Why banked, not lost:** genuinely grep-0 (first true hyperbolic geometry in the lab, and the log-polar/form-constant "single biggest lever" pushed to its curved limit) and the most visually spectacular of the fire — lost only to unbind's research-chain + pole-diversity. **Resurrect** on any INTENSE/visionary-geometry slot; strong "huh, we can do that" for a design-director demo. ### ⭐⭐⭐ `9880-poseroom` — "dance the room": full-body pose → listener walk + arm-span opens the chord — status: queued (RESURRECT on a full-body/installation slot) — built demoable + clean in-fire, folder removed unbuilt, never committed

The most fully-embodied of the three. Six voices tuned to **partials 4·5·6·7·8·9 of ~66 Hz** (spectral overtone chord, NOT pentatonic/12-TET), each an HRTF `PannerNode`. **MediaPipe Pose / BlazePose** (33 landmarks, CDN, try/catch-wrapped) drives the whole body: **head/nose → listener pan**, **shoulder-width → depth walk**, and **wrist-to-wrist arm span → a global "openness"** (wide arms open the master lowpass ~11 kHz + lift the upper partials for a bright full chord; arms in close it ~380 Hz for a dark cluster); a **raised wrist** boosts the nearest voice and inks that arm red. Live **stick-figure skeleton** drawn from the landmarks. 4-tier degrade (MediaPipe Pose → frame-diff motion centroid+width → pointer+slider → seeded `mulberry32(0x9880)` ~18/13/24 s auto-**dancer** that walks + breathes depth + opens/closes arms). Cold clinical near-white + Ikeda red. **Why banked, not weak:** a full-body single-gesture-reshapes-the-chord is a lovely embodiment and its auto-dancer reads well muted — but full-body pose in front of a webcam needs room to step back (weaker fit for a laptop review), and body-tracking-as-instrument is a more-trodden lab lane (`217-dance-avatar`/`1590-body-mirror`/`6296-flowbody`/`9560-handflux`) than the winner's hand-conducted sound-sculpture. **Resurrect on:** a full-body / dance / installation slot with room to move (Tauri venue mode). **Refs:** HRTF `PannerNode`/`AudioListener` · MediaPipe Pose / BlazePose 33-landmark on-device tracking · embodied-instrument lineage (Theremin gestural field, motion-tracked installations). Full clean build in-fire (page + room.ts + audio.ts + pose.ts + prng.ts + README; tsc 0).

### ⭐⭐⭐ `9832-wanglace` — "a melody that mathematically never repeats": aperiodic Wang/substitution tiling → JI score — status: queued — built demoable + clean in-fire, folder removed unbuilt, never committed

A self-generating audio-visual score whose STRUCTURE is a proof of non-repetition made audible (**substitution-tiling is grep-0** in the lab). A square field of **edge-matched Wang tiles** grows deterministically along a diagonal frontier; each tile's 4-edge signature selects one of **nine 7-limit/5-limit JI degrees** (`1/1,9/8,6/5,5/4,4/3,3/2,5/3,15/8,2/1` — deliberately NOT pentatonic) and rings a bell over a drone — so the aperiodic tiling drives a provably non-repeating melody (motifs almost-recur, never exactly). The scheme (honest, not overclaimed): a **two-axis product of the Fibonacci word** (`0→01, 1→0`) colours the grid lines; adjacent tiles share an edge by construction (real Wang tiling), and since the word never contains `11` there are exactly nine tile types → bijection to the nine ratios (`degree = hpair·3 + vpair`). **Non-repetition proof:** a period `p` would force `W[i]=W[i+p]`, i.e. a periodic Fibonacci word — impossible (letter frequencies `1/φ`, `1/φ²` are irrational). Stark clinical pale-on-dark duotone + one violet frontier accent; autonomous on load (~4 tiles/s, ~1.6 reduced-motion) → **excellent muted-phone read**; keyboard nudge (↑/↓ = 16/15, Shift = 81/80 comma), Space pause, F frontier, hover = ratio/cents; refills fresh non-overlapping word-windows forever. **Why banked, not weak:** its *look* (stark geometric JI lattice growing on Canvas2D) rhymes with the recent JI+geometric cluster (`9688-purelock`/`9720-testcard`/`9768-mpelace`) the jury warns against — the winner (a meditative bloom driven by *inactivity*) broke the monoculture harder. **Resurrect on:** an autonomous-generative / mathematical-music / long-form slot; next-cycle a true 2-D substitution (Ammann stone-inflation → nested self-similar growth) + frontier voice-leading. **Refs:** Hao Wang 1961 · Berger 1966 · Robinson 1971 · Culík II 1996. Full clean build in-fire (page + tiling.ts + tuning.ts + synth.ts + README; tsc 0).

### ⭐⭐⭐ `9784-glideharp` — "the fretless microtonal ribbon": continuous per-note streamed MPE-out — status: queued (built demoable + clean in-fire, folder removed unbuilt, never committed)

A **continuous pitch ribbon** (`freq(x) = 146.83·2^(3x)`, three octaves, no discretizing step anywhere in audio or MIDI) you **glide** with pointer/touch (portamento; up/down = brightness); polyphonic per-contact, plus keys `1–9` land the nine 5-limit guide ratios. Faint **JI guide-lines** (1/1, 9/8, 6/5, 5/4, 4/3, 3/2, 5/3, 15/8, 2/1) with ratio+cents labels show the pure landing spots without ever snapping (optional gentle `Magnetize` toggle, off by default — Partch free-pitch continuum). **The novel axis (why it's worth resurrecting):** each voice STREAMS its own **continuous** per-note pitch-bend OUT via MPE — round-robin ch 2–16, MCM + ±48-st RPN init, per-frame `bendSemitones = 12·log2(f/midiToFreq(note)) → bend14`, throttled ~55 msg/s/voice, all-notes-off + bend-reset on teardown/switch — the continuous-expression use MPE was actually designed for (Osmose/Haken Continuum), a step beyond the winner's discrete per-note bend. Internal triangle+FM synth with true `setTargetAtTime` portamento so it works unplugged; `text-destructive` notice + live readout when no Web MIDI. **Beating→Lissajous:** the most-recently-moved voice vs a drone root drives a classic Lissajous (octave-reduced p/q lobes; true P/Q beat rate drives precession ≤2 Hz) that **locks to a still glowing curve on a pure ratio** and precesses off it — an oscilloscope of consonance. Clinical high-key, Canvas2D. Seeded `mulberry32(0x9784)` auto-glide demo sweeps + pauses on pure landings, alive on a muted phone. **Refs:** Osmose/Continuum · MPE spec RP-053 · Lissajous/phase-interference · Plomp–Levelt 1965 · Partch *Genesis of a Music* · xen-wiki 5-limit. **Resurrect on:** an expressive-gesture / continuous-controller / Tauri-installation slot, ideally an on-device pass with a DAW + touchscreen. ## §1085 (2026-08-10) — DEEP runner-up (mnemonic cycle-2, the derivation-tree approach)

Cycle 1085 ran **DEEP** — extending the jury-named "conceptually richest build," `9080-mnemonic` ("the memory ribbon"), into its claimed **cycle 2** ("developing variation over minutes"): a machine that forms a LASTING THEME from your recurring motifs and develops it over minutes, so minute five is a grown structure, not a per-phrase echo. Grounded in the §1085 research dive — **DSMR, arXiv:2602.19816 (Feb 2026)**, distributed memory horizons (a long window carries "motif repetition and developmental variation"). Raced via 2 approaches. Winner shipped: **`9624-counterpoint`** (answers your line in real canon/inversion/stretto — a living fugue-exposition whose voice count grows 2→3→4 with theme strength). Both built demoable, passed the full compliance sweep (use-client, PrototypeNav, master 0.18 + compressor, seeded auto-demo, reduced-motion, no off-brand hues, drug-clean), broke the jury's warm/particle monoculture identically (inline-SVG clinical manuscript, zero GPU), and dodged every banned tag.

### ⭐⭐⭐ `9608-thematic` — "the growing theme": recurring motifs cluster into ONE theme + a branching derivation tree (runner-up, resurrect on a notation/long-form slot) — status: queued
- **Brief:** the sibling approach to the shipped `9624-counterpoint`. SAME north star (mnemonic cycle-2 — theme over minutes, not a per-phrase echo) but the load-bearing subsystem is a **long-horizon theme MEMORY + a visible derivation TREE**, not counterpoint. Three cooperating pieces in `engine.ts`: (1) **melodic clustering** — each captured phrase reduced to interval contour and routed by similarity into a cluster; recurring motifs *reinforce* one cluster (weight = Σ salience); the strongest cluster is "the theme," its most-salient member the trunk. (2) **compound chained transforms** (invert→augment, augment×2) so each generation drifts further from the seed and is drawn as a **child node** of the motif it derived from — lineage deepens instead of resetting; edges labelled with the transform. (3) **tempo tracking + eighth-note quantization** → barred, metered manuscript. Two SVG panels (violet-accented theme trunk + branching tree up to 16 nodes / 5 generations) + a cluster ledger. Built clean (page 46.9KB + engine.ts 369L + README; scoped tsc 0; compliance-swept clean; seeded `mulberry32(0x9608)` auto-demo lands theme-formation + first branches in ~1.5s).
- **Why it lost (DEEP curation):** `9624-counterpoint` won on (1) the BIGGER concept realized — turning a monophonic line into self-accompanying POLYPHONY (real canon/inversion/stretto) is a larger, more surprising leap than accreting variations into a tree, which is conceptually closer to cycle-1's transform-quoting; (2) **surprise** — "it built a fugue out of my humming" reads bigger than "it drew my variations as a family tree"; (3) **love-signal** — Karel loves `1354-glyph-canon` (canon) + `1392-phase-loom`, biasing directly toward the contrapuntal sibling; (4) counterpoint adds audible polyphony (distinct timbres + stereo pan), opening a new *sonic* territory alongside the visual, where thematic's payoff is primarily visual.
- **Why it's still worth resurrecting:** the derivation tree is the more *legible* "watch it grow over minutes" visual and the faithful realization of mnemonic's written next-pass plan (clustering + compound developments + metered manuscript). It's the strongest LONG-FORM candidate in the lab (state/memory/evolution — the menu item that stays thin) and looks genuinely different from a fugue. Ship it on a notation/long-form slot; its own README already sketches a cycle-3 (branch retirement into a longer horizon + two clusters merging into a double theme).

- **On-device tuning owed:** MediaPipe pinch/spread thresholds + the downward-strike velocity threshold need a real-device pass (they were set headless); the flow-→-shimmer gain may want rebalancing on a phone speaker.

## §1082 (2026-08-10) — WIDE runners-up, banked verbatim (built demoable + compliance-swept, curated OUT; folders removed, never committed)

Cycle 1082 ran **WIDE** — 3 divergent explorers off every live ban. Winner shipped: `9512-heliosong` (the live NOAA sky as an infinite Eno-style cosmic drone-field under DOM/CSS aurora). Both runners-up built demoable, passed the full compliance sweep (`"use client"` L1 · named `PrototypeNav` + self-slug · `createSafeMaster @0.18` · `prefersReducedMotion` · no api route · no off-brand chrome · drug-language clean), and dodged every live ban — genuinely shippable on their own slots.

### ⭐⭐⭐ `handglyph` — hands conduct a boundless field of light & sound (resurrect FIRST) — status: queued
- **Brief:** camera + **MediaPipe HandLandmarker** (21 landmarks/hand, runtime-CDN `webpackIgnore` loader — no package.json change) → conduct an immersive WebGPU glow-field that sings. Hand height→register, finger-spread→chord width, two-hand distance→shimmer/reverb depth/brightness, pinch→pluck a voice. Seeded two-synthetic-hands auto-demo when no camera; Canvas2D radial-glow fallback when no WebGPU. Meditative/ecstatic, embodied. Ref **WJARR 2026-0860 (MediaPipe+TD, <35 ms)** + MediaPipe Hands (RESEARCH §1082).
- **Why it was promising:** the HIGHEST-ambition lane of the fire (6 subsystems: camera + tracking model + spatial harmonic field + generative voices + WebGPU render + seeded fallback), and the only *embodied* input in the last 10. Built clean end-to-end (page 565L + gpu/fallback/field/audio/handLoader/rng + README).
- **Why it lost (WIDE weighting):** camera input AND the WebGPU shader are both **headless-unverifiable** — I can't confirm they run on Karel's device, and MediaPipe spread/pinch thresholds will need on-device tuning, so the 06:30 muted-phone review most likely sees only the auto-demo. Also camera hand-tracking has in-lab precedent (`1590-body-mirror`, `5096-theurgy`, `862-kids-solfege-signs`), so it's less *fresh* than it looks. `9512` won on reliability (pure DOM/CSS always renders) + a thin-gap-that-never-shipped (real-data-sonification) + the freshest substrate.
- **Resurrect when:** it can get a real on-device pass (camera + GPU). Then it's a strong DEEP headliner — claim criterion #4 and deepen: per-finger voices, both-hands-as-a-two-instrument-duet, record a gesture-score.

### ⭐⭐ `tidewalk` — tilt-walk a latent timbre map, layer paths into a phasing canon — status: queued
- **Brief:** device-tilt (arrow-key + on-screen drag-pad fallback) walks a **4×4 bilinear-interpolated FM-timbre latent map**; record→replay→layer ≤4 paths, each at a near-unity independent period so they phase (Reich/Eno *Bloom*); inline-SVG cool-indigo nebula (doubles as the drag-pad). Seeded auto-drift + pre-baked canon so it's never blank/silent. Ref Fiebrink/Wekinator + Eno *Bloom*.
- **Why it was promising:** the phasing canon genuinely drifts-and-re-locks; complete tilt→keys→pad→auto-drift fallback chain; clean build (page 577L + timbre/rng + README). Cross-modal/latent-walk is a jury menu-thin category.
- **Why it lost:** lowest ambition (2/5), oldest references, and latent-walk is the least-*fresh* verb this fire (it's a re-home of banked `9448-driftmap` / `9336`). Solid but not surprising enough to headline a WIDE.
- **Resurrect when:** it gets a sharper surprise — two-phone SHARED tilt-map (WebRTC, the 0× multi-user category), or the corner timbres derived from Karel's real Path-piano samples instead of synthetic FM.

## §1143 (WIDE) — two banked seeds (built clean, not shipped this cycle)

**13472-cymatics** ⭐⭐⭐ (resurrect-strong — the material/visual slot) — *Karel's recording sculpts sand into sound-figures.* A **Chladni cymatic plate** seen from above: his real track's live spectrum (5 log bands off `safeMaster.analyser`) drives the eigenmodes `(m,n)` of a square plate; ~30k "sand" grains gradient-descend `field²` onto the nodal lines, forming/dissolving fourfold-symmetric figures that morph with his harmony (quiet=simple, dense=fine lattice). Canvas2D, separable 1-D cosine/sine tables, eased mode slots, loudness-coupled convergence/jitter, seeded self-demo fallback, `prefers-reduced-motion`. Built demoable & lint/type-clean. Refs: Chladni 1787, Jenny 1967, arXiv:2605.09846 *ChladniSonify* (May 2026). **Why banked not shipped:** Chladni already exists in-lab (7032-chladni, 1628-chladni-choir) so it's not first-in-lab, and it's a *watch* piece — striketemple's playable-duet verb was the bigger departure this WIDE. **Resurrect:** ship as-is when the material/visual slot rotates in, or fold as a "cymatic view" alt-renderer on a harmony piece. Folder was removed after curation; rebuild from this spec.

**13504-crossweave** ⭐⭐ (resurrect — the spectral-transform slot) — *Two of Karel's songs fuse into one instrument.* Live **channel-vocoder cross-synthesis**: two parallel banks of 28 constant-Q log bandpass biquads split a **body** track (carrier, e.g. Welcome Home) and a **voice** track (envelope, e.g. Snowflake); each voice band's RMS (small per-band AnalyserNode) is written onto the matching body-band gain via `setTargetAtTime`, so the body wears the voice's moving spectrum. A **morph slider** = three-bus equal-power crossfade dry-body → cross-synth → dry-voice; swap-carrier control; graceful single-track degradation. Output is a deliberately non-canvas **inline-SVG spectral ribbon** (body/voice/hybrid rows on the violet ramp). Built demoable & lint/type-clean. Refs: Dudley 1939 (channel vocoder), Wishart *Audible Design* 1994, Flanagan–Golden 1966. **Why banked not shipped:** conceptually adjacent to 13040-spectralhold's spectral DSP and lighter on interaction (one slider) than striketemple. **Resurrect:** ship when a spectral-transform slot rotates in; deepen with per-note formant-locking or letting the morph itself follow one track's dynamics. Folder was removed after curation; rebuild from this spec.

## §1149 (WIDE) — two banked seeds (built clean, not shipped this cycle)

**13808-tiltpour** ⭐⭐⭐ (resurrect-strong — the physical/embodied-input slot) — *Your recordings are a LIQUID you can pour.* Tilt your phone (`deviceorientation` gamma → gravity vector; pointer-drag fallback on desktop) and Karel's piano, decoded into ~1000 particles of a hand-rolled **2D Position Based Fluids** solver (predict → uniform-grid neighbours → density-constraint λ iterations w/ poly6+spiky kernels → tensile s_corr → XSPH viscosity → wall confinement, auto-calibrated rest density, zero per-frame alloc), pours toward the low corner and pools. A capped pool of 16 overlap-add grain emitters biased toward crowded regions makes **grain density rise where the liquid gathers**; each grain is a real-track slice, Hann-enveloped, `StereoPanner` by particle-x, gain by local crowd, `playbackRate` ±0.5% — so pouring the fluid to one side physically pans his music there. three.js additive `THREE.Points` in a faint glass box, cool ink→violet on near-black. Seeded slow-rock self-demo, full teardown. Built demoable, **tsc 0 / eslint 0**. Ref: Macklin & Müller *Position Based Fluids* (SIGGRAPH 2013). **Why banked not shipped:** its granular-of-his-recording DSP family is the exact lane the recent window is saturated with (conductorwell/dreambetween/callback/spectralsmear + banked tapewarp/grainloom); auroraconductor's **live camera + optical flow** is a genuinely first-in-lab axis the window lacks. **Resurrect:** ship near-verbatim when the granular family ages out, or as the physical-input slot; the PBF solver is reusable shared-infra. Folder removed after curation; rebuild from this spec.

**13824-growfromsound** ⭐⭐⭐ (resurrect — the morphogenesis/botanical slot) — *Your recording grows a living plant.* Drag a point of light through the dark and a bioluminescent tree grows toward it out of his music, **one branch per note**, via the **space colonization algorithm** (attractor cloud pulls nearest branch tip → tip advances one segment along averaged attractor direction within influence radius → reached attractors consumed; cloud re-seeded near the moving cursor each note so it grows *toward where you point*). Each of his real note onsets (`loadTrackAnalysis`, seeded-onset-clock fallback) fires one growth step + one pitch-matched **grain of his real recording** seated at that note's own moment — so the tree grows at his exact rate and phrasing. Low notes→violet branches, high→cyan; bright tips fade to dim wood; three.js additive LineSegments+Points, no axes/grid/labels (NOT a data diagram). Seeded light-drift self-demo, full teardown w/ `forceContextLoss`. Built demoable, **tsc 0 / eslint 0**. Ref: Runions, Lane & Prusinkiewicz, *Modeling Trees with a Space Colonization Algorithm* (2007). **Why banked not shipped:** pointer-place-light is the finger-on-glass modality this cycle's research said to break (camera/tilt are the fresher inputs), and it leans on the note-analysis→grain lineage; space colonization itself is first-in-lab and worth shipping. **Resurrect:** ship when the morphogenesis/botanical slot rotates in (pairs with physarumreef's lineage). Folder removed after curation; rebuild from this spec.

## §1150 (DEEP "your POSITION is the mix — a spatial-audio world of your whole catalog") — two banked seeds (built clean, not shipped this cycle; winner = `13840-hallofsongs`, the first-person 3D sound-walk)

The DEEP concept: load MANY of Karel's real recordings at once and spatialize each with a Web Audio HRTF `PannerNode`, so *moving through / arranging the space* continuously re-mixes his own music in 3D. Raced 3 interaction geometries. Both losers are strong, fully built, compliance-clean (real-catalog audio via `loadRealTrackBuffer`→`createSafeMaster`, no synth, semantic tokens, back-link + notes modal, seeded PRNG, full teardown), folders `rm -rf`'d before the authoritative build (never committed). They lost on ONE decisive gate: **both use Canvas2D-primary output, which JURY 2026-08-15 BANNED for this window** (Canvas2D 5×); the winner's three.js WebGL 3D dodges it and is the most embodied verb (walk *inside* your catalog). The shared HRTF multi-track proximity-mixing engine is reusable shared-infra — worth lifting into `_shared/` when either resurrects.

**13856-songpath** ⭐⭐⭐ (resurrect-strong — the "compose a journey by drawing it" slot) — *Draw a listening path through your whole catalog and it plays back as a moving spatial mix.* His recordings (first 8 of `REAL_TRACKS`) are seeded-scatter, draggable nodes on a dark-violet field; you draw a path with pointer/touch; a listener-dot travels it (play / pause / manual scrub / loop / speed) while `ctx.listener` moves to the dot each frame and each track's `AudioBufferSourceNode(loop)→PannerNode(HRTF)→GainNode→safeMaster` is spatialized + level-mixed by an equal-power inverse-square proximity law (whisper floor, power-normalized, `setTargetAtTime`-smoothed). Sweeping near a node brings that piece forward and pans it; curving between two cross-fades; looping holds a rotating cluster. Seeded default path so it sounds alive on Start; nearest song named; nodes glow + pull-line to their live weight. **Why banked:** Canvas2D-primary output is the jury-banned tag this window. **Resurrect:** re-render the field in WebGL2/SVG (dodges the ban) OR ship as-is once Canvas2D ages off the ban list; the drawn-path→spatial-arrangement idea is the freshest part. Rebuild from this spec.

**13872-orbitmix** ⭐⭐⭐ (resurrect — the "spatial sequencer / rotating mobile" slot) — *Your whole catalog orbits you; arrange it into a rotating spatial sequence.* You sit at origin (listener facing −Z); his recordings (first 8 of `REAL_TRACKS`) are satellites on a radial dial you drag to set each track's ANGLE (its HRTF direction around you) and RADIUS (near = present, far = whisper, equal-power radius→gain). All loop at once (`src→PannerNode(HRTF, rolloff-0 direction-only)→per-track GainNode→safeMaster`); a rotating **playhead beam** adds a smooth Gaussian gain bump to whichever satellite it sweeps past — so arranged angles compose a rotating spatial sequence and radii set the standing balance. Controls: sweep speed / width / pause / reset. Cool violet/indigo/ice, alive + draggable from load. **Why banked:** Canvas2D-primary output is the jury-banned tag this window; and its "arrange around a center" is a hair closer to a static dial than the winner's embodied walk. **Resurrect:** re-render on WebGL2 (dodges the ban) as a genuine 2.5D mobile; the sweep-as-spatial-sequencer verb is the keeper. Rebuild from this spec.
